                INSERT INTO model_benchmark_experiments (
                    id, experiment_key, name, execution_mode, cohort_hash,
                    snapshot_hash, frozen_snapshot_json, quality_gate_json
                ) VALUES (1, 'legacy-benchmark', 'legacy', 'test',
                          'aaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaa',
                          'bbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbb',
                          '{}', '{}')
            """)
            connection.exec_driver_sql("""
                INSERT INTO model_benchmark_variants (
                    id, experiment_id, model_key, provider, model_id, pricing_json
                ) VALUES (1, 1, 'sol', 'test', 'legacy-model', '{}')
            """)

            run_migrations(connection)
            run_migrations(connection)

            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT run_key, status, input_tokens, retryable "
                "FROM automation_optimization_runs WHERE id=1"
            ).one() == ("legacy-run", "awaiting_executor", None, 0)
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT idempotency_key, status, automation_run_id "
                "FROM optimization_case_queue WHERE id=1"
            ).one() == ("legacy-case", "batched", 1)
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT experiment_key, execution_mode, max_round_cost_micros "
                "FROM model_benchmark_experiments WHERE id=1"
            ).one() == ("legacy-benchmark", "test", 0)
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT model_id, model_config_id, actual_cost_micros "
                "FROM model_benchmark_variants WHERE id=1"
            ).one() == ("legacy-model", None, 0)
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT is_admin FROM users WHERE id=1"
            ).scalar_one() == 1
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT input_micros_per_million_tokens, benchmark_enabled "
                "FROM model_configs WHERE id=1"
            ).one() == (0, 0)
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT input_micros_per_million_tokens "
                "FROM optimizer_configs WHERE id=1"
            ).scalar_one() == 0
            connection.exec_driver_sql("""
                INSERT INTO model_benchmark_experiments (
                    id, experiment_key, name, execution_mode, cohort_hash,
                    snapshot_hash, frozen_snapshot_json, quality_gate_json
                ) VALUES (2, 'real-benchmark', 'real', 'real',
                          'cccccccccccccccccccccccccccccccccccccccccccccccccccccccccccccccc',
                          'dddddddddddddddddddddddddddddddddddddddddddddddddddddddddddddddd',
                          '{}', '{}')
            """)
            connection.exec_driver_sql("""
                INSERT INTO automation_optimization_runs (
                    id, run_key, base_prompt_version, policy_revision, status,
                    trigger_reason, case_ids_json, frozen_input_json
                ) VALUES (2, 'processing-run', 'B1', 2, 'processing',
                          'case_threshold', '[]', '{}')
            """)
            with pytest.raises(IntegrityError, match="immutable"):
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "UPDATE model_benchmark_experiments "
                    "SET max_round_cost_micros=999 WHERE id=2"
                )
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT max(version) FROM schema_migrations"
            ).scalar_one() == len(MIGRATION_NAMES)
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("statement", "message"),
    [
        (
            "UPDATE material_packages SET name = 'changed' WHERE id = 1",
            "MaterialPackage is immutable",
        ),
        (
            "DELETE FROM material_packages WHERE id = 1",
            "MaterialPackage cannot be deleted",
        ),
        (
            "UPDATE material_package_items "
            "SET original_name = 'changed.jpg' WHERE id = 1",
            "MaterialPackageItem is immutable",
        ),
        (
            "DELETE FROM material_package_items WHERE id = 1",
            "MaterialPackageItem cannot be deleted",
        ),
    ],
    ids=[
        "package-update",
        "package-delete",
        "package-item-update",
        "package-item-delete",
    ],
)
def test_material_package_database_guards_reject_mutation(
    tmp_path,
    statement: str,
    message: str,
) -> None:
    engine = _engine(tmp_path, f"{message.split()[0]}-{statement.split()[0]}.db")
    _create_latest_and_run_migrations(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            asset = models.Asset(
                original_name="source.jpg",
                stored_name="source.jpg",
                mime_type="image/jpeg",
                size_bytes=10,
                sha256="f" * 64,
            )
            package = models.MaterialPackage(
                id=1,
                package_key="immutable-package",
                name="不可变素材包",
                source="manual_upload",
                created_by="test",
            )
            db.add_all([asset, package])
            db.flush()
            db.add(
                models.MaterialPackageItem(
                    id=1,
                    package_id=package.id,
                    asset_id=asset.id,
                    original_name=asset.original_name,
                    duplicate=False,
                    position=1,
                )
            )
            db.commit()

        with pytest.raises(IntegrityError, match=message):
            with engine.begin() as connection:
                connection.exec_driver_sql(statement)

        with engine.connect() as connection:
            package_row = connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT name FROM material_packages WHERE id = 1"
            ).one()
            item_row = connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT original_name FROM material_package_items WHERE id = 1"
            ).one()
            assert package_row[0] == "不可变素材包"
            assert item_row[0] == "source.jpg"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_migration_26_repairs_dangling_prompt_fk_and_allows_real_insert(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    engine = _engine(tmp_path, "migration-26-prompt-fk.db")
    _create_latest_and_run_migrations(engine)
    try:
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "DELETE FROM schema_migrations WHERE version = 25"
            )
            connection.exec_driver_sql("PRAGMA writable_schema=ON")
            connection.exec_driver_sql("""
                UPDATE sqlite_master
                SET sql = replace(
                    sql,
                    'automation_optimization_runs',
                    'automation_optimization_runs_v24'
                )
                WHERE type='table' AND name='prompt_versions'
            """)
            connection.exec_driver_sql("PRAGMA writable_schema=OFF")
            version = connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "PRAGMA schema_version"
            ).scalar_one()
            connection.exec_driver_sql(f"PRAGMA schema_version={version + 1}")
            assert any(
                row[2] == "automation_optimization_runs_v24"
                for row in connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "PRAGMA foreign_key_list(prompt_versions)"
                )
            )
            run_migrations(connection)
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "PRAGMA foreign_key_check"
            ).all() == []
            assert any(
                row[2] == "automation_optimization_runs"
                for row in connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "PRAGMA foreign_key_list(prompt_versions)"
                )
            )
            connection.exec_driver_sql("""
                INSERT INTO automation_optimization_runs (
                    id, run_key, base_prompt_version, policy_revision,
                    status, dry_run, trigger_reason, case_ids_json,
                    frozen_input_json, result_json, candidate_count,
                    estimated_cost_micros, actual_cost_micros, retryable,
                    error_message, created_by, created_at
                ) VALUES (
                    9001, 'm26-smoke', 'B1', 1, 'planned', 1, 'test',
                    '[]', '{}', '{}', 0, 0, 0, 0, '', 'test',
                    CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                )
            """)
            connection.exec_driver_sql("""
                INSERT INTO prompt_versions (
                    id, stage, name, version, system_prompt, user_prompt,
                    source_automation_run_id
                ) VALUES (9001, 'B', 'smoke', 'm26-smoke', 's', 'u', 9001)
            """)
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT source_automation_run_id FROM prompt_versions "
                "WHERE id=9001"
            ).scalar_one() == 9001
            connection.exec_driver_sql("DELETE FROM prompt_versions WHERE id=9001")
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "DELETE FROM automation_optimization_runs WHERE id=9001"
            )
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "PRAGMA foreign_key_check"
            ).all() == []
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_migration_71_creates_runtime_tables_without_rewriting_history(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'runtime-v71.db'}")
    try:
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "CREATE TABLE evaluation_production_runs "
                "(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, status VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL)"
            )
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "INSERT INTO evaluation_production_runs (id, status) "
                "VALUES (7, 'published')"
            )
            migration = next(item for item in MIGRATIONS if item.version == 71)
            migration.up(connection)
            migration.up(connection)

            tables = {
                row[0]
                for row in connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'"
                )
            }
            assert {
                "script_definitions",
                "script_versions",
                "workflow_definitions",
                "workflow_versions",
                "production_runs",
                "production_step_attempts",
                "runtime_dispatch_items",
                "runtime_audit_events",
            } <= tables
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT status FROM evaluation_production_runs WHERE id=7"
            ).scalar_one() == "published"
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql("PRAGMA foreign_key_check").all() == []
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_migration_74_adds_review_rounds_and_replaces_reviewer_uniqueness(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'review-rounds-v74.db'}")
    try:
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "CREATE TABLE review_panels ("
                "id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, revision INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0)"
            )
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "CREATE TABLE human_reviews ("
                "id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, panel_id INTEGER, "
                "reviewer_name VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL)"
            )
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uq_human_review_panel_reviewer "
                "ON human_reviews(panel_id, reviewer_name) "
                "WHERE panel_id IS NOT NULL"
            )
            migration = next(item for item in MIGRATIONS if item.version == 74)
            migration.up(connection)
            migration.up(connection)

            panel_columns = {
                row[1]
                for row in connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "PRAGMA table_info(review_panels)"
                )
            }
            review_columns = {
                row[1]
                for row in connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "PRAGMA table_info(human_reviews)"
                )
            }
            assert "review_round" in panel_columns
            assert "review_round" in review_columns

            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "INSERT INTO review_panels (id, revision, review_round) "
                "VALUES (1, 0, 2)"
            )
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "INSERT INTO human_reviews "
                "(id, panel_id, reviewer_name, review_round) "
                "VALUES (1, 1, 'reviewer', 1)"
            )
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "INSERT INTO human_reviews "
                "(id, panel_id, reviewer_name, review_round) "
                "VALUES (2, 1, 'reviewer', 2)"
            )
            with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "INSERT INTO human_reviews "
                    "(id, panel_id, reviewer_name, review_round) "
                    "VALUES (3, 1, 'reviewer', 2)"
                )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_migration_89_scopes_nas_source_uri_to_category(tmp_path) -> None:
    """NAS 来源唯一性必须真的从全局换成按类目，且存量数据不受影响。"""

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'nas-scope-v82.db'}")
    try:
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "CREATE TABLE assets ("
                "id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, source_uri VARCHAR(1000), "
                "category_key VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL, stored_name VARCHAR(200))"
            )
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uq_assets_source_uri "
                "ON assets(source_uri) WHERE source_uri IS NOT NULL"
            )
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "INSERT INTO assets VALUES (1, 'nas://maps/a.jpg', 'inspiration_image', 's1')"
            )

            migration = next(item for item in MIGRATIONS if item.version == 89)
            migration.up(connection)
            migration.up(connection)  # 迁移必须可重复执行

            indexes = {
                row[0]
                for row in connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='index'"
                )
            }
            assert "uq_assets_source_uri_category" in indexes
            assert "uq_assets_source_uri" not in indexes, "旧的全局唯一索引必须移除"

            # 同一来源换个类目要放得进去
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "INSERT INTO assets VALUES (2, 'nas://maps/a.jpg', 'knowledge_graph', 's2')"
            )
            # 同一类目内重复仍要被拒
            with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "INSERT INTO assets VALUES (3, 'nas://maps/a.jpg', 'knowledge_graph', 's3')"
                )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_migration_89_is_safe_without_the_assets_table(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'nas-scope-empty.db'}")
    try:
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            migration = next(item for item in MIGRATIONS if item.version == 89)
            migration.up(connection)
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_mysql_upgrade_path_reaches_latest_migration() -> None:
    """加了迁移却忘了扩 MySQL 升级路径，会在发布的迁移步被拒。

    真 MySQL 的升级用例在没有 MySQL 的机器上全部跳过，指望不上；这条是纯
    Python 断言，本地就能拦住遗漏。2026-09-04 迁移 102 就是漏了这一步，
    deploy.sh 跑到迁移那步才报「不支持自动跨级升级：期望 102，实际 101」。
    """

    import re
    from pathlib import Path

    from app.migrations import mysql_baseline
    from app.migrations.runner import MIGRATIONS

    latest = max(migration.version for migration in MIGRATIONS)
    assert latest in mysql_baseline.UPGRADE_TARGET_VERSIONS, (
        f"迁移 {latest} 不在 MySQL 自动升级的可达版本里"
    )
    assert latest - 1 in mysql_baseline.UPGRADE_SOURCE_VERSIONS, (
        f"schema {latest - 1} 不在 MySQL 自动升级的出发版本里"
    )

    # 光在白名单里还不够：必须真的写了那一级的升级步
    source = Path(mysql_baseline.__file__).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    step = re.search(
        rf"if actual == {latest - 1} and expected >= {latest}:(.+?)actual = {latest}",
        source,
        re.S,
    )
    assert step is not None, f"upgrade_mysql_schema 缺少 {latest - 1} → {latest} 的升级步"
    recorded = re.search(
        rf"\(\s*{latest},\s*[\"']([a-z0-9_]+)[\"']\s*\)", step.group(1)
    )
    assert recorded is not None, f"{latest} 的升级步没有写入 schema_migrations"
    expected_name = next(m.name for m in MIGRATIONS if m.version == latest)
    assert recorded.group(1) == expected_name, (
        f"MySQL 升级步登记的迁移名 {recorded.group(1)} 与 runner 里的 "
        f"{expected_name} 不一致"
    )


def test_mysql_upgrade_creates_tables_through_mysql_metadata() -> None:
    """MySQL 升级步里建表必须走 ``mysql_metadata()``，不能直接 ``Model.__table__.create()``。

    模型上的 ``Text`` + ``server_default`` 在 SQLite 上没问题，MySQL 8 直接拒：
    ERROR 1101 «BLOB, TEXT, GEOMETRY or JSON column can't have a default value»。
    ``mysql_metadata()`` 会把 TEXT 换成 LONGTEXT、把默认值包成表达式默认值。

    116 步 2026-09-16 栽过一次（注释还在那），122 步 09-20 又栽了一次——本机
    SQLite 用例全绿、到 .137 的真 MySQL 才炸。这条用例按语法树盯死写法本身。
    """
    import ast
    import inspect as _inspect
    import textwrap

    from app.migrations import mysql_baseline

    tree = ast.parse(textwrap.dedent(_inspect.getsource(mysql_baseline.upgrade_mysql_schema)))
    offenders = [
        ast.unparse(node.func)
        for node in ast.walk(tree)
        if isinstance(node, ast.Call)
        and getattr(node.func, "attr", "") == "create"
        and isinstance(getattr(node.func, "value", None), ast.Attribute)
        and node.func.value.attr == "__table__"
    ]
    assert not offenders, f"这些建表要改走 mysql_metadata()：{offenders}"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_migration_branch_convergence.py =====
from unittest.mock import Mock

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, inspect

from app import models  # noqa: F401
from app.database import Base
from app.migrations.runner import MIGRATIONS, run_migrations


@pytest.mark.parametrize('version,name,missing', [
    (102, 'add_design_case_samples', 'incremental_automation_controls'),
    (102, 'incremental_automation_controls', 'design_case_samples'),
    (105, 'incremental_automation_controls', 'design_case_samples'),
])
def test_divergent_history_converges_without_rewriting_ledger(version, name, missing):
    engine = create_engine('sqlite://')
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        run_migrations(connection)
        connection.exec_driver_sql('DELETE FROM schema_migrations WHERE version > ?', (version,))
        connection.exec_driver_sql('UPDATE schema_migrations SET name=? WHERE version=102', (name,))
        connection.exec_driver_sql(f'DROP TABLE {missing}')
        connection.exec_driver_sql('CREATE TABLE operator_evidence (value TEXT)')
        connection.exec_driver_sql("INSERT INTO operator_evidence VALUES ('preserve')")
        run_migrations(connection)
        run_migrations(connection)
        assert {'design_case_samples', 'incremental_automation_controls'} <= set(inspect(connection).get_table_names())
        assert connection.exec_driver_sql('SELECT name FROM schema_migrations WHERE version=102').scalar_one() == name
        assert connection.exec_driver_sql('SELECT value FROM operator_evidence').scalar_one() == 'preserve'
        assert connection.exec_driver_sql('SELECT max(version) FROM schema_migrations').scalar_one() == max(item.version for item in MIGRATIONS)
    engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize('version', [102, 105])
def test_mysql_convergence_creates_both_branch_tables(monkeypatch, version):
    from app.migrations import mysql_baseline as module
    connection = Mock()
    connection.exec_driver_sql.return_value.scalar_one_or_none.return_value = version
    tables = {name: Mock() for name in Base.metadata.tables}
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, '_require_mysql', lambda c: None)
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, 'mysql_metadata', lambda: Mock(tables=tables))
    module.upgrade_mysql_schema(connection, expected=106)
    for name in ('design_case_samples', 'incremental_automation_controls'):
        tables[name].create.assert_called_once_with(connection, checkfirst=True)
    assert connection.exec_driver_sql.call_args.args[1] == (106, 'converge_case_and_incremental_branches')

def _engine():
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    return engine

def test_model_3d_su_has_three_tracks_and_document_weights() -> None:
    contract = build_model_3d_su_contract()
    classification_map = build_model_3d_su_classification_map()
    dimensions = build_model_3d_su_subcategory_dimensions()
    track_keys = {track["key"] for track in contract["track_classification"]["tracks"]}

    validate_classification_map(classification_map, valid_track_keys=track_keys)
    assert track_keys == {"space_building", "soft_furnishing", "functional_model"}
    assert set(dimensions) == track_keys
    assert classification_map["category_to_subcategory"]["电子电器"] == "functional_model"
    assert classification_map["category_to_subcategory"]["家具"] == "soft_furnishing"

    expected_weights = {
        "space_building": [0.20, 0.25, 0.20, 0.20, 0.15],
        "soft_furnishing": [0.25, 0.25, 0.20, 0.20, 0.10],
        # The source document's functional-model proportions are 35:25:20:15:10
        # (105 total).  The persisted contract keeps that relative priority but
        # normalizes it to a strict 1.0 weight sum.
        "functional_model": [35 / 105, 25 / 105, 20 / 105, 15 / 105, 10 / 105],
    }
    for track_key, config in dimensions.items():
        validate_subcategory_dimensions(config)
        rows = config["common_group"]["schema_definition"]["dimensions"]
        assert [row["weight"] for row in rows] == pytest.approx(
            expected_weights[track_key]
        )
        assert sum(row["weight"] for row in rows) == pytest.approx(1.0)
        assert all(
            row["grade_points"]
            == {"1": 0.0, "2": 25.0, "3": 50.0, "4": 75.0, "5": 100.0}
            for row in rows
        )
        assert {
            rule["deduction"]
            for row in rows
            for rule in row["deduction_rules"]
        } == {20, 50, 80}
        assert {
            rule["rule_id"]
            for row in rows
            for rule in row["deduction_rules"]
        } == {"minor_defect", "obvious_defect", "severe_defect"}
        assert all(
            len(rule["description"]) >= 12
            for row in rows
            for rule in row["deduction_rules"]
        )
        assert config["grade_output_contract"] == {
            "format_version": "dimension-grade-output-v1",
            "require_exact_keys": True,
            "evidence_required": True,
        }
        assert rule_scoring_mode(config) == "deduction_v1"

def test_model_3d_su_seed_is_idempotent_and_preserves_operator_rows() -> None:
    engine = _engine()
    with Session(engine) as db:
        db.add(ModelConfig(active=True))
        db.commit()
        settings = SimpleNamespace(project_root=PROJECT_ROOT)

        seed_model_3d_su(db, settings)
        db.commit()
        first_profile = db.scalar(
            select(EvaluationCategoryProfile).where(
                EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key == MODEL_3D_SU_CATEGORY_KEY
            )
        )
        first_config = db.scalar(
            select(CategoryEvaluationV3Config).where(
                CategoryEvaluationV3Config.category_key == MODEL_3D_SU_CATEGORY_KEY
            )
        )
        assert first_profile is not None
        assert first_config is not None
        assert first_profile.status == "active"
        assert first_config.status == "active"
        assert first_profile.rubric_version == MODEL_3D_SU_RUBRIC_VERSION
        assert first_config.revision == 1
        assert first_config.projected_revision_id is not None

        first_profile.description = "运营已编辑描述"
        db.commit()
        seed_model_3d_su(db, settings)
        db.commit()

        prompts = db.scalars(
            select(PromptVersion).where(
                PromptVersion.category_key == MODEL_3D_SU_CATEGORY_KEY
            )
        ).all()
        assert {prompt.version for prompt in prompts} == {
            MODEL_3D_SU_CALL_A_VERSION,
            MODEL_3D_SU_CALL_B_VERSION,
        }
        assert first_config.revision == 1
        assert first_profile.description == "运营已编辑描述"
        assert db.scalar(
            select(PromptVersion).where(
                PromptVersion.version == MODEL_3D_SU_CALL_A_VERSION
            )
        ).status == "published"

def test_model_3d_su_seed_isolates_operator_same_version_prompt_collision() -> None:
    engine = _engine()
    with Session(engine) as db:
        db.add(ModelConfig(active=True))
        prompt = PromptVersion(
            stage="A",
            category_key=MODEL_3D_SU_CATEGORY_KEY,
            pipeline_scope="shared",
            name="3D/SU 模型分类与字段预检",
            version=MODEL_3D_SU_CALL_A_VERSION,
            system_prompt=(
                PROJECT_ROOT / "backend/prompts/model_3d_su_call_a_v3.txt"
            ).read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip(),
            user_prompt="operator poison",
            rubric_version=MODEL_3D_SU_RUBRIC_VERSION,
            status="draft",
            source="manual",
            change_note="operator collision",
            created_by="operator:prompt-owner",
        )
        db.add(prompt)
        db.commit()

        seed_model_3d_su(db, SimpleNamespace(project_root=PROJECT_ROOT))
        db.commit()

        rows = db.scalars(
            select(PromptVersion).where(
                PromptVersion.category_key == MODEL_3D_SU_CATEGORY_KEY,
                PromptVersion.stage == "A",
            )
        ).all()
        assert len(rows) == 2
        operator_row = next(row for row in rows if row.created_by == "operator:prompt-owner")
        system_row = next(row for row in rows if row.created_by == MODEL_3D_SU_CREATED_BY)
        assert operator_row.version == MODEL_3D_SU_CALL_A_VERSION
        assert system_row.version.startswith(f"{MODEL_3D_SU_CALL_A_VERSION}-system-seed-")
        config = db.scalar(
            select(CategoryEvaluationV3Config).where(
                CategoryEvaluationV3Config.category_key == MODEL_3D_SU_CATEGORY_KEY
            )
        )
        assert config is not None
        assert json.loads(config.contract_json)["prompt_bindings"]["call_a_version"] == system_row.version

def test_model_3d_su_seed_rejects_operator_owned_same_spec_projection_repair() -> None:
    engine = _engine()
    with Session(engine) as db:
        db.add(ModelConfig(active=True))
        db.commit()
        settings = SimpleNamespace(project_root=PROJECT_ROOT)

        seed_model_3d_su(db, settings)
        db.commit()
        config = db.scalar(
            select(CategoryEvaluationV3Config).where(
                CategoryEvaluationV3Config.category_key == MODEL_3D_SU_CATEGORY_KEY
            )
        )
        assert config is not None
        original_fields = {
            "display_name": config.display_name,
            "status": config.status,
            "contract_json": config.contract_json,
            "classification_map_json": config.classification_map_json,
            "subcategory_dimensions_json": config.subcategory_dimensions_json,

        config.created_by = "operator:category-owner"
        config.projected_revision_id = None

            seed_model_3d_su(db, settings)

        assert config.created_by == "operator:category-owner"
        assert config.projected_revision_id is None
        for field, expected in original_fields.items():
            assert getattr(config, field) == expected


def test_model_3d_su_seed_appends_revision_when_system_spec_is_outdated() -> None:
    engine = _engine()
    with Session(engine) as db:
        db.add(ModelConfig(active=True))
        db.commit()
        settings = SimpleNamespace(project_root=PROJECT_ROOT)

        seed_model_3d_su(db, settings)
        db.commit()
        config = db.scalar(
            select(CategoryEvaluationV3Config).where(
                CategoryEvaluationV3Config.category_key == MODEL_3D_SU_CATEGORY_KEY
            )
        )
        assert config is not None
        assert config.projected_revision_id is not None
        original_revision_id = config.projected_revision_id

        legacy_contract = json.loads(config.contract_json)
        legacy_contract["spec_version"] = "model-3d-su-v0-test"
        # 故意造出防护上线之前就可能存在的「运行时改了、冻结版本没跟上」状态：
        # 这正是种子要修的形态，构造它需要显式豁免写入防护。
        with reconciling_projection():
            config.contract_json = json.dumps(
                legacy_contract, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True
            )
            config.contract_hash = "legacy-test-hash"
            db.commit()

        seed_model_3d_su(db, settings)
        db.commit()

        assert config.revision == 2
        assert config.projected_revision_id != original_revision_id
        original_revision = db.get(CategoryEvaluationV3Revision, original_revision_id)
        active_revision = db.get(CategoryEvaluationV3Revision, config.projected_revision_id)
        assert original_revision is not None
        assert original_revision.status == "retired"
        assert active_revision is not None
        assert active_revision.status == "active"
        assert active_revision.parent_revision_id == original_revision_id


def test_model_3d_su_prompts_keep_common_fields_and_forbid_final_level() -> None:
    engine = _engine()
    with Session(engine) as db:
        db.add(ModelConfig(active=True))
        db.commit()
        seed_model_3d_su(db, SimpleNamespace(project_root=PROJECT_ROOT))
        db.commit()

        prompt_a = db.scalar(
            select(PromptVersion).where(PromptVersion.version == MODEL_3D_SU_CALL_A_VERSION)
        )
        prompt_b = db.scalar(
            select(PromptVersion).where(PromptVersion.version == MODEL_3D_SU_CALL_B_VERSION)
        )
        assert prompt_a is not None and prompt_b is not None
        assert prompt_a.rubric_version == MODEL_3D_SU_RUBRIC_VERSION
        assert '"production_fields"' in prompt_a.system_prompt
        assert '"model_3d_su_fields"' in prompt_a.system_prompt
        assert "predicted_level" not in prompt_a.system_prompt
        assert "不得输出 final_level" in prompt_b.system_prompt
        assert "任何最终等级" in prompt_b.system_prompt
        assert "全部为 5" in prompt_b.system_prompt
        assert "全部为 4" in prompt_b.system_prompt
        assert "全部为 3" in prompt_b.system_prompt
        assert "全部为 1 或 2" in prompt_b.system_prompt


def test_model_3d_su_seed_upgrades_known_v1_rows_once_without_rewriting_history() -> None:
    engine = _engine()
    with Session(engine) as db:
        model = ModelConfig(active=True)
        db.add(model)
        db.flush()
        prompt_a_v1 = PromptVersion(
            stage="A",
            category_key=MODEL_3D_SU_CATEGORY_KEY,

            user_prompt="",

            status="published",
            source="imported",
            created_by="system:model-3d-su-v1",
        )
        prompt_b_v1 = PromptVersion(
            stage="B",
            category_key=MODEL_3D_SU_CATEGORY_KEY,
            pipeline_scope="shared",
            name="3D/SU v1 B",
            version="model-3d-su-b-v1-20260814",
            system_prompt=(PROJECT_ROOT / "backend/prompts/model_3d_su_call_b_v1.txt").read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip(),
            user_prompt="",
            rubric_version="model-3d-su-rubric-v1",
            status="published",
            source="imported",
            created_by="system:model-3d-su-v1",
        )
        db.add_all([prompt_a_v1, prompt_b_v1])
        db.flush()
        profile = EvaluationCategoryProfile(
            category_key=MODEL_3D_SU_CATEGORY_KEY,
            display_name="3D & SU 模型",
            description="运营已编辑描述",
            status="active",
            allowed_mime_types_json=canonical_json(
                ["image/jpeg", "image/png", "image/webp", "image/gif"]
            ),
            preprocess_config_json=canonical_json(
                {"preprocess": "image", "su_unrendered_marker": True}
            ),
            pipeline_config_json=canonical_json(model_3d_su_pipeline()),
            pipeline_revision=1,
            prompt_a_id=prompt_a_v1.id,
            prompt_b_id=prompt_b_v1.id,
            model_config_id=model.id,
            rubric_version="model-3d-su-rubric-v1",
            created_by="system:model-3d-su-v1",
        )
        legacy_contract = build_model_3d_su_contract()
        legacy_contract["spec_version"] = "model-3d-su-v1-dingtalk-20260814"
        legacy_contract["prompt_bindings"] = {
            "call_a_version": prompt_a_v1.version,
            "call_b_version": prompt_b_v1.version,
        }
        legacy_dimensions = build_model_3d_su_subcategory_dimensions()
        for config in legacy_dimensions.values():
            config.pop("grade_output_contract", None)
            for dimension in config["common_group"]["schema_definition"]["dimensions"]:
                dimension.pop("grade_points", None)
                dimension["deduction_rules"] = [
                    {
                        "rule_id": "minor_defect",
                        "description": "微瑕：可见轻微缺陷",
                        "deduction": 20,
                        "tags": ["模型美感"],
                    },
                    {
                        "rule_id": "obvious_defect",
                        "description": "明显缺陷：可见明显问题",
                        "deduction": 50,
                        "tags": ["模型美感"],
                    },
                    {
                        "rule_id": "severe_defect",
                        "description": "严重硬伤：可见严重问题",
                        "deduction": 80,
                        "tags": ["模型美感"],
                    },
                ]
        legacy_config = CategoryEvaluationV3Config(
            category_key=MODEL_3D_SU_CATEGORY_KEY,
            display_name="3D & SU 模型",
            revision=1,
            status="active",
            contract_json=canonical_json(legacy_contract),
            classification_map_json=canonical_json(
                build_model_3d_su_classification_map()
            ),
            subcategory_dimensions_json=canonical_json(legacy_dimensions),
            dimension_deduction_rules_json=canonical_json(
                extract_dimension_deduction_rules(legacy_dimensions)
            ),
            media_penalty_enabled=False,
            contract_hash=canonical_contract_hash(legacy_contract),
            created_by="system:model-3d-su-v1",
        )
        db.add_all([profile, legacy_config])
        db.flush()
        legacy_revision = ensure_projected_revision(db, legacy_config)
        legacy_revision_contract = legacy_revision.contract_json
        db.commit()

        settings = SimpleNamespace(project_root=PROJECT_ROOT)
        seed_model_3d_su(db, settings)
        db.commit()
        first_v2_revision_id = legacy_config.projected_revision_id
        first_v2_revision_number = legacy_config.revision
        seed_model_3d_su(db, settings)
        db.commit()

        prompts = db.scalars(
            select(PromptVersion).where(
                PromptVersion.category_key == MODEL_3D_SU_CATEGORY_KEY
            )
        ).all()
        assert MODEL_3D_SU_CALL_A_VERSION == "model-3d-su-a-v4-20260821"
        assert MODEL_3D_SU_CALL_B_VERSION == "model-3d-su-b-v4-20260821"
        assert {prompt.version for prompt in prompts} == {
            prompt_a_v1.version,
            prompt_b_v1.version,
            MODEL_3D_SU_CALL_A_VERSION,
            MODEL_3D_SU_CALL_B_VERSION,
        }
        assert profile.prompt_a_id != prompt_a_v1.id
        assert profile.prompt_b_id != prompt_b_v1.id
        assert profile.rubric_version == MODEL_3D_SU_RUBRIC_VERSION
        assert profile.description == "运营已编辑描述"
        assert legacy_config.revision == first_v2_revision_number == 2
        assert legacy_config.projected_revision_id == first_v2_revision_id
        assert legacy_revision.status == "retired"
        assert legacy_revision.contract_json == legacy_revision_contract
        active_revision = db.get(
            CategoryEvaluationV3Revision, legacy_config.projected_revision_id
        )
        assert active_revision.status == "active"
        assert active_revision.parent_revision_id == legacy_revision.id


def test_model_3d_su_semantic_contract_seed_is_draft_and_platform_wide() -> None:
    contract = build_model_3d_su_semantic_contract()
    assert set(contract["semantic_schema"]["fields"]) >= {

        db.commit()

def test_seed_appends_v2_draft_without_activating_or_overwriting_v1() -> None:
    engine = _engine()
    with Session(engine) as db:
        db.add(ModelConfig(active=True))
        db.commit()
        settings = SimpleNamespace(project_root=PROJECT_ROOT)
        seed_model_3d_su(db, settings)
        db.commit()
        first_rows = db.scalars(
            select(TagDemandContract)
            .where(
                TagDemandContract.contract_key == MODEL_3D_SU_SEMANTIC_CONTRACT_KEY
            )
            .order_by(TagDemandContract.version)
        ).all()
        assert len(first_rows) == 1
        assert first_rows[0].status == "draft"
        assert json.loads(first_rows[0].definition_json)["schema_version"] == (
            "tag-demand-contract-v2"
        )
        first_hash = first_rows[0].contract_hash

        seed_model_3d_su(db, settings)
        db.commit()
        second_rows = db.scalars(
            select(TagDemandContract)
            .where(
                TagDemandContract.contract_key == MODEL_3D_SU_SEMANTIC_CONTRACT_KEY
            )
            .order_by(TagDemandContract.version)
        ).all()
        assert len(second_rows) == 1
        assert second_rows[0].contract_hash == first_hash
        assert second_rows[0].approved_by is None
    engine.dispose()


def test_seed_appends_v2_after_existing_v1_without_retiring_operator_contract() -> None:
    from tests.test_semantic_tag_contracts import valid_contract

    engine = _engine()
    with Session(engine) as db:
        db.add_all(
            [
                ModelConfig(active=True),
                TagDemandContract(
                    contract_key=MODEL_3D_SU_SEMANTIC_CONTRACT_KEY,
                    version=1,
                    status="active",
                    definition_json=json.dumps(
                        valid_contract(), ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True
                    ),
                    contract_hash="f" * 64,
                    created_by="operator:semantic-owner",
                ),
            ]
        )
        db.commit()

        seed_model_3d_su(db, SimpleNamespace(project_root=PROJECT_ROOT))
        db.commit()

        rows = db.scalars(
            select(TagDemandContract)
            .where(
                TagDemandContract.contract_key == MODEL_3D_SU_SEMANTIC_CONTRACT_KEY
            )
            .order_by(TagDemandContract.version)
        ).all()
        assert [(row.version, row.status) for row in rows] == [
            (1, "active"),
            (2, "draft"),
        ]
        assert rows[0].created_by == "operator:semantic-owner"
        assert json.loads(rows[0].definition_json)["schema_version"] == (
            "tag-demand-contract-v1"
        )
        assert json.loads(rows[1].definition_json)["schema_version"] == (
            "tag-demand-contract-v2"
        )
    engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_model_3d_su_semantic_slice.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.label_governance import (
    approve_semantic_facts,
    create_release,
    publish_release,
    resolve_semantic_execution_route,
)
from app.models import AssetVersion, ContentRecord, SemanticTagFact, TagDemandContract
from app.model_3d_su_category_seed import seed_model_3d_su
from app.projection_contracts import LocalProjectionAdapter, build_projection_manifest, create_contract_version
from app.schema_adapter import attach_semantic_candidates
from app.semantic_tag_mapping import map_standard_entities

from tests.test_unified_label_platform import _client, _database


PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
FIXTURE = json.loads((PROJECT_ROOT / "backend/fixtures/semantic/model_3d_su_domestic_v1.json").read_text())


def run_fixture_slice(*, asset_scope: str) -> dict:
    sample = next(item for item in FIXTURE["samples"] if item["asset_scope"] == asset_scope)
    engine, db, user, result = _database()
    client = _client(db, user)
    try:
        result.asset.category_key = "model_3d_su"
        result.asset.sha256 = sample["asset_sha256"]
        result.job.category_key = "model_3d_su"
        seed_model_3d_su(db, SimpleNamespace(project_root=PROJECT_ROOT))
        db.flush()
        draft = db.query(TagDemandContract).filter(TagDemandContract.contract_key == "semantic-platform").one()
        verification = client.post("/api/source-identity-verifications", json={
            "contract_key": "semantic-platform",
            "source_system": "aliyun_3d66_dw",
            "key_fields": ["res_type", "ll_id"],
            "result": "verified",
            "probe_hash": "a" * 64,
            "data_window": "fixture-window",
            "scoped_row_count": 2,
            "duplicate_key_count": 0,
            "res_id_conflict_count": 0,
        })
        assert verification.status_code == 201, verification.text
        approved = client.post(
            f"/api/source-identity-verifications/{verification.json()['id']}/approve"
        )
        assert approved.status_code == 200, approved.text
        candidate = client.post(
            f"/api/tag-demand-contracts/{draft.id}/bind-source-identity-verification",
            json={"verification_id": approved.json()["id"]},
        )
        assert candidate.status_code == 200, candidate.text
        activated = client.post(f"/api/tag-demand-contracts/{candidate.json()['id']}/activate")
        assert activated.status_code == 200, activated.text
        contract = db.get(TagDemandContract, candidate.json()["id"])
        asset_version = AssetVersion(
            asset_id=result.asset_id,
            version=1,
            asset_sha256=sample["asset_sha256"],
            source_version=f"fixture-{sample['key']}",
            created_by="fixture",
        )
        db.add(asset_version)
        db.add(ContentRecord(
            source_system="fixture",
            source_content_id=sample["key"],
            category_key="model_3d_su",
            source_version="v1",
            source_occurred_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
            asset_id=result.asset_id,
            status="ready",
        ))
        db.flush()
        route = resolve_semantic_execution_route(
            db,
            content_record=db.query(ContentRecord).filter(ContentRecord.asset_id == result.asset_id).one(),
            asset_version=asset_version,
            site_scope="domestic",
            asset_scope=asset_scope,
            locale="zh",
            prompt_variant=asset_scope,
            prompt_version=f"model-3d-su-semantic-{asset_scope}-v1",
            model_version="fixture-model-v1",
        )
        precheck = attach_semantic_candidates(
            json.loads(result.precheck_json),
            route=route,
            provider_payload={"semantic": sample["semantic"]},
            evidence_prefix=f"evaluation:{result.id}",
        )
        result.precheck_json = canonical_json(precheck)
        for field_key, bundle in precheck["semantic_candidates"].items():
            from app.semantic_tag_mapping import candidate_bundle, candidate
            mapped = map_standard_entities(
                bundle=candidate_bundle(field_key=field_key, values=[candidate(
                    item["value"], locale=item["locale"], rank=item["rank"], weight=item["weight"], evidence_ref=item["evidence_ref"],
                ) for item in bundle]),
                mapping_registry=FIXTURE["mapping_registry"][field_key],
                normalization_version="semantic-normalization-v1",
                mapping_version="kg-entity-map-v1",
            )
            values = [{
                "value": value.localized_names.get("zh", value.entity_id),
                "entity_id": value.entity_id,
                "localized_names": dict(value.localized_names),
                "locale": "zh",
                "rank": value.rank,
                "weight": value.weight,
                "evidence_ref": value.evidence_refs[0],
            } for value in mapped.values]
            db.add(SemanticTagFact(
                asset_version_id=asset_version.id,
                field_key=field_key,
                fact_version=1,
                field_status="needs_review",
                values_json=canonical_json(values),
                evidence_json=canonical_json(list(mapped.evidence_refs)),
                source_evaluation_id=result.id,
                contract_id=contract.id,
                normalization_version="semantic-normalization-v1",
                mapping_version="kg-entity-map-v1",
                status="candidate",
                payload_hash="f" * 64,
            ))
        db.flush()
        approve_semantic_facts(db, evaluation_id=result.id, actor=user.username)
        release, _ = create_release(db, release_key=f"fixture-{sample['key']}", evaluation_id=result.id, content_key=None, requested_by=user.username)
        published, _ = publish_release(db, release=release, actor=user.username)
        projection = create_contract_version(
            db,
            contract_key=f"fixture-{sample['key']}-projection",
            target_role="unified_dimension",
            table_name="unified_dimension_table",
            environment="local",
            primary_key=["content_key"],
            field_mappings={
                "content_key": "content_key",
                "space": "semantic.space.primary_name.zh",
                "object": "semantic.object.weighted_names.zh",
                "is_single": "provenance.is_single",
            },
            input_versions={"label_schema_version": "published-label-v2"},
            mode="snapshot",
            idempotency_key_template="{content_key}:{label_version}",
            checkpoint={"kind": "published_label_id"},
            reconciliation={"checks": ["row_count", "payload_hash", "version"]},
            rollback={"strategy": "rebuild"},
            owner="fixture",
            status="draft",
            created_by="fixture",
        )
        manifest, manifest_body = build_projection_manifest(db, contract=projection)
        adapter = LocalProjectionAdapter()
        adapter.apply(db, contract=projection, manifest=manifest)
        reconciliation = adapter.reconcile(db, contract=projection, manifest=manifest)
        return {
            "route": route.__dict__,
            "published_label": json.loads(published.label_payload_json),
            "projection_row": manifest_body["rows"][0],
            "reconciliation": reconciliation.__dict__,
        }
    finally:
        client.close()
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("asset_scope", "expected_is_single"), [("whole", 0), ("single", 1)])
def test_domestic_3d_su_slice_reaches_dry_run_projection(asset_scope: str, expected_is_single: int) -> None:
    result = run_fixture_slice(asset_scope=asset_scope)
    assert result["route"]["category_key"] == "model_3d_su"
    assert result["published_label"]["schema_version"] == "published-label-v2"
    assert result["projection_row"]["is_single"] == expected_is_single
    assert result["reconciliation"]["status"] == "matched"

    result = run_fixture_slice(asset_scope="whole")
    assert "semantic" in result["published_label"]
    assert "quality" in result["published_label"]
    assert result["published_label"]["quality"]["level"] in {"L1", "L2", "L3", "L4"}

def test_single_variant_marks_space_not_applicable() -> None:
    from app.model_3d_su_category_seed import build_model_3d_su_semantic_contract

    contract = build_model_3d_su_semantic_contract()
    single = next(
        item for item in contract["execution_variants"] if item["asset_scope"] == "single"
    )
    whole = next(
        item for item in contract["execution_variants"] if item["asset_scope"] == "whole"
    )
    assert single["field_applicability_overrides"]["space"] == "not_applicable"
    assert whole["field_applicability_overrides"] == {}


def test_model_3d_su_contract_covers_jiaxin_fields_and_all_four_routes() -> None:
    from app.model_3d_su_category_seed import build_model_3d_su_semantic_contract

    contract = build_model_3d_su_semantic_contract()
    fields = contract["semantic_schema"]["fields"]
    assert {"form", "structure"} <= set(fields)
    assert fields["form"]["max_values"] == 3
    assert fields["structure"]["max_values"] == 3
    assert set(contract["field_supply"]) == set(fields)
    assert {
        (
            variant["site_scope"],
            variant["asset_scope"],
            variant["locale"],
        )
        for variant in contract["execution_variants"]
    } == {
        ("domestic", "whole", "zh"),
        ("domestic", "single", "zh"),
        ("overseas", "whole", "en"),
        ("overseas", "single", "en"),
    }
    assert {
        (target["target_key"], target["locale"])
        for target in contract["projection_targets"]
    } == {
        ("domestic_material_tags", "zh"),
        ("overseas_material_tags", "en"),
        ("knowledge_graph", "zh"),
        ("knowledge_graph", "en"),
    }

===== FILE backend/tests/test_model_3d_su_strict_release.py =====
"""3D/SU 最新机制严格结构化输出与旧链路投影。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.dimension_deduction_bridge import (
    DimensionDeductionBridgeError,
    build_dimension_deduction_output_contract,
    normalize_dimension_deduction_output,
)
from app.model_3d_su_legacy_projection import legacy_3d_su_projection
from app.model_3d_su_strict_release import attach_model_3d_su_strict_contracts
from app.models import WorkflowDefinition, WorkflowVersion
from app.prompt_output_contract import parse_output_contract, validate_output_payload
from app.schema_adapter import validate_production_correction
from app.strict_prompt_contracts import strict_output_contract
from app.workflow_registry import persisted_workflow_manifest
from tests.test_strict_prompt_release import _prepare_database

RISK_KEYS = ["watermark", "unrendered", "brand_trademark", "brand_name", "chinese", "film_ip",
             "celebrities", "game_ip", "other_ip", "qr_code", "landmark", "religion"]


def _call_a_payload(**overrides):
    risk = {key: "" for key in RISK_KEYS}
    risk["high_risk"] = []
    payload = {
        "classification": {"scope_status": "in_scope", "primary_category": "家具", "primary_confidence": 0.9,
                            "evidence": ["可见家具"], "doc_primary": "家具模型", "doc_secondary": "", "doc_tertiary": "", "doc_group": "软装配景"},
        "production_fields": {"title": "现代沙发", "seotitle": "现代简约布艺沙发效果图", "category": "家具", "style": "现代",
                               "tags": ["沙发", "现代", "布艺", "客厅"], "cons": "仅达到常规可用", "design": "简洁", "score": 70,
                               "reason": ["cg_model"], "image_defects": "CG_GAME_ANIMATION_REDLINE", "trait": "3D数字效果图"},
        "image_quality": {"quality_severity": "moderate"},
        "media_form": {"rendered_status": {"status": "yes", "confidence": 0.9, "evidence": ["已渲染"]}},
        "model_3d_su_fields": risk,
    }
    for key, value in overrides.items():
        payload["model_3d_su_fields"][key] = value
    return payload


def test_call_a_contract_accepts_v29_output_with_machine_markers() -> None:
    contract = parse_output_contract(strict_output_contract("model_3d_su", "A", evaluation_contract={}))
    validate_output_payload(contract, _call_a_payload(watermark="有水印"))
    schema = strict_output_contract("model_3d_su", "A", evaluation_contract={})["schema"]
    assert set(schema["properties"]["model_3d_su_fields"]["properties"]) == set(RISK_KEYS) | {"high_risk"}
    assert "doc_primary" in schema["properties"]["classification"]["properties"]


def test_call_b_contract_follows_configured_rules_and_bridge_validation() -> None:
    from app.model_3d_su_category_seed import build_model_3d_su_subcategory_dimensions

    config = {"subcategory_dimensions": build_model_3d_su_subcategory_dimensions()}
    contract = build_dimension_deduction_output_contract(config, name="model_3d_su_call_b")
    schema = contract["schema"]
    dims = schema["properties"]["dimensions"]["properties"]
    assert set(dims) == {"model_detail", "material_rendering", "lighting", "design_trend", "visual_composition"}
    rule_ids = dims["model_detail"]["properties"]["hit_rules"]["items"]["properties"]["rule_id"]["enum"]
    assert rule_ids == ["minor_defect", "obvious_defect", "severe_defect"]
    assert "aesthetic_score" in schema["required"]
    parsed = parse_output_contract(contract)
    payload = {
        "aesthetic_score": 62, "aesthetic_evidence": ["整体普通"], "aesthetic_confidence": 0.7,
        "dimensions": {
            key: {"evaluation": "", "hit_rules": ([{"rule_id": "obvious_defect", "confidence": "high", "evidence": "倒角缺失"}] if key == "model_detail" else [])}
            for key in dims
        },
        "overall_note": "中等",
    }
    validate_output_payload(parsed, payload)
    # 合同放行的输出，桥接校验也必须接受（同源）
    track = config["subcategory_dimensions"]["space_building"]
    normalized = normalize_dimension_deduction_output(payload, track)
    assert normalized["dimensions"]["model_detail"]["hit_rules"][0]["rule_id"] == "obvious_defect"
    bad = {**payload, "dimensions": {**payload["dimensions"], "model_detail": {"evaluation": "", "hit_rules": [{"rule_id": "unknown", "confidence": "high", "evidence": "x"}]}}}
    with pytest.raises(Exception):
        validate_output_payload(parsed, bad)


def test_legacy_projection_compresses_risk_fields_into_single_values() -> None:
    stage = _call_a_payload(watermark="有水印", brand_name="有知名品牌名称", qr_code="有二维码")
    projected = legacy_3d_su_projection(stage, score=58)
    assert projected["reason"] == "有水印" and projected["hwreason"] == "有知名品牌名称"
    assert projected["score"] == 58 and projected["title"] == "现代沙发" and len(projected["tags"]) == 4
    assert any("同时命中" in note for note in projected["notes"])
    unrendered_su = legacy_3d_su_projection(_call_a_payload(unrendered="是未渲染模型图", other_ip="是其它热门IP"), score=None, res_type=6)
    assert unrendered_su["reason"] == "是未渲染模型图" and unrendered_su["hwreason"] == "是热门IP形象"
    assert unrendered_su["score"] is None
    unrendered_max = legacy_3d_su_projection(_call_a_payload(unrendered="是未渲染模型图"), score=40, res_type=1)
    assert unrendered_max["reason"] == "" and any("Max" in note for note in unrendered_max["notes"])
    illegal = legacy_3d_su_projection(_call_a_payload(high_risk=["涉政元素"]), score=10)
    assert illegal["reason"] == "是色情暴力违规涉政内容"


def test_legacy_projection_ignores_negated_illegal_phrases_and_reads_defect_lists() -> None:
    for phrase in ("无违规内容", "未发现色情、暴力内容", "不涉政", "不是色情暴力违规涉政内容", "no nsfw"):
        negated = legacy_3d_su_projection(_call_a_payload(high_risk=[phrase]), score=70)
        assert negated["reason"] == "", phrase
    stage = _call_a_payload()
    stage["production_fields"]["image_defects"] = ["涉政标语"]
    assert legacy_3d_su_projection(stage, score=10)["reason"] == "是色情暴力违规涉政内容"
    mixed = legacy_3d_su_projection(_call_a_payload(high_risk=["无违规内容", "暴力画面"]), score=10)
    assert mixed["reason"] == "是色情暴力违规涉政内容"


def test_correction_validation_accepts_markers_and_risk_fields() -> None:
    validate_production_correction("production_fields.image_defects", "CG_GAME_ANIMATION_REDLINE, UNRENDERED_WHITE_MODEL")
    validate_production_correction("model_3d_su_fields.watermark", "有水印")
    validate_production_correction("model_3d_su_fields.watermark", "")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        validate_production_correction("model_3d_su_fields.watermark", "有水印吗")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        validate_production_correction("model_3d_su_fields.unknown", "")


def test_attach_strict_contracts_generates_new_mechanism_version(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = _prepare_database(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            from app.workflow_working_copies import editable_workflow_template, get_or_create_workflow_working_copy

            definition = db.scalar(select(WorkflowDefinition).where(WorkflowDefinition.workflow_key == "model_3d_su.evaluation"))
            template = editable_workflow_template(db, definition.id)
            get_or_create_workflow_working_copy(db, workflow_definition_id=definition.id, initial_manifest=template.manifest, actor="system:3d-su-strict")
            db.commit()
            preview = attach_model_3d_su_strict_contracts(db, actor="system:3d-su-strict", apply=False)
            db.rollback()
            assert preview["mode"] == "preview" and preview["contract_a"]["name"] == "model_3d_su_call_a"
            assert preview["contract_b"]["dimensions"]
            before = db.scalar(select(WorkflowVersion).where(WorkflowVersion.workflow_definition_id == definition.id).order_by(WorkflowVersion.id.desc()).limit(1))
            receipt = attach_model_3d_su_strict_contracts(db, actor="system:3d-su-strict", apply=True)
            db.commit()
            assert receipt["validation_ok"] is True, receipt.get("validation_errors")
            assert receipt["workflow_version_id"] != (before.id if before else None)
            version = db.get(WorkflowVersion, receipt["workflow_version_id"])
            manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(version)
            prompts = {node["node_instance_id"]: node for node in manifest["nodes"] if node["kind"] == "prompt"}
            assert prompts["prompt_a"]["config"]["output_contract"]["mode"] == "json_schema"
            assert prompts["prompt_a"]["config"]["output_contract"]["name"] == "model_3d_su_call_a"
            assert prompts["prompt_b"]["config"]["output_contract"]["name"] == "model_3d_su_call_b"
            assert set(prompts["prompt_b"]["config"]["output_contract"]["schema"]["properties"]["dimensions"]["properties"]) >= {"model_detail", "lighting"}
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_model_media_settlement.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace
import os
import subprocess
import sys

import pytest
from PIL import Image
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database import Base
from app.model_media_usage import (
    ModelMediaItem,
    ModelMediaPolicy,
    ModelMediaUsageError,
    prepare_model_media,
    record_prepared_media,
    settle_media_usage,
)
from app.model_pricing import doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing
from app.models import WorkflowModelInvocation, WorkflowModelMediaUsage
from app.vision_input_policy import default_vision_input_policy, seed_20_capability


def _image(path: Path, *, size: tuple[int, int], color: tuple[int, int, int]) -> Path:
    Image.new("RGB", size, color).save(path, format="PNG")
    return path


def _sha(path: Path) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def _bundle(tmp_path: Path, *, explicit: bool = False):
    target = _image(tmp_path / "target.png", size=(640, 480), color=(1, 2, 3))
    anchor = _image(tmp_path / "anchor.png", size=(1200, 900), color=(4, 5, 6))
    capability = seed_20_capability()
    vision_policy = default_vision_input_policy(capability)
    media_policy = (
        ModelMediaPolicy(
            version="vision-input-policy-v1",
            target=vision_policy.roles["target"],
            anchor=vision_policy.roles["anchor"],
        )
        if explicit
        else ModelMediaPolicy()
    )
    return prepare_model_media(
        (
            ModelMediaItem(
                role="target",
                ordinal=0,
                source_path=target,
                mime_type="image/png",
                content_sha256=_sha(target),
                asset_id=11,
            ),
            ModelMediaItem(
                role="anchor",
                ordinal=0,
                source_path=anchor,
                mime_type="image/png",
                content_sha256=_sha(anchor),
                asset_id=12,
                anchor_level="L3",
            ),
        ),
        cache_root=tmp_path / "cache",
        policy=media_policy,
        capability=capability if explicit else None,
    )


def _invocation() -> WorkflowModelInvocation:
    return WorkflowModelInvocation(
        baseline_run_id=None,
        production_run_id=1,
        step_attempt_id=1,
        node_instance_id="prompt_b",
        status="reserved",
        provider="doubao",
        model_id="doubao-test",
        reserved_input_tokens=10_000,
        reserved_output_tokens=1_000,
        estimated_cost_micros=50_000,
        evidence_json="{}",
    )


def test_prepared_media_rows_exist_before_dispatch_and_recording_is_idempotent(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'record.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            invocation = _invocation()
            db.add(invocation)
            db.flush()
            bundle = _bundle(tmp_path)

            first = record_prepared_media(db, invocation.id, bundle)
            second = record_prepared_media(db, invocation.id, bundle)

            assert [row.id for row in first] == [row.id for row in second]
            assert [(row.media_role, row.ordinal) for row in first] == [
                ("target", 0),
                ("anchor", 0),
            ]
            assert all(row.settled_at is None for row in first)
            assert all(row.usage_source == "unavailable" for row in first)
            assert all(row.image_tokens == 0 for row in first)
            rendered = "".join(row.evidence_json for row in first)
            assert str(tmp_path) not in rendered
            assert "source_path" not in rendered
            assert "sent_path" not in rendered
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_provider_per_image_usage_settles_actual_provenance_and_is_idempotent(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'actual.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            invocation = _invocation()
            db.add(invocation)
            db.flush()
            rows = record_prepared_media(
                db, invocation.id, _bundle(tmp_path, explicit=True)
            )
            usage = {
                "tokens_by_identity": {"target:0": 120, "anchor:0": 40},
                "total_image_tokens": 160,
                "request_correlation_id": "req-media-1",
            }
            pricing = {
                "version": "model-config-pricing-v1",
                "input_micros_per_million_tokens": 2_000_000,
            }
            first = settle_media_usage(
                db,
                invocation.id,
                provider_usage=usage,
                pricing_snapshot=pricing,
            )
            second = settle_media_usage(
                db,
                invocation.id,
                provider_usage=usage,
                pricing_snapshot=pricing,
            )

            assert [row.id for row in first] == [row.id for row in second]
            by_role = {row.media_role: row for row in rows}
            assert by_role["target"].image_tokens == 120
            assert by_role["anchor"].image_tokens == 40
            assert all(row.usage_source == "provider_actual" for row in rows)
            assert all(row.actual_cost_micros is None for row in rows)
            assert by_role["target"].estimated_cost_micros == 240
            assert by_role["anchor"].estimated_cost_micros == 80
            assert all(row.provider_correlation_id == "req-media-1" for row in rows)
            assert all(row.settled_at is not None for row in rows)
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_tiered_media_breakdown_uses_the_whole_request_input_tier(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'tiered-media.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            invocation = _invocation()
            db.add(invocation)
            db.flush()
            rows = record_prepared_media(
                db, invocation.id, _bundle(tmp_path, explicit=True)
            )

            legacy_pricing = doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing()
            legacy_pricing["schema_version"] = "model-pricing-v2"
            for tier in legacy_pricing["tiers"]:
                tier.pop("cached_input_micros_per_1k")

            settle_media_usage(
                db,
                invocation.id,
                provider_usage={
                    "tokens_by_identity": {"target:0": 120, "anchor:0": 40},
                    "total_image_tokens": 160,
                },
                pricing_snapshot={
                    "version": "model-pricing-v2",
                    "pricing_contract": legacy_pricing,
                    "request_input_tokens": 32_001,
                },
            )

            by_role = {row.media_role: row for row in rows}
            assert by_role["target"].estimated_cost_micros == 108
            assert by_role["anchor"].estimated_cost_micros == 36
            evidence = json.loads(rows[0].evidence_json)["settlement"]
            assert evidence["pricing_tier_key"] == "32000-128000"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

def test_provider_aggregate_usage_is_reconciled_as_estimate_not_fake_actual(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'estimate.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            invocation = _invocation()
            db.add(invocation)
            db.flush()
            rows = record_prepared_media(
                db, invocation.id, _bundle(tmp_path, explicit=True)
            )

            settled = settle_media_usage(
                db,
                invocation.id,
                provider_usage={
                    "total_image_tokens": 101,
                    "request_correlation_id": "req-media-aggregate",
                },
                pricing_snapshot={
                    "version": "model-config-pricing-v1",
                    "input_micros_per_million_tokens": 1_000_000,
                },
            )

            # 目标图 detail:high 固定 1280；锚点自 2026-09-02 起默认 pixel_limit，
            # 按实际像素估算（1200x900 压到 ≤1024² 后为 595），两者用的不是同一套公式。
            assert [row.image_tokens for row in settled] == [1280, 595]
            assert all(row.usage_source == "official_estimate" for row in settled)
            evidence = [json.loads(row.evidence_json)["settlement"] for row in rows]
            assert all(
                item["estimator_version"] == "seed-2.0-v1"
                for item in evidence
            )
            assert all(item["provider_total_image_tokens"] == 101 for item in evidence)
            # 1 micro/token（定价 1_000_000 micros/百万 token）：1280 + 595
            assert sum(row.estimated_cost_micros for row in rows) == 1875
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_failed_provider_call_keeps_prepared_rows_without_fabricated_usage(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'failed.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            invocation = _invocation()
            db.add(invocation)
            db.flush()
            record_prepared_media(db, invocation.id, _bundle(tmp_path))
            invocation.status = "failed"
            invocation.error_code = "model_provider_timeout"
            db.flush()

            rows = db.scalars(
                select(WorkflowModelMediaUsage).where(
                    WorkflowModelMediaUsage.invocation_id == invocation.id
                )
            ).all()
            assert len(rows) == 2
            assert all(row.settled_at is None for row in rows)
            assert all(row.usage_source == "unavailable" for row in rows)
            assert all(row.image_tokens == 0 for row in rows)
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_media_settlement_rejects_changed_replay(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'conflict.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            invocation = _invocation()
            db.add(invocation)
            db.flush()
            record_prepared_media(db, invocation.id, _bundle(tmp_path))
            pricing = {
                "version": "model-config-pricing-v1",
                "input_micros_per_million_tokens": 1_000_000,
            }
            settle_media_usage(
                db,
                invocation.id,
                provider_usage={
                    "tokens_by_identity": {"target:0": 10, "anchor:0": 20},
                    "total_image_tokens": 30,
                },
                pricing_snapshot=pricing,
            )
            with pytest.raises(ModelMediaUsageError) as error:
                settle_media_usage(
                    db,
                    invocation.id,
                    provider_usage={
                        "tokens_by_identity": {"target:0": 11, "anchor:0": 20},
                        "total_image_tokens": 31,
                    },
                    pricing_snapshot=pricing,
                )
            assert error.value.code == "media_usage_idempotency_conflict"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def _prompt() -> dict[str, object]:
    return {
        "node_instance_id": "prompt_a",
        "kind": "prompt",
        "config": {
            "max_tokens": 1_000,
            "model_snapshot": {
                "source_model_config_id": 1,
                "provider": "doubao",
                "model_id": "cost-observation-model",
            },
        },
    }


def _priced_model() -> SimpleNamespace:
    return SimpleNamespace(
        id=1,
        max_input_tokens=10_000,
        max_tokens=1_000,
        input_micros_per_million_tokens=100_000_000,
        output_micros_per_million_tokens=100_000_000,
        benchmark_enabled=True,
    )


def test_money_ceiling_blocks_planning_and_token_ceiling_still_blocks(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    """Catches a paid run being created after the approved CNY ceiling."""

    import app.workflow_model_budget as budget_module

    manifest = {"nodes": [_prompt()], "edges": []}
    # 全局天花板 2026-09-16 起默认关闭；这里显式打开，因为本测试要证的就是它。
    monkeypatch.setattr(budget_module, "REAL_EXECUTION_CEILINGS_ENFORCED", True)
    monkeypatch.setattr(budget_module, "MAX_REAL_COST_MICROS", 1)
    with pytest.raises(budget_module.WorkflowModelBudgetError) as cost_error:
        budget_module.real_execution_budget_for_selection(
            manifest,
            item_count=1,
            model_configs={1: _priced_model()},
        )
    assert cost_error.value.code == "model_budget_cost_exhausted"

    monkeypatch.setattr(budget_module, "MAX_REAL_INPUT_TOKENS", 1)
    with pytest.raises(budget_module.WorkflowModelBudgetError) as error:
        budget_module.real_execution_budget_for_selection(
            manifest,
            item_count=1,
            model_configs={1: _priced_model()},
        )
    assert error.value.code == "model_budget_input_exhausted"


def test_authorized_real_call_and_cost_caps_cannot_be_raised_by_environment() -> None:
    """Catches an operator environment override expanding paid authorization."""

    environment = dict(os.environ)
    environment.update(
        {
            "PYTHONPATH": "backend",
            "MAX_REAL_REQUESTS": "20000",
            "MAX_REAL_COST_MICROS": "2000000000",
        }
    )
    completed = subprocess.run(
        [
            sys.executable,
            "-c",
            (
                "from app.workflow_model_budget import "
                "MAX_REAL_REQUESTS,MAX_REAL_COST_MICROS;"
                "print(f'{MAX_REAL_REQUESTS},{MAX_REAL_COST_MICROS}')"
            ),
        ],
        cwd=str(__import__("pathlib").Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]),
        env=environment,
        check=True,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
    )

    assert completed.stdout.strip() == "10000,1000000000"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_model_media_usage.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from PIL import Image
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

import app.model_media_usage as model_media_usage
from app.database import Base
from app.model_media_usage import (
    ModelMediaItem,
    ModelMediaPolicy,
    ModelMediaPreparationError,
    prepare_model_media,
    record_prepared_media,
)
from app.models import WorkflowModelInvocation
from app.vision_input_policy import (
    DetailFidelity,
    OriginalPreprocess,
    ShortEdgePreprocess,
    VisionRolePolicy,
    seed_20_capability,
)


def test_target_cache_lookup_does_not_scan_unrelated_files(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    target = _image(tmp_path / "target.png", size=(64, 48), color=(1, 2, 3))
    cache_root = tmp_path / "cache"
    cache_dir = cache_root / "evaluation"
    cache_dir.mkdir(parents=True)
    unrelated = cache_dir / "unrelated-cache-entry.png"
    unrelated.write_bytes(b"must-not-be-decoded")
    original_image_facts = model_media_usage._image_facts

    def bounded_image_facts(path: Path):
        if path == unrelated:
            raise AssertionError("target cache lookup scanned an unrelated file")
        return original_image_facts(path)

    monkeypatch.setattr(model_media_usage, "_image_facts", bounded_image_facts)

    prepared = prepare_model_media(
        (_target(target),),
        cache_root=cache_root,
        policy=ModelMediaPolicy(),
    ).target

    assert prepared.cache_outcome == "not_applicable"


def test_corrupt_target_cache_is_rebuilt_without_scanning_directory(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    target = _image(
        tmp_path / "oversized.png",
        size=(4100, 16),
        color=(10, 20, 30),
    )
    items = (_target(target),)
    cache_root = tmp_path / "cache"

    first = prepare_model_media(
        items,
        cache_root=cache_root,
        policy=ModelMediaPolicy(),
    ).target
    assert first.cache_outcome == "miss"
    first.sent_path.write_bytes(b"corrupt-target-cache")

    rebuilt = prepare_model_media(
        items,
        cache_root=cache_root,
        policy=ModelMediaPolicy(),
    ).target

    assert rebuilt.sent_path == first.sent_path
    assert rebuilt.cache_outcome == "miss"
    with Image.open(rebuilt.sent_path) as image:
        image.verify()
        assert image.format == "JPEG"


def _image(
    path: Path,
    *,
    size: tuple[int, int],
    color: tuple[int, int, int],
    image_format: str = "PNG",
    exif_orientation: int | None = None,
) -> Path:
    image = Image.new("RGB", size, color)
    if exif_orientation is None:
        image.save(path, format=image_format)
    else:
        exif = image.getexif()
        exif[274] = exif_orientation
        image.save(path, format=image_format, exif=exif)
    return path


def _sha(path: Path) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def _target(path: Path, *, asset_id: int = 1) -> ModelMediaItem:
    return ModelMediaItem(
        role="target",
        ordinal=0,
        source_path=path,
        mime_type="image/png",
        content_sha256=_sha(path),
        asset_id=asset_id,
    )


def _anchor(
    path: Path,
    *,
    ordinal: int,
    asset_id: int,
    level: str = "L3",
    mime_type: str = "image/png",
) -> ModelMediaItem:
    return ModelMediaItem(
        role="anchor",
        ordinal=ordinal,
        source_path=path,
        mime_type=mime_type,
        content_sha256=_sha(path),
        asset_id=asset_id,
        anchor_level=level,
    )


def _explicit_policy(
    *,
    target: VisionRolePolicy | None = None,
    anchor: VisionRolePolicy | None = None,
) -> ModelMediaPolicy:
    high_original = VisionRolePolicy(
        fidelity=DetailFidelity("high"),
        preprocess=OriginalPreprocess(),
    )
    return ModelMediaPolicy(
        version="vision-input-policy-v1",
        target=target or high_original,
        anchor=anchor or high_original,
    )


def test_explicit_high_original_keeps_target_and_anchor_geometry(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    target = _image(
        tmp_path / "target-original.png",
        size=(1600, 1000),
        color=(10, 20, 30),
    )
    anchor = _image(
        tmp_path / "anchor-original.png",
        size=(1600, 1000),
        color=(50, 60, 70),
    )
    capability = seed_20_capability()

    bundle = prepare_model_media(
        (_target(target), _anchor(anchor, ordinal=0, asset_id=2)),
        cache_root=tmp_path / "cache-explicit-original",
        policy=_explicit_policy(),
        capability=capability,
    )

    assert [
        (item.role, item.sent_width, item.sent_height) for item in bundle.items
    ] == [("target", 1600, 1000), ("anchor", 1600, 1000)]
    assert all(item.fidelity_mode == "detail" for item in bundle.items)
    assert all(item.fidelity_value == "high" for item in bundle.items)
    assert all(item.preprocess_mode == "original" for item in bundle.items)
    assert all(item.image_tokens == 1280 for item in bundle.items)
    assert all(item.size_gate_result == "pass" for item in bundle.items)
    assert all(item.capability_profile_key == capability.profile_key for item in bundle.items)
    assert len({item.policy_hash for item in bundle.items}) == 1
    assert all(item.normalized_pixel_hash for item in bundle.items)


def test_explicit_role_policies_keep_target_original_and_shrink_only_anchor(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    target = _image(
        tmp_path / "target-role.png", size=(1600, 1000), color=(10, 20, 30)
    )
    anchor = _image(
        tmp_path / "anchor-role.png", size=(1600, 1000), color=(50, 60, 70)
    )
    capability = seed_20_capability()
    policy = _explicit_policy(
        anchor=VisionRolePolicy(
            fidelity=DetailFidelity("low"),
            preprocess=ShortEdgePreprocess(600),
        )
    )

    bundle = prepare_model_media(
        (_target(target), _anchor(anchor, ordinal=0, asset_id=2)),
        cache_root=tmp_path / "cache-role-policy",
        policy=policy,
        capability=capability,
    )

    assert (bundle.target.sent_width, bundle.target.sent_height) == (1600, 1000)
    assert (bundle.anchors[0].sent_width, bundle.anchors[0].sent_height) == (960, 600)
