    assert bundle.target.fidelity_value == "high"
    assert bundle.anchors[0].fidelity_value == "low"
    assert bundle.anchors[0].preprocess_mode == "short_edge"
    assert bundle.anchors[0].preprocess_value == "600"
    assert bundle.anchors[0].image_tokens < bundle.target.image_tokens


def test_missing_policy_preserves_frozen_legacy_600_behavior(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    target = _image(
        tmp_path / "target-legacy.png", size=(1600, 1000), color=(10, 20, 30)
    )
    anchor = _image(
        tmp_path / "anchor-legacy.png", size=(1600, 1000), color=(50, 60, 70)
    )

    bundle = prepare_model_media(
        (_target(target), _anchor(anchor, ordinal=0, asset_id=2)),
        cache_root=tmp_path / "cache-legacy",
        policy=ModelMediaPolicy.legacy(),
    )

    assert min(bundle.anchors[0].sent_width, bundle.anchors[0].sent_height) == 600
    assert bundle.anchors[0].size_gate_result == "legacy_default"
    assert bundle.anchors[0].fidelity_mode == "legacy_default"
    assert bundle.anchors[0].policy_hash is None


def test_explicit_policy_evidence_is_redacted_and_persisted_queryably(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    target = _image(
        tmp_path / "persist-target.png", size=(640, 480), color=(10, 20, 30)
    )
    capability = seed_20_capability()
    bundle = prepare_model_media(
        (_target(target),),
        cache_root=tmp_path / "persist-cache",
        policy=_explicit_policy(),
        capability=capability,
    )
    evidence = json.dumps(bundle.target.as_evidence(), ensure_ascii=False)
    assert str(tmp_path) not in evidence
    assert "source_path" not in evidence
    assert "sent_path" not in evidence

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'media.db'}")
    try:
        Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
        with Session(engine) as db:
            invocation = WorkflowModelInvocation(
                production_run_id=1,
                step_attempt_id=1,
                node_instance_id="prompt_test",
                status="reserved",
                provider="doubao",
                model_id="doubao-seed-2-0-lite-260215",
                reserved_input_tokens=10_000,
                reserved_output_tokens=1_000,
                estimated_cost_micros=0,
                evidence_json="{}",
            )
            db.add(invocation)
            db.flush()
            row = record_prepared_media(db, invocation.id, bundle)[0]

            assert row.fidelity_mode == "detail"
            assert row.fidelity_value == "high"
            assert row.preprocess_mode == "original"
            assert row.policy_hash == bundle.target.policy_hash
            assert row.capability_profile_key == capability.profile_key
            assert row.normalized_pixel_hash == bundle.target.normalized_pixel_hash
            assert row.size_gate_result == "pass"
            assert row.estimation_formula_version == capability.token_formula
            assert row.image_tokens == 1280
            assert row.usage_source == "official_estimate"
            assert json.loads(row.evidence_json) == bundle.target.as_evidence()
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_foreach_media_evidence_carries_parent_attempt_and_item_identity(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    target = _image(
        tmp_path / "foreach-target.png",
        size=(640, 480),
        color=(10, 20, 30),
    )
    capability = seed_20_capability()
    bundle = prepare_model_media(
        (_target(target),),
        cache_root=tmp_path / "foreach-cache",
        policy=_explicit_policy(),
        capability=capability,
    )
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'foreach-media.db'}")
    try:
        Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
        with Session(engine) as db:
            invocation = WorkflowModelInvocation(
                production_run_id=1,
                step_attempt_id=7,
                invocation_key="item:image-a:attempt:1",
                node_instance_id="foreach_images",
                status="reserved",
                provider="doubao",
                model_id="seed",
                evidence_json="{}",
            )
            db.add(invocation)
            db.flush()
            row = record_prepared_media(
                db,
                invocation.id,
                bundle,
                invocation_context={
                    "parent_step_attempt_id": 7,
                    "invocation_key": "item:image-a:attempt:1",
                    "foreach_item_key": "image-a",
                },
            )[0]

            assert json.loads(row.evidence_json)["workflow_invocation"] == {
                "parent_step_attempt_id": 7,
                "invocation_key": "item:image-a:attempt:1",
                "foreach_item_key": "image-a",
            }
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_prepare_bundle_keeps_target_and_each_anchor_independent_and_redacted(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    target = _image(
        tmp_path / "target.png", size=(640, 480), color=(10, 20, 30)
    )
    anchor_l2 = _image(
        tmp_path / "anchor-l2.png", size=(1600, 1000), color=(50, 60, 70)
    )
    anchor_l4 = _image(
        tmp_path / "anchor-l4.png", size=(1200, 900), color=(80, 90, 100)
    )
    source_bytes = {
        path: path.read_bytes() for path in (target, anchor_l2, anchor_l4)
    }

    bundle = prepare_model_media(
        (
            _target(target),
            _anchor(anchor_l2, ordinal=0, asset_id=20, level="L2"),
            _anchor(anchor_l4, ordinal=1, asset_id=40, level="L4"),
        ),
        cache_root=tmp_path / "external-cache",
        policy=ModelMediaPolicy(),
    )

    assert bundle.target.role == "target"
    assert bundle.target.cache_outcome == "not_applicable"
    assert [(item.ordinal, item.anchor_level) for item in bundle.anchors] == [
        (0, "L2"),
        (1, "L4"),
    ]
    assert all(item.cache_outcome in {"miss", "hit"} for item in bundle.anchors)
    assert all(item.sent_format == "JPEG" for item in bundle.anchors)
    assert all(min(item.sent_width, item.sent_height) == 600 for item in bundle.anchors)
    assert [
        (item.sent_width, item.sent_height) for item in bundle.anchors
    ] == [(960, 600), (800, 600)]
    assert all(len(item.content_hash) == 64 for item in bundle.items)
    assert all(len(item.sent_hash) == 64 for item in bundle.items)
    for item in bundle.items:
        rendered = json.dumps(item.as_evidence(), ensure_ascii=False)
        assert str(tmp_path) not in rendered
        assert "source_path" not in rendered
        assert "sent_path" not in rendered
    assert all(path.read_bytes() == original for path, original in source_bytes.items())


def test_identical_anchor_content_reuses_one_artifact_and_changes_invalidate_key(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    target = _image(tmp_path / "target.png", size=(64, 48), color=(1, 2, 3))
    first = _image(tmp_path / "first.png", size=(1400, 900), color=(4, 5, 6))
    second = tmp_path / "second.png"
    second.write_bytes(first.read_bytes())
    cache_root = tmp_path / "cache"

    bundle = prepare_model_media(
        (
            _target(target),
            _anchor(first, ordinal=0, asset_id=2),
            _anchor(second, ordinal=1, asset_id=3),
        ),
        cache_root=cache_root,
        policy=ModelMediaPolicy(),
    )

    assert bundle.anchors[0].cache_outcome == "miss"
    assert bundle.anchors[1].cache_outcome == "hit"
    assert bundle.anchors[0].sent_path == bundle.anchors[1].sent_path
    assert (
        bundle.anchors[0].compression_cache_key
        == bundle.anchors[1].compression_cache_key
    )
    assert len(list((cache_root / "anchor-reference").glob("*.jpg"))) == 1

    _image(second, size=(1400, 900), color=(9, 8, 7))
    changed = prepare_model_media(
        (
            _target(target),
            _anchor(second, ordinal=0, asset_id=3),
        ),
        cache_root=cache_root,
        policy=ModelMediaPolicy(),
    ).anchors[0]
    assert changed.cache_outcome == "miss"
    assert changed.compression_cache_key != bundle.anchors[0].compression_cache_key
    assert changed.sent_path != bundle.anchors[0].sent_path


def test_corrupt_anchor_cache_is_ignored_and_atomically_rebuilt(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    target = _image(tmp_path / "target.png", size=(64, 48), color=(1, 2, 3))
    anchor = _image(tmp_path / "anchor.png", size=(1200, 900), color=(4, 5, 6))
    items = (_target(target), _anchor(anchor, ordinal=0, asset_id=2))
    cache_root = tmp_path / "cache"
    first = prepare_model_media(
        items, cache_root=cache_root, policy=ModelMediaPolicy()
    ).anchors[0]
    first.sent_path.write_bytes(b"corrupt-cache")

    rebuilt = prepare_model_media(
        items, cache_root=cache_root, policy=ModelMediaPolicy()
    ).anchors[0]

    assert rebuilt.cache_outcome == "miss"
    assert rebuilt.sent_path == first.sent_path
    with Image.open(rebuilt.sent_path) as image:
        image.verify()
        assert image.format == "JPEG"


def test_anchor_preparation_failure_fails_closed_without_exposing_error_or_path(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    target = _image(tmp_path / "target.png", size=(64, 48), color=(1, 2, 3))
    anchor = _image(tmp_path / "anchor.png", size=(1200, 900), color=(4, 5, 6))

    def fail(*args: object, **kwargs: object) -> tuple[Path, str]:
        raise OSError(f"private failure at {anchor}")

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        "app.model_media_usage.prepare_anchor_reference_image",
        fail,
    )
    with pytest.raises(ModelMediaPreparationError) as exc_info:
        prepare_model_media(
            (_target(target), _anchor(anchor, ordinal=0, asset_id=2)),
            cache_root=tmp_path / "cache",
            policy=ModelMediaPolicy(),
        )

    assert exc_info.value.code == "anchor_prepare_failed"
    rendered = str(exc_info.value)
    assert str(anchor) not in rendered
    assert "private failure" not in rendered


def test_anchor_orientation_is_normalized_without_mutating_source(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    target = _image(tmp_path / "target.png", size=(64, 48), color=(1, 2, 3))
    anchor = _image(
        tmp_path / "rotated.jpg",
        size=(100, 60),
        color=(4, 5, 6),
        image_format="JPEG",
        exif_orientation=6,
    )
    original = anchor.read_bytes()

    prepared = prepare_model_media(
        (
            _target(target),
            _anchor(
                anchor,
                ordinal=0,
                asset_id=2,
                mime_type="image/jpeg",
            ),
        ),
        cache_root=tmp_path / "cache",
        policy=ModelMediaPolicy(),
    ).anchors[0]

    assert (prepared.original_width, prepared.original_height) == (100, 60)
    assert (prepared.sent_width, prepared.sent_height) == (60, 100)
    assert prepared.sent_mime_type == "image/jpeg"
    assert anchor.read_bytes() == original

===== FILE backend/tests/test_model_media_usage_api.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app.database import Base, get_db
from app.models import User, WorkflowModelInvocation, WorkflowModelMediaUsage


def _client() -> tuple[TestClient, sessionmaker[Session]]:
    from app.model_media_usage_api import build_model_media_usage_router

    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)

    def current_user() -> User:
        return User(
            id=1,
            username="analyst",
            password_hash="x",
            display_name="分析员",
            role="analyst",
            is_admin=False,
        )

    def db_override():
        with sessions() as db:
            yield db

    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(build_model_media_usage_router(current_user))
    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = db_override
    return TestClient(app), sessions


def _seed(sessions: sessionmaker[Session]) -> tuple[int, int]:
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    with sessions() as db:
        first = WorkflowModelInvocation(
            baseline_run_id=None,
            production_run_id=101,
            step_attempt_id=201,
            node_instance_id="prompt_b",
            status="succeeded",
            provider="doubao",
            model_id="doubao-2.0-pro",
            request_correlation_id="req-safe-one",
            upstream_status_code=200,
            reserved_input_tokens=10_000,
            reserved_output_tokens=1_000,
            input_tokens=500,
            output_tokens=50,
            total_tokens=550,
            estimated_cost_micros=1_000,
            evidence_json=json.dumps(
                {
                    "authorization": "must-not-return",
                    "source_path": "/private/secret/source.jpg",
                    "request_body": "must-not-return",
                }
            ),
            created_at=now - timedelta(hours=1),
            finished_at=now,
        )
        second = WorkflowModelInvocation(
            baseline_run_id=None,
            production_run_id=102,
            step_attempt_id=202,
            node_instance_id="prompt_a",
            status="failed",
            provider="doubao",
            model_id="doubao-2.0-pro",
            reserved_input_tokens=10_000,
            reserved_output_tokens=1_000,
            estimated_cost_micros=1_000,
            evidence_json="{}",
            created_at=now - timedelta(hours=2),
            finished_at=now,
        )
        db.add_all([first, second])
        db.flush()
        rows = [
            WorkflowModelMediaUsage(
                invocation_id=first.id,
                asset_id=None,
                media_role="target",
                ordinal=0,
                anchor_level=None,
                original_width=1600,
                original_height=1200,
                original_bytes=1_000_000,
                original_format="PNG",
                sent_width=1280,
                sent_height=960,
                sent_bytes=500_000,
                sent_format="JPEG",
                content_hash="a" * 64,
                sent_hash="b" * 64,
                compression_cache_key="c" * 64,
                cache_outcome="hit",
                image_tokens=120,
                usage_source="provider_actual",
                estimated_cost_micros=240,
                actual_cost_micros=None,
                provider_correlation_id="req-safe-one",
                evidence_json=json.dumps(
                    {"sent_path": "/private/secret/sent.jpg", "image": "base64-secret"}
                ),
                created_at=now - timedelta(hours=1),
                settled_at=now,
            ),
            WorkflowModelMediaUsage(
                invocation_id=first.id,
                asset_id=None,
                media_role="anchor",
                ordinal=0,
                anchor_level="L2",
                original_width=1200,
                original_height=900,
                original_bytes=800_000,
                original_format="PNG",
                sent_width=768,
                sent_height=576,
                sent_bytes=200_000,
                sent_format="JPEG",
                content_hash="d" * 64,
                sent_hash="e" * 64,
                compression_cache_key="f" * 64,
                cache_outcome="miss",
                image_tokens=40,
                usage_source="provider_actual",
                estimated_cost_micros=80,
                actual_cost_micros=None,
                provider_correlation_id="req-safe-one",
                evidence_json="{}",
                created_at=now - timedelta(hours=1),
                settled_at=now,
            ),
            WorkflowModelMediaUsage(
                invocation_id=second.id,
                asset_id=None,
                media_role="target",
                ordinal=0,
                anchor_level=None,
                original_width=800,
                original_height=600,
                original_bytes=400_000,
                original_format="JPEG",
                sent_width=800,
                sent_height=600,
                sent_bytes=400_000,
                sent_format="JPEG",
                content_hash="1" * 64,
                sent_hash="1" * 64,
                compression_cache_key=None,
                cache_outcome="fallback_source",
                image_tokens=0,
                usage_source="unavailable",
                estimated_cost_micros=0,
                actual_cost_micros=None,
                provider_correlation_id=None,
                evidence_json="{}",
                created_at=now - timedelta(hours=2),
                settled_at=None,
            ),
        ]
        db.add_all(rows)
        db.commit()
        return first.id, second.id


def _assert_redacted(payload: object) -> None:
    rendered = json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False)
    for forbidden in (
        "/private/",
        "must-not-return",
        "source_path",
        "sent_path",
        "request_body",
        "authorization",
        "base64-secret",
    ):
        assert forbidden not in rendered


def test_invocation_media_usage_reconciles_rows_and_returns_only_safe_facts() -> None:
    client, sessions = _client()
    try:
        invocation_id, _failed_id = _seed(sessions)

        response = client.get(f"/api/model-invocations/{invocation_id}/media-usage")

        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        payload = response.json()
        assert payload["invocation"] == {
            "id": invocation_id,
            "production_run_id": 101,
            "baseline_run_id": None,
            "node_instance_id": "prompt_b",
            "status": "succeeded",
            "provider": "doubao",
            "model_id": "doubao-2.0-pro",
            "request_correlation_id": "req-safe-one",
        }
        assert [item["role_label"] for item in payload["items"]] == [
            "目标图",
            "锚点图",
        ]
        assert payload["items"][0]["sent"] == {
            "width": 1280,
            "height": 960,
            "bytes": 500_000,
            "format": "JPEG",
        }
        assert payload["items"][0]["cache_outcome_label"] == "缓存命中"
        assert payload["items"][0]["usage_source_label"] == "供应商实际"
        assert payload["totals"] == {
            "image_count": 2,
            "target_count": 1,
            "anchor_count": 1,
            "original_bytes": 1_800_000,
            "sent_bytes": 700_000,
            "saved_bytes": 1_100_000,
            "cache_hit_count": 1,
            "cache_miss_count": 1,
            "fallback_count": 0,
            "image_tokens": 160,
            "estimated_cost_micros": 320,
            "actual_cost_micros": 0,
        }
        assert payload["totals"]["image_tokens"] == sum(
            item["image_tokens"] for item in payload["items"]
        )
        _assert_redacted(payload)
    finally:
        client.close()


def test_media_usage_summary_groups_provenance_and_cache_outcomes() -> None:
    client, sessions = _client()
    try:
        _seed(sessions)

        response = client.get("/api/model-media-usage/summary?days=7")

        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        payload = response.json()
        assert payload["window_days"] == 7
        assert payload["totals"] == {
            "invocation_count": 2,
            "image_count": 3,
            "target_count": 2,
            "anchor_count": 1,
            "original_bytes": 2_200_000,
            "sent_bytes": 1_100_000,
            "saved_bytes": 1_100_000,
            "cache_hit_count": 1,
            "cache_miss_count": 1,
            "fallback_count": 1,
            "image_tokens": 160,
            "estimated_cost_micros": 320,
            "actual_cost_micros": 0,
        }
        assert payload["usage_sources"] == {
            "provider_actual": {"label": "供应商实际", "image_count": 2},
            "official_estimate": {"label": "官方估算", "image_count": 0},
            "unavailable": {"label": "供应商未返回", "image_count": 1},
        }
        _assert_redacted(payload)
    finally:
        client.close()


def test_invocation_media_usage_returns_not_found_without_leaking_database_state() -> None:
    client, _sessions = _client()
    try:
        response = client.get("/api/model-invocations/999/media-usage")
        assert response.status_code == 404
        assert response.json()["detail"] == {
            "code": "model_invocation_missing",
            "message": "模型调用不存在",
        }
    finally:
        client.close()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_model_media_usage_migration.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, inspect
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError

from app import models
from app.database import Base
from app.migrations.runner import MIGRATIONS


def test_schema_85_adds_per_image_usage_without_rewriting_invocations(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'media-usage-v85.db'}")
    try:
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.exec_driver_sql("CREATE TABLE assets (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY)")
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "CREATE TABLE workflow_model_invocations "
                "(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL)"
            )
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "INSERT INTO workflow_model_invocations(id, status) "
                "VALUES (7, 'succeeded')"
            )
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "CREATE TABLE schema_migrations "
                "(version INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, name VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL)"
            )
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "INSERT INTO schema_migrations(version, name) "
                "VALUES (84, 'add_daily_human_review_quota')"
            )

            migration = next(item for item in MIGRATIONS if item.version == 85)
            migration.up(connection)
            migration.up(connection)

            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT status FROM workflow_model_invocations WHERE id=7"
            ).scalar_one() == "succeeded"
            assert "workflow_model_media_usage" in inspect(connection).get_table_names()
            assert {
                "invocation_id",
                "asset_id",
                "media_role",
                "ordinal",
                "anchor_level",
                "original_width",
                "original_height",
                "original_bytes",
                "original_format",
                "sent_width",
                "sent_height",
                "sent_bytes",
                "sent_format",
                "content_hash",
                "sent_hash",
                "compression_cache_key",
                "cache_outcome",
                "image_tokens",
                "usage_source",
                "estimated_cost_micros",
                "actual_cost_micros",
                "provider_correlation_id",
                "evidence_json",
            } <= {
                column["name"]
                for column in inspect(connection).get_columns(
                    "workflow_model_media_usage"
                )
            }
            insert_sql = (
                "INSERT INTO workflow_model_media_usage "
                "(invocation_id, media_role, ordinal, anchor_level, "
                "content_hash, sent_hash, cache_outcome, usage_source, "
                "evidence_json) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)"
            )
            valid_values = (
                7,
                "target",
                0,
                None,
                "a" * 64,
                "b" * 64,
                "not_applicable",
                "unavailable",
                "{}",
            )
            connection.exec_driver_sql(insert_sql, valid_values)
            with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
                connection.exec_driver_sql(insert_sql, valid_values)
            with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    insert_sql,
                    (
                        7,
                        "anchor",
                        1,
                        None,
                        "c" * 64,
                        "d" * 64,
                        "miss",
                        "official_estimate",
                        "{}",
                    ),
                )
            with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    insert_sql,
                    (
                        7,
                        "anchor",
                        2,
                        "L1",
                        "not-a-sha256",
                        "e" * 64,
                        "miss",
                        "official_estimate",
                        "{}",
                    ),
                )
            with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    insert_sql,
                    (
                        7,
                        "anchor",
                        3,
                        "L1",
                        "f" * 64,
                        "0" * 64,
                        "hit",
                        "provider_actual",
                        "[]",
                    ),
                )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_media_usage_model_enforces_identity_hashes_and_nonnegative_usage() -> None:
    assert models.WorkflowModelMediaUsage.__tablename__ == "workflow_model_media_usage"
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.exec_driver_sql("PRAGMA foreign_keys=OFF")
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "INSERT INTO workflow_model_invocations "
                "(id, production_run_id, step_attempt_id, node_instance_id, status, "
                "provider, model_id, evidence_json, created_at) VALUES "
                "(7, 1, 1, 'prompt-a', 'reserved', 'doubao', 'model-1', '{}', "
                "CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)"
            )
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "INSERT INTO workflow_model_media_usage "
                "(invocation_id, media_role, ordinal, anchor_level, "
                "original_width, original_height, original_bytes, original_format, "
                "sent_width, sent_height, sent_bytes, sent_format, content_hash, "
                "sent_hash, cache_outcome, image_tokens, usage_source, "
                "estimated_cost_micros, evidence_json, created_at) VALUES "
                "(7, 'target', 0, NULL, 100, 80, 10, 'JPEG', 100, 80, 10, "
                "'JPEG', ?, ?, 'not_applicable', 0, 'unavailable', 0, '{}', "
                "CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)",
                ("a" * 64, "b" * 64),
            )
            with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "INSERT INTO workflow_model_media_usage "
                    "(invocation_id, media_role, ordinal, original_width, "
                    "original_height, original_bytes, original_format, sent_width, "
                    "sent_height, sent_bytes, sent_format, content_hash, sent_hash, "
                    "cache_outcome, image_tokens, usage_source, "
                    "estimated_cost_micros, evidence_json, created_at) VALUES "
                    "(7, 'target', 1, -1, 1, 1, 'JPEG', 1, 1, 1, 'JPEG', ?, ?, "
                    "'not_applicable', 0, 'unavailable', 0, '{}', CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)",
                    ("c" * 64, "d" * 64),
                )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_model_pricing.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from copy import deepcopy

import pytest


_LEGACY_V2_HASH = (
    "d35677072dd5e5b281e57a1f65fe490234353d035fe4e4c265b0d1063600a593"
)


def _legacy_v2_contract() -> dict:
    return {
        "schema_version": "model-pricing-v2",
        "model_key": "doubao-seed-2.0-lite",
        "currency": "CNY",
        "unit_tokens": 1_000,
        "source_url": "https://www.volcengine.com/docs/84458/1585097",
        "effective_at": "2026-06-23T17:37:12+08:00",
        "tiers": [
            {
                "tier_key": "0-32000",
                "min_input_tokens": 0,
                "max_input_tokens": 32_000,
                "input_micros_per_1k": 600,
                "output_micros_per_1k": 3_600,
            },
            {
                "tier_key": "32000-128000",
                "min_input_tokens": 32_001,
                "max_input_tokens": 128_000,
                "input_micros_per_1k": 900,
                "output_micros_per_1k": 5_400,
            },
            {
                "tier_key": "128000-256000",
                "min_input_tokens": 128_001,
                "max_input_tokens": 256_000,
                "input_micros_per_1k": 1_800,
                "output_micros_per_1k": 10_800,
            },
        ],
    }


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("input_tokens", "tier_key"),
    [
        (32_000, "0-32000"),
        (32_001, "32000-128000"),
        (128_000, "32000-128000"),
        (128_001, "128000-256000"),
        (256_000, "128000-256000"),
    ],
)
def test_lite_boundaries(input_tokens: int, tier_key: str) -> None:
    from app.model_pricing import (
        doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing,
        select_pricing_tier,
    )

    contract = doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing()

    assert select_pricing_tier(contract, input_tokens).tier_key == tier_key


def test_components_round_up_separately() -> None:
    from app.model_pricing import doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing, price_usage

    priced = price_usage(
        doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing(),
        input_tokens=1,
        output_tokens=1,
    )

    assert priced.input_cost_micros == 1
    assert priced.output_cost_micros == 4
    assert priced.total_cost_micros == 5
    assert priced.tier_key == "0-32000"


def test_cached_components_round_up_separately_and_report_savings() -> None:
    from app.model_pricing import doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing, price_usage

    priced = price_usage(
        doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing(),
        input_tokens=8_000,
        cached_input_tokens=7_000,
        output_tokens=300,
    )

    assert priced.uncached_input_tokens == 1_000
    assert priced.cached_input_tokens == 7_000
    assert priced.uncached_input_cost_micros == 600
    assert priced.cached_input_cost_micros == 840
    assert priced.output_cost_micros == 1_080
    assert priced.input_cost_micros == 1_440
    assert priced.total_cost_micros == 2_520
    assert priced.full_price_cost_micros == 5_880
    assert priced.cache_savings_micros == 3_360


def test_cached_input_tokens_cannot_exceed_total_input() -> None:
    from app.model_pricing import (
        ModelPricingError,
        doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing,
        price_usage,
    )

    with pytest.raises(ModelPricingError) as exc_info:
        price_usage(
            doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing(),
            input_tokens=999,
            cached_input_tokens=1_000,
            output_tokens=1,
        )

    assert exc_info.value.code == "model_pricing_usage_invalid"


def test_256001_is_unpriced_and_fails_closed() -> None:
    from app.model_pricing import (
        ModelPricingError,
        doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing,
        select_pricing_tier,
    )

    with pytest.raises(ModelPricingError) as exc_info:
        select_pricing_tier(doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing(), 256_001)

    assert exc_info.value.code == "model_pricing_tier_not_found"


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "mutate",
    [
        lambda contract: contract["tiers"].__setitem__(
            1, {**contract["tiers"][1], "min_input_tokens": 32_002}
        ),
        lambda contract: contract["tiers"].__setitem__(
            1, {**contract["tiers"][1], "min_input_tokens": 32_000}
        ),
        lambda contract: contract["tiers"].__setitem__(
            1, {**contract["tiers"][1], "max_input_tokens": 127_999}
        ),
    ],
)
def test_gaps_overlaps_and_non_contiguous_bounds_fail(mutate) -> None:
    from app.model_pricing import (
        ModelPricingError,
        doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing,
        parse_pricing_contract,
    )

    contract = deepcopy(doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing())
    mutate(contract)

    with pytest.raises(ModelPricingError) as exc_info:
        parse_pricing_contract(contract)

    assert exc_info.value.code == "model_pricing_tiers_invalid"


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "field",
    [
        "input_micros_per_1k",
        "cached_input_micros_per_1k",
        "output_micros_per_1k",
    ],
)
def test_decreasing_tier_rates_fail_closed(field: str) -> None:
    from app.model_pricing import (
        ModelPricingError,
        doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing,
        parse_pricing_contract,
    )

    contract = deepcopy(doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing())
    contract["tiers"][1][field] = contract["tiers"][0][field] - 1

    with pytest.raises(ModelPricingError) as exc_info:
        parse_pricing_contract(contract)

    assert exc_info.value.code == "model_pricing_tiers_invalid"
    assert "单调" in str(exc_info.value)


def test_cached_input_rate_cannot_exceed_normal_input_rate() -> None:
    from app.model_pricing import (
        ModelPricingError,
        doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing,
        parse_pricing_contract,
    )

    contract = deepcopy(doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing())
    contract["tiers"][0]["cached_input_micros_per_1k"] = 601

    with pytest.raises(ModelPricingError) as exc_info:
        parse_pricing_contract(contract)

    assert exc_info.value.code == "model_pricing_tiers_invalid"
    assert "缓存" in str(exc_info.value)


def test_official_lite_rates_and_canonical_hash_are_stable() -> None:
    from app.model_pricing import (
        doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing,
        parse_pricing_contract,
        pricing_contract_hash,
    )

    contract = doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing()
    parsed = parse_pricing_contract(contract)

    assert contract["schema_version"] == "model-pricing-v3"
    assert [
        (
            tier.input_micros_per_1k,
            tier.cached_input_micros_per_1k,
            tier.output_micros_per_1k,
        )
        for tier in parsed.tiers
    ] == [(600, 120, 3_600), (900, 180, 5_400), (1_800, 360, 10_800)]
    assert parsed.source_url == (
        "https://docs.volcengine.com/docs/82379/1544106?lang=zh"
    )
    assert pricing_contract_hash(contract) == pricing_contract_hash(
        deepcopy(contract)
    )


def test_legacy_v2_canonical_form_and_hash_are_unchanged() -> None:
    from app.model_pricing import parse_pricing_contract, pricing_contract_hash

    raw = _legacy_v2_contract()
    parsed = parse_pricing_contract(raw)

    assert parsed.to_dict() == raw
    assert all(
        "cached_input_micros_per_1k" not in tier
        for tier in parsed.to_dict()["tiers"]
    )
    assert pricing_contract_hash(parsed) == _LEGACY_V2_HASH


def test_direct_v3_contract_is_revalidated_before_cache_pricing() -> None:
    from dataclasses import replace

    from app.model_pricing import (
        ModelPricingError,
        doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing,
        parse_pricing_contract,
        price_usage,
    )

    parsed = parse_pricing_contract(doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing())
    invalid = replace(
        parsed,
        tiers=(
            replace(parsed.tiers[0], cached_input_micros_per_1k=None),
            *parsed.tiers[1:],
        ),
    )

    with pytest.raises(ModelPricingError) as exc_info:
        price_usage(
            invalid,
            input_tokens=1_000,
            cached_input_tokens=500,
            output_tokens=100,
        )

    assert exc_info.value.code == "model_pricing_tiers_invalid"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_model_pricing_migration.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, inspect
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError

from app.migrations.runner import MIGRATIONS


def _legacy_v95_engine(path: Path):
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{path}")
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "CREATE TABLE model_configs ("
            "id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, name VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL)"
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "CREATE TABLE model_registry_entries ("
            "id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, name VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL)"
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "CREATE TABLE workflow_model_invocations ("
            "id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, invocation_key VARCHAR(240) NOT NULL, "
            "estimated_cost_micros INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0)"
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "INSERT INTO model_configs(id, name) VALUES (1, 'legacy-model')"
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "INSERT INTO model_registry_entries(id, name) "
            "VALUES (2, 'legacy-registry')"
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "INSERT INTO workflow_model_invocations("
            "id, invocation_key, estimated_cost_micros) "
            "VALUES (3, 'root', 4321)"
        )
    return engine


def test_migration_96_adds_pricing_contract_and_invocation_evidence(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    engine = _legacy_v95_engine(tmp_path / "pricing-v96.db")
    try:
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            migration = next(item for item in MIGRATIONS if item.version == 96)
            migration.up(connection)
            migration.up(connection)

            inspector = inspect(connection)
            assert "pricing_contract_json" in {
                column["name"]
                for column in inspector.get_columns("model_configs")
            }
            assert "pricing_contract_json" in {
                column["name"]
                for column in inspector.get_columns("model_registry_entries")
            }
            assert {
                "pricing_snapshot_json",
                "pricing_tier_key",
                "actual_cost_micros",
            } <= {
                column["name"]
                for column in inspector.get_columns(
                    "workflow_model_invocations"
                )
            }

            # Never guess an official preset for historical flat-price rows.
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT pricing_contract_json FROM model_configs WHERE id=1"
            ).scalar_one() == "{}"
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT pricing_contract_json FROM model_registry_entries "
                "WHERE id=2"
            ).scalar_one() == "{}"
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT pricing_snapshot_json, pricing_tier_key, "
                "actual_cost_micros, estimated_cost_micros "
                "FROM workflow_model_invocations WHERE id=3"
            ).one() == ("{}", "", 0, 4321)
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_migration_96_freezes_invocation_pricing_snapshot(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    engine = _legacy_v95_engine(tmp_path / "pricing-v96-frozen.db")
    try:
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            next(item for item in MIGRATIONS if item.version == 96).up(connection)
            with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "UPDATE workflow_model_invocations SET "
                    "pricing_snapshot_json='{\"schema_version\":\"later\"}' "
                    "WHERE id=3"
                )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_migration_97_repairs_legacy_trigger_without_freezing_settlement_tier(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    engine = _legacy_v95_engine(tmp_path / "pricing-v97-trigger-repair.db")
    try:
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            next(item for item in MIGRATIONS if item.version == 96).up(connection)
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "DROP TRIGGER trg_workflow_model_invocations_identity_frozen"
            )
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "CREATE TRIGGER trg_workflow_model_invocations_identity_frozen "
                "BEFORE UPDATE OF pricing_snapshot_json, pricing_tier_key "
                "ON workflow_model_invocations BEGIN "
                "SELECT RAISE(ABORT, "
                "'Workflow model invocation identity is immutable'); END"
            )

            migration = next(item for item in MIGRATIONS if item.version == 97)
            migration.up(connection)
            migration.up(connection)

            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "UPDATE workflow_model_invocations SET "
                "pricing_tier_key='input_0_32k', actual_cost_micros=1234 "
                "WHERE id=3"
            )
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT pricing_tier_key, actual_cost_micros "
                "FROM workflow_model_invocations WHERE id=3"
            ).one() == ("input_0_32k", 1234)
            with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "UPDATE workflow_model_invocations SET "
                    "pricing_snapshot_json='{\"schema_version\":\"tampered\"}' "
                    "WHERE id=3"
                )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_model_protocols.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import json
from types import SimpleNamespace

from app.doubao import DoubaoClient, _extract_message_text
from app.models import ModelConfig
from app.model_pricing import doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing
from app.strategy_bundle import build_model_config_snapshot
from app.vision_input_policy import seed_20_capability


def _client(protocol: str) -> DoubaoClient:
    client = object.__new__(DoubaoClient)
    client.api_key = "secret-value"
    client.config = SimpleNamespace(protocol=protocol, model_id="model-x")
    return client


def test_openai_chat_protocol_shape() -> None:
    client = _client("openai_chat")
    assert client._headers()["Authorization"] == "Bearer secret-value"
    payload = client._protocol_payload("system", "user")
    assert payload["messages"] == [
        {"role": "system", "content": "system"},
        {"role": "user", "content": "user"},
    ]


def test_openai_responses_protocol_shape() -> None:
    client = _client("openai_responses")
    payload = client._protocol_payload("system", "user")
    assert payload["input"][1] == {"role": "user", "content": "user"}


def test_anthropic_protocol_shape_and_response() -> None:
    client = _client("anthropic_messages")
    assert client._headers() == {
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
        "x-api-key": "secret-value",
        "anthropic-version": "2023-06-01",
    }
    payload = client._protocol_payload("system", "user")
    assert payload["system"] == "system"
    assert payload["messages"] == [{"role": "user", "content": "user"}]
    assert _extract_message_text({"content": [{"type": "text", "text": "ok"}]}) == "ok"


def test_chat_text_accepts_natural_language_without_json_retry() -> None:
    client = _client("openai_chat")
    client.config = SimpleNamespace(
        protocol="openai_chat",
        provider="doubao",
        model_id="model-x",
        max_tokens=1000,
        temperature=0.2,
        structured_output=True,
        max_retries=3,
    )
    calls: list[dict[str, object]] = []

    async def fake_post(payload: dict[str, object]) -> dict[str, object]:
        calls.append(payload)
        return {"choices": [{"message": {"content": "这是一段自由结论。"}}]}

    client._post = fake_post  # type: ignore[method-assign]
    response = asyncio.run(client.chat_text("自由系统提示", "自由用户提示"))
    assert response.raw_text == "这是一段自由结论。"
    assert response.parsed == {}
    assert response.attempt_count == 1
    assert len(calls) == 1
    assert "response_format" not in calls[0]


def test_model_snapshot_freezes_vision_capability_profile_without_secret() -> None:
    profile = seed_20_capability(
        provider="doubao",
        model_id="doubao-seed-2-0-lite-260215",
    ).as_dict()
    config = ModelConfig(
        name="视觉模型",
        provider="doubao",
        model_id="doubao-seed-2-0-lite-260215",
        base_url="https://ark.cn-beijing.volces.com/api/v3",
        api_path="/chat/completions",
        temperature=0.1,
        max_tokens=4096,
        timeout_seconds=120,
        max_retries=1,
        max_concurrency=8,
        structured_output=True,
        high_risk_review_enabled=True,
        encrypted_api_key="must-not-leak",
        vision_capability_profile_json=json.dumps(profile),
        pricing_contract_json=json.dumps(doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing()),
    )

    snapshot = build_model_config_snapshot(config)

    assert snapshot["vision_capability_profile"] == profile
    assert snapshot["pricing_contract"] == doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing()
    assert "must-not-leak" not in json.dumps(snapshot, ensure_ascii=False)
    assert build_model_config_snapshot(SimpleNamespace(**snapshot)) == snapshot

===== FILE backend/tests/test_model_quota.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, event, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database import Base
from app.model_quota import (
    ModelQuotaUnavailable,
    acquire_model_quota,
    cool_down_model_quota,
    model_quota_snapshot,
    release_model_quota,
)
from app.models import ModelQuotaLease, ModelQuotaMinuteWindow, ModelQuotaPool
from app.migrations.mysql_baseline import mysql_metadata


NOW = datetime(2026, 8, 29, 4, 30, 15, tzinfo=timezone.utc)


def _pool(
    db: Session,
    *,
    key: str,
    concurrency: int = 1,
    rpm: int | None = None,
    tpm: int | None = None,
    ipm: int | None = None,
) -> ModelQuotaPool:
    row = ModelQuotaPool(
        pool_key=key,
        name=key,
        provider="doubao",
        scope_kind="endpoint",
        scope_identifier=f"endpoint-{key}",
        max_concurrency=concurrency,
        requests_per_minute=rpm,
        tokens_per_minute=tpm,
        images_per_minute=ipm,
        lease_seconds=30,
        enabled=True,
        created_by="test",
    )
    db.add(row)
    db.flush()
    return row


def test_shared_pool_saturates_but_independent_pool_remains_available() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///:memory:")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            first_pool = _pool(db, key="endpoint-a")
            second_pool = _pool(db, key="endpoint-b")
            db.commit()

            first = acquire_model_quota(
                db,
                quota_pool_id=first_pool.id,
                owner_key="run:1:node:a",
                reserved_tokens=100,
                reserved_images=1,
                now=NOW,
            )
            duplicate = acquire_model_quota(
                db,
                quota_pool_id=first_pool.id,
                owner_key="run:1:node:a",
                reserved_tokens=100,
                reserved_images=1,
                now=NOW,
            )
            assert duplicate.lease_token == first.lease_token
            assert duplicate.reused is True

            with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable) as exc_info:
                acquire_model_quota(
                    db,
                    quota_pool_id=first_pool.id,
                    owner_key="run:2:node:a",
                    now=NOW,
                )
            assert exc_info.value.code == "quota_pool_concurrency_exhausted"
            assert exc_info.value.retry_after_seconds <= 5

            independent = acquire_model_quota(
                db,
                quota_pool_id=second_pool.id,
                owner_key="run:3:node:a",
                now=NOW,
            )
            assert independent.pool_key == "endpoint-b"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("limit_kwargs", "first_kwargs", "second_kwargs", "expected_code"),
    [
        ({"rpm": 1}, {}, {}, "quota_pool_requests_per_minute_exhausted"),
        (
            {"tpm": 100},
            {"reserved_tokens": 60},
            {"reserved_tokens": 41},
            "quota_pool_tokens_per_minute_exhausted",
        ),
        (
            {"ipm": 1},
            {"reserved_images": 1},
            {"reserved_images": 1},
            "quota_pool_images_per_minute_exhausted",
        ),
    ],
)
def test_minute_boundaries_are_reserved_before_provider_side_effect(
    limit_kwargs: dict[str, int],
    first_kwargs: dict[str, int],
    second_kwargs: dict[str, int],
    expected_code: str,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///:memory:")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            pool = _pool(db, key=expected_code, concurrency=2, **limit_kwargs)
            first = acquire_model_quota(
                db,
                quota_pool_id=pool.id,
                owner_key="run:1:node:a",
                now=NOW,
                **first_kwargs,
            )
            release_model_quota(db, lease_token=first.lease_token, now=NOW)

            with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable) as exc_info:
                acquire_model_quota(
                    db,
                    quota_pool_id=pool.id,
                    owner_key="run:2:node:a",
                    now=NOW,
                    **second_kwargs,
                )
            assert exc_info.value.code == expected_code
            assert exc_info.value.retry_after_seconds == 45

            admitted_next_minute = acquire_model_quota(
                db,
                quota_pool_id=pool.id,
                owner_key="run:2:node:a",
                now=NOW + timedelta(seconds=45),
                **second_kwargs,
            )
            assert admitted_next_minute.reused is False
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_disabled_and_cooling_pools_fail_closed_without_creating_a_lease() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///:memory:")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            pool = _pool(db, key="cooldown")
            pool.enabled = False
            db.commit()
            with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable) as exc_info:
                acquire_model_quota(
                    db,
                    quota_pool_id=pool.id,
                    owner_key="run:1:node:a",
                    now=NOW,
                )
            assert exc_info.value.code == "quota_pool_disabled"

            pool.enabled = True
            db.commit()
            cool_down_model_quota(
                db,
                quota_pool_id=pool.id,
                retry_after_seconds=20,
                reason="provider_rate_limited",
                now=NOW,
            )
            with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable) as exc_info:
                acquire_model_quota(
                    db,
                    quota_pool_id=pool.id,
                    owner_key="run:2:node:a",
                    now=NOW,
                )
            assert exc_info.value.code == "quota_pool_cooling_down"
            assert exc_info.value.retry_after_seconds == 20
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ModelQuotaLease.id))) == 0
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_release_is_idempotent_and_stale_lease_recovers_capacity() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///:memory:")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            pool = _pool(db, key="recovery")
            first = acquire_model_quota(
                db,
                quota_pool_id=pool.id,
                owner_key="run:1:node:a",
                now=NOW,
            )
            released = release_model_quota(
                db, lease_token=first.lease_token, now=NOW
            )
            repeated = release_model_quota(
                db, lease_token=first.lease_token, now=NOW
            )
            assert released.status == repeated.status == "released"
            assert released.reused is False
            assert repeated.reused is True

            stale = acquire_model_quota(
                db,
                quota_pool_id=pool.id,
                owner_key="run:2:node:a",
                now=NOW,
            )
            recovered = acquire_model_quota(
                db,
                quota_pool_id=pool.id,
                owner_key="run:3:node:a",
                now=NOW + timedelta(seconds=31),
            )
            assert recovered.lease_token != stale.lease_token
            stale_row = db.scalar(
                select(ModelQuotaLease).where(
                    ModelQuotaLease.lease_token == stale.lease_token
                )
            )
            assert stale_row is not None and stale_row.status == "expired"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_snapshot_contains_capacity_not_credentials_or_request_material() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///:memory:")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            pool = _pool(db, key="safe-health", concurrency=2, rpm=10)
            acquire_model_quota(
                db,
                quota_pool_id=pool.id,
                owner_key="run:1:node:a",
                now=NOW,
            )
            snapshot = model_quota_snapshot(db, quota_pool_id=pool.id, now=NOW)
            assert snapshot == {
                "pool_id": pool.id,
                "pool_key": "safe-health",
                "provider": "doubao",
                "enabled": True,
                "cooling_down": False,
                "cooldown_until": None,
                "max_concurrency": 2,
                "active_concurrency": 1,
                "requests_per_minute": 10,
                "requests_reserved_this_minute": 1,
                "tokens_per_minute": None,
                "tokens_reserved_this_minute": 0,
                "images_per_minute": None,
                "images_reserved_this_minute": 0,
            }
            assert "key" not in " ".join(snapshot).replace("pool_key", "")
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ModelQuotaMinuteWindow.id))) == 1
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_sqlite_workers_cannot_oversubscribe_one_pool(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'model-quota.db'}",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False, "timeout": 30},
        pool_pre_ping=True,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        pool = _pool(db, key="concurrent", concurrency=4)
        pool_id = pool.id
        db.commit()

    def reserve(index: int) -> str | None:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            try:
                item = acquire_model_quota(
                    db,
                    quota_pool_id=pool_id,
                    owner_key=f"run:{index}:node:a",
                    now=NOW,
                )
                db.commit()
                return item.lease_token
            except ModelQuotaUnavailable as exc:
                db.rollback()
                assert exc.code == "quota_pool_concurrency_exhausted"
                return None

    try:
        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=12) as workers:
            tokens = list(workers.map(reserve, range(24)))
        assert len({item for item in tokens if item is not None}) == 4
        with Session(engine) as db:
            assert db.scalar(
                select(func.count(ModelQuotaLease.id)).where(
                    ModelQuotaLease.status == "active"
                )
            ) == 4
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_mysql_workers_cannot_oversubscribe_one_pool(mysql_engine) -> None:
    metadata = mysql_metadata()
    metadata.tables[ModelQuotaPool.__tablename__].create(mysql_engine)
    metadata.tables[ModelQuotaLease.__tablename__].create(mysql_engine)
    metadata.tables[ModelQuotaMinuteWindow.__tablename__].create(mysql_engine)
    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        pool = _pool(db, key="mysql-concurrent", concurrency=8)
        pool_id = pool.id
        db.commit()

    def reserve(index: int) -> str | None:
        with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
            try:
                item = acquire_model_quota(
                    db,
                    quota_pool_id=pool_id,
                    owner_key=f"run:{index}:node:a",
                    now=NOW,
                )
                db.commit()
                return item.lease_token
            except ModelQuotaUnavailable as exc:
                db.rollback()
                assert exc.code == "quota_pool_concurrency_exhausted"
                return None

    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=16) as workers:
        tokens = list(workers.map(reserve, range(32)))
    assert len({item for item in tokens if item is not None}) == 8


@pytest.mark.parametrize("active_count", [8, 256])
def test_admission_loads_bounded_lease_objects_at_capacity(active_count: int) -> None:
    """Admission must not hydrate all live or expired leases on every request."""
    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///:memory:")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            pool = _pool(db, key="bounded", concurrency=active_count + 1)
            pool_id = pool.id
            db.add_all([
                ModelQuotaLease(
                    pool_id=pool_id,
                    owner_key=f"seed:{index}",
                    lease_token=f"seed-token-{index}",
                    acquired_at=NOW - timedelta(seconds=30),
                    expires_at=(NOW + timedelta(seconds=2) if index < active_count else NOW),
                )
                for index in range(active_count + 128)
            ])
            db.commit()
            db.expunge_all()
            loaded: list[int] = []

            def record_load(session, instance) -> None:
                if isinstance(instance, ModelQuotaLease):
                    loaded.append(instance.id)

            event.listen(db, "loaded_as_persistent", record_load)
            admitted = acquire_model_quota(
                db, quota_pool_id=pool_id, owner_key="new:owner", now=NOW
            )
            assert admitted.reused is False
            duplicate = acquire_model_quota(
                db, quota_pool_id=pool_id, owner_key="new:owner", now=NOW
            )
            assert duplicate.lease_token == admitted.lease_token
            assert duplicate.reused is True
            with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable) as exc_info:
                acquire_model_quota(
                    db, quota_pool_id=pool_id, owner_key="overflow:owner", now=NOW
                )
            assert exc_info.value.code == "quota_pool_concurrency_exhausted"
            assert exc_info.value.retry_after_seconds == 2
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ModelQuotaLease.id)).where(
                ModelQuotaLease.status == "expired",
                ModelQuotaLease.released_at == NOW,
            )) == 128
            assert len(loaded) <= 2
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_expired_cached_owner_reacquires_and_old_token_cannot_release() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///:memory:")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            pool = _pool(db, key="cached-expiry", concurrency=2)
            first = acquire_model_quota(
                db, quota_pool_id=pool.id, owner_key="cached:owner",
                reserved_tokens=10, now=NOW,
            )
            cached = db.scalar(select(ModelQuotaLease).where(
                ModelQuotaLease.lease_token == first.lease_token
            ))
            other = acquire_model_quota(
                db, quota_pool_id=pool.id, owner_key="cached:other", now=NOW,
            )
            cached_other = db.scalar(select(ModelQuotaLease).where(
                ModelQuotaLease.lease_token == other.lease_token
            ))
            second = acquire_model_quota(
                db, quota_pool_id=pool.id, owner_key="cached:owner",
                reserved_tokens=20, now=NOW + timedelta(seconds=30),
            )
            assert second.reused is False
            assert second.lease_token != first.lease_token
            assert cached.lease_token == second.lease_token
            assert cached.status == "active"
            assert cached.released_at is None
            assert cached_other.status == "expired"
            with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable) as exc_info:
                release_model_quota(db, lease_token=first.lease_token, now=NOW)
            assert exc_info.value.code == "quota_lease_missing"
            window = db.scalar(select(ModelQuotaMinuteWindow))
            assert window.request_count == 3
            assert window.token_count == 30
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_pending_lease_extension_is_flushed_before_expiration_and_count() -> None:
    """A renewed cached lease must continue occupying its slot at old expiry."""
    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///:memory:")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            pool = _pool(db, key="pending-extension")
            first = acquire_model_quota(
                db, quota_pool_id=pool.id, owner_key="renewed:owner", now=NOW,
            )
            cached = db.scalar(select(ModelQuotaLease).where(
                ModelQuotaLease.lease_token == first.lease_token
            ))
            cached.expires_at = NOW + timedelta(seconds=60)
            with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable) as exc_info:
                acquire_model_quota(
                    db, quota_pool_id=pool.id, owner_key="next:owner",
                    now=NOW + timedelta(seconds=30),
                )
            assert exc_info.value.code == "quota_pool_concurrency_exhausted"
            assert exc_info.value.retry_after_seconds == 5
            assert cached.status == "active"
            assert cached.released_at is None
            db.expire(cached)
            assert cached.status == "active"
            assert cached.expires_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc) == NOW + timedelta(seconds=60)
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_model_quota_api.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

from sqlalchemy import select

from app.models import ModelConfig, ModelQuotaPool
from test_model_registry import _close, _isolated_client, _valid_payload


def _pool_payload(**overrides: object) -> dict[str, object]:
    payload: dict[str, object] = {
        "pool_key": "doubao-endpoint-primary",
        "name": "豆包主评测端点",
        "provider": "doubao",
        "scope_kind": "endpoint",
        "scope_identifier": "ep-primary",
        "max_concurrency": 8,
        "requests_per_minute": 120,
        "tokens_per_minute": 2_000_000,
        "images_per_minute": 240,
        "lease_seconds": 180,
        "enabled": True,
    }
    payload.update(overrides)
    return payload


def test_quota_pool_api_creates_lists_and_never_returns_credentials() -> None:
    client, db, engine = _isolated_client()
    try:
        created = client.post("/api/model-quota-pools", json=_pool_payload())
        assert created.status_code == 200, created.text
        body = created.json()
        assert body["pool_key"] == "doubao-endpoint-primary"
        assert body["active_concurrency"] == 0
        assert body["bound_model_count"] == 0
        assert body["cooling_down"] is False
        assert "api_key" not in created.text.lower()
        assert "encrypted" not in created.text.lower()

        listed = client.get("/api/model-quota-pools")
        assert listed.status_code == 200, listed.text
        assert [item["pool_key"] for item in listed.json()["items"]] == [
            "doubao-endpoint-primary"
        ]
    finally:
        _close(client, db, engine)


def test_model_registry_binds_multiple_keys_to_one_shared_pool() -> None:
    client, db, engine = _isolated_client()
    try:
        pool = client.post(
            "/api/model-quota-pools", json=_pool_payload(max_concurrency=3)
        )
        assert pool.status_code == 200, pool.text
        pool_id = pool.json()["id"]

        first = client.post(
            "/api/model-registry",
            json=_valid_payload(
                name="共享端点 Key A",
                provider="doubao",
                quota_pool_id=pool_id,
            ),
        )
        second = client.post(
            "/api/model-registry",
            json=_valid_payload(
                name="共享端点 Key B",
                provider="doubao",
                quota_pool_id=pool_id,
            ),
        )
        assert first.status_code == 200, first.text
        assert second.status_code == 200, second.text
        assert first.json()["quota_pool_id"] == pool_id
        assert second.json()["quota_pool_id"] == pool_id
        assert first.json()["quota_pool"]["max_concurrency"] == 3

        first_source = db.get(ModelConfig, first.json()["source_model_config_id"])
        second_source = db.get(ModelConfig, second.json()["source_model_config_id"])
        assert first_source is not None and first_source.quota_pool_id == pool_id
        assert second_source is not None and second_source.quota_pool_id == pool_id

        refreshed = client.get("/api/model-quota-pools").json()["items"][0]
        assert refreshed["bound_model_count"] == 2
    finally:
        _close(client, db, engine)


def test_quota_pool_binding_rejects_provider_mismatch_and_short_lease() -> None:
    client, db, engine = _isolated_client()
    try:
        openai_pool = client.post(
            "/api/model-quota-pools",
            json=_pool_payload(
                pool_key="openai-project-primary",
                provider="openai",
                scope_kind="project",
                scope_identifier="project-primary",
            ),
        )
        assert openai_pool.status_code == 200, openai_pool.text
        mismatch = client.post(
            "/api/model-registry",
            json=_valid_payload(
                provider="doubao",
                quota_pool_id=openai_pool.json()["id"],
            ),
        )
        assert mismatch.status_code == 422, mismatch.text
        assert mismatch.json()["detail"]["code"] == "quota_pool_provider_mismatch"

        short_pool = client.post(
            "/api/model-quota-pools",
            json=_pool_payload(
                pool_key="doubao-short-lease",
                scope_identifier="ep-short",
                lease_seconds=60,
            ),
        )
        assert short_pool.status_code == 200, short_pool.text
        short = client.post(
            "/api/model-registry",
            json=_valid_payload(
                provider="doubao",
                timeout_seconds=120,
                quota_pool_id=short_pool.json()["id"],
            ),
        )
        assert short.status_code == 422, short.text
        assert short.json()["detail"]["code"] == "quota_pool_lease_too_short"
    finally:
        _close(client, db, engine)


def test_quota_pool_update_preserves_scope_uniqueness_and_bound_timeouts() -> None:
    client, db, engine = _isolated_client()
    try:
        first = client.post("/api/model-quota-pools", json=_pool_payload())
        second = client.post(
            "/api/model-quota-pools",
            json=_pool_payload(
                pool_key="doubao-endpoint-secondary",
                scope_identifier="ep-secondary",
            ),
        )
        assert first.status_code == second.status_code == 200
        duplicate = client.put(
            f"/api/model-quota-pools/{second.json()['id']}",
            json=_pool_payload(pool_key="another-key", scope_identifier="ep-primary"),
        )
        assert duplicate.status_code == 409, duplicate.text
        assert duplicate.json()["detail"]["code"] == "quota_pool_scope_conflict"

        model = db.scalar(
            select(ModelConfig).where(
                ModelConfig.provider == "doubao"
            )
        )
        assert model is not None
        model.quota_pool_id = first.json()["id"]
        model.timeout_seconds = 160
        db.commit()
        unsafe = client.put(
            f"/api/model-quota-pools/{first.json()['id']}",
            json=_pool_payload(lease_seconds=170),
        )
        assert unsafe.status_code == 422, unsafe.text
        assert unsafe.json()["detail"]["code"] == "quota_pool_lease_too_short"
    finally:
        _close(client, db, engine)


def test_quota_pool_health_reports_cooldown_without_raw_provider_headers() -> None:
    client, db, engine = _isolated_client()
    try:
        created = client.post("/api/model-quota-pools", json=_pool_payload())
        pool = db.get(ModelQuotaPool, created.json()["id"])
        assert pool is not None
        pool.cooldown_until = datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(minutes=1)
        pool.cooldown_reason = "provider_rate_limited"
        db.commit()

        body = client.get("/api/model-quota-pools").json()["items"][0]
        assert body["cooling_down"] is True
        assert body["cooldown_reason"] == "provider_rate_limited"
        assert "header" not in str(body).lower()
    finally:
        _close(client, db, engine)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_model_quota_pool_migration.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace

from sqlalchemy import create_engine, inspect
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError

from app import models
from app import database as database_module
from app import migrations as migrations_module
from app.database import Base
from app.migrations.runner import MIGRATIONS


def test_quota_pool_metadata_keeps_credentials_on_models_not_pools() -> None:
    tables = Base.metadata.tables

    assert {
        "model_quota_pools",
        "model_quota_leases",
        "model_quota_minute_windows",
    } <= set(tables)
    assert "quota_pool_id" in tables["model_configs"].c
    assert "quota_pool_id" in tables["workflow_model_invocations"].c
    assert "quota_lease_token" in tables["workflow_model_invocations"].c
    assert "encrypted_api_key" not in tables["model_quota_pools"].c
    assert models.ModelQuotaPool.__tablename__ == "model_quota_pools"


def test_schema_88_adds_quota_pools_without_rewriting_existing_rows(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'quota-v88.db'}")
    try:
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "CREATE TABLE model_configs "
                "(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, name VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL)"
            )
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "INSERT INTO model_configs(id, name) VALUES (7, 'existing-model')"
            )
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "CREATE TABLE workflow_model_invocations "
                "(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL)"
            )
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "INSERT INTO workflow_model_invocations(id, status) "
                "VALUES (9, 'succeeded')"
            )

            migration = next(item for item in MIGRATIONS if item.version == 88)
            migration.up(connection)
            migration.up(connection)

            inspector = inspect(connection)
            assert {
                "model_quota_pools",
                "model_quota_leases",
                "model_quota_minute_windows",
            } <= set(inspector.get_table_names())
            assert "quota_pool_id" in {
                column["name"]
                for column in inspector.get_columns("model_configs")
            }
            assert {"quota_pool_id", "quota_lease_token"} <= {
                column["name"]
                for column in inspector.get_columns(
                    "workflow_model_invocations"
                )
            }
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT name FROM model_configs WHERE id=7"
            ).scalar_one() == "existing-model"
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT status FROM workflow_model_invocations WHERE id=9"
            ).scalar_one() == "succeeded"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_schema_88_tolerates_legacy_snapshots_without_binding_tables(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'quota-v88-partial.db'}")
    try:
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            migration = next(item for item in MIGRATIONS if item.version == 88)
            migration.up(connection)
            migration.up(connection)

            inspector = inspect(connection)
            assert {
                "model_quota_pools",
                "model_quota_leases",
                "model_quota_minute_windows",
            } <= set(inspector.get_table_names())
            assert "model_configs" not in inspector.get_table_names()
            assert "workflow_model_invocations" not in inspector.get_table_names()
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_quota_pool_scope_and_lease_owner_are_unique() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "INSERT INTO model_quota_pools "
                "(id, pool_key, name, provider, scope_kind, scope_identifier, "
                "max_concurrency, requests_per_minute, tokens_per_minute, "
                "images_per_minute, lease_seconds, enabled, created_by) "
                "VALUES (1, 'doubao-main', '豆包主池', 'doubao', 'endpoint', "
                "'ep-main', 8, 600, 1000000, 600, 180, 1, 'test')"
            )
            try:
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "INSERT INTO model_quota_pools "
                    "(id, pool_key, name, provider, scope_kind, "
                    "scope_identifier, max_concurrency, lease_seconds, enabled, "
                    "created_by) VALUES "
                    "(2, 'different-key', '重复端点', 'doubao', 'endpoint', "
                    "'ep-main', 8, 180, 1, 'test')"
                )
            except IntegrityError:
                pass
            else:
                raise AssertionError("同一供应商配额范围被重复登记")

            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "INSERT INTO model_quota_leases "
                "(pool_id, lease_token, owner_key, status, reserved_tokens, "
                "reserved_images, acquired_at, expires_at) VALUES "
                "(1, 'lease-1', 'run:1:node:a', 'active', 100, 1, "
                "CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, datetime(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, '+3 minutes'))"
            )
            try:
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "INSERT INTO model_quota_leases "
                    "(pool_id, lease_token, owner_key, status, "
                    "reserved_tokens, reserved_images, acquired_at, expires_at) "
                    "VALUES (1, 'lease-2', 'run:1:node:a', 'active', 100, 1, "
                    "CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, datetime(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, '+3 minutes'))"
                )
            except IntegrityError:
                pass
            else:
                raise AssertionError("同一配额池重复抢占相同请求身份")
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_existing_mysql_schema_is_upgraded_before_version_assertion(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    events: list[str] = []

    class BeginContext:
        def __enter__(self):
            return object()

        def __exit__(self, exc_type, exc, traceback):
            return False

    fake_engine = SimpleNamespace(
        dialect=SimpleNamespace(name="mysql"),
        begin=lambda: BeginContext(),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(database_module, "engine", fake_engine)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        database_module,
        "inspect",
        lambda _connection: SimpleNamespace(
            get_table_names=lambda: ["schema_migrations", "model_configs"]
        ),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        migrations_module,
        "upgrade_mysql_schema",
        lambda _connection, *, expected: events.append(f"upgrade:{expected}"),
        raising=False,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        migrations_module,
        "assert_mysql_schema_version",
        lambda _connection, *, expected: events.append(f"assert:{expected}"),
    )

    database_module.init_database()

    latest = max(item.version for item in MIGRATIONS)
    assert events == [f"upgrade:{latest}", f"assert:{latest}"]
