from __future__ import annotations

import copy
import json
from typing import Iterator

import app.main as main_module
import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app.database import Base, get_db
from app.main import app, current_user
from app.migrations import run_migrations
from app.model_pricing import doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing
from app.models import ModelConfig, ModelRegistryEntry, OptimizerConfig, User
from app.vision_input_policy import SEED_20_PROFILE


def _isolated_client() -> tuple[TestClient, Session, object]:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        run_migrations(connection)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    user = User(is_admin=True,
        username="registry-owner",
        password_hash="unused",
        display_name="Registry Owner",
        role="admin",
    )
    db.add_all(
        [
            user,
            ModelConfig(
                name="主评测模型",
                provider="doubao",
                model_id="doubao-test",
                encrypted_api_key="keychain:v1:main",
            ),
            OptimizerConfig(
                name="调优模型",
                provider="openai",
                model_id="optimizer-test",
                encrypted_api_key="keychain:v1:tuning",
            ),
        ]
    )
    db.commit()

    def override_db() -> Iterator[Session]:
        yield db

    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = override_db
    app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: user
    return TestClient(app), db, engine


def _close(client: TestClient, db: Session, engine: object) -> None:
    client.close()
    app.dependency_overrides.clear()
    db.close()
    engine.dispose()  # type: ignore[attr-defined]


def test_model_registry_migrates_existing_configs_and_defaults_role() -> None:
    client, db, engine = _isolated_client()
    try:
        response = client.get("/api/model-registry")
        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        items = response.json()["items"]
        assert {item["role"] for item in items} == {"main", "tuning"}
        assert {item["model_id"] for item in items} == {"doubao-test", "optimizer-test"}
        assert all(item["has_api_key"] is True for item in items)
        assert all("encrypted_api_key" not in item for item in items)
    finally:
        _close(client, db, engine)


def test_model_registry_distinguishes_stored_but_unavailable_credentials() -> None:
    client, db, engine = _isolated_client()
    try:
        initial = client.get("/api/model-registry")
        assert initial.status_code == 200, initial.text
        entry = db.scalar(
            select(ModelRegistryEntry).where(ModelRegistryEntry.role == "main")
        )
        assert entry is not None
        entry.encrypted_api_key = "dpapi:v1:AA=="
        db.commit()

        response = client.get("/api/model-registry?role=main")
        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        item = response.json()["items"][0]
        assert item["has_api_key"] is True
        assert item["credential_status"] == "stored_unavailable"
        assert "dpapi:v1" not in response.text
    finally:
        _close(client, db, engine)


def test_model_registry_filters_and_rejects_unknown_protocol() -> None:
    client, db, engine = _isolated_client()
    try:
        response = client.get("/api/model-registry?role=tuning&active=true")
        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        assert [item["role"] for item in response.json()["items"]] == ["tuning"]

        payload = {
            "role": "main",
            "name": "非法协议模型",
            "provider": "example",
            "protocol": "arbitrary_headers",
            "capabilities": ["text"],
            "description": "",
            "base_url": "https://example.test/v1",
            "api_path": "/chat/completions",
            "model_id": "example-model",
            "api_key": "secret-that-must-not-leak",
            "temperature": 0.1,
            "max_tokens": 4096,
            "timeout_seconds": 120,
            "max_retries": 1,
            "max_concurrency": 2,
            "max_input_tokens": 0,
            "input_micros_per_million_tokens": 0,
            "output_micros_per_million_tokens": 0,
            "monthly_budget_micros": 0,
            "thinking_mode": "auto",
            "level": "standard",
            "active": True,
        }
        rejected = client.post("/api/model-registry", json=payload)
        assert rejected.status_code == 422, rejected.text
        assert "secret-that-must-not-leak" not in rejected.text

        missing_text = client.post(
            "/api/model-registry",
            json=_valid_payload(capabilities=["vision"]),
        )
        assert missing_text.status_code == 422

        missing_structured_capability = client.post(
            "/api/model-registry",
            json=_valid_payload(capabilities=["text"], structured_output=True),
        )
        assert missing_structured_capability.status_code == 422
    finally:
        _close(client, db, engine)


def _valid_payload(**overrides: object) -> dict[str, object]:
    profile = copy.deepcopy(dict(SEED_20_PROFILE))
    profile["profile_key"] = "test-explicit-vision-v1"
    payload: dict[str, object] = {
        "role": "main",
        "name": "新增主模型",
        "provider": "openai",
        "protocol": "openai_chat",
        "capabilities": ["text", "vision", "structured_output"],
        "description": "用于类目评测",
        "base_url": "https://example.test/v1",
        "api_path": "/chat/completions",
        "model_id": "gpt-test",
        "temperature": 0.2,
        "max_tokens": 4096,
        "timeout_seconds": 120,
        "max_retries": 1,
        "max_concurrency": 4,
        "max_requests_per_minute": 60,
        "max_input_tokens": 12000,
        "input_micros_per_million_tokens": 100,
        "output_micros_per_million_tokens": 200,
        "monthly_budget_micros": 10000,
        "thinking_mode": "auto",
        "level": "standard",
        "structured_output": True,
        "vision_capability_profile": profile,
        "active": True,
    }
    payload.update(overrides)
    if (
        "vision_capability_profile" not in overrides
        and "vision" not in payload["capabilities"]
    ):
        payload["vision_capability_profile"] = {}
    return payload


def test_model_registry_roundtrips_explicit_vision_profile_without_secret_leak() -> None:
    client, db, engine = _isolated_client()
    try:
        payload = _valid_payload(api_key="profile-secret")
        created = client.post("/api/model-registry", json=payload)
        assert created.status_code == 200, created.text
        body = created.json()
        assert body["vision_capability_profile"] == payload[
            "vision_capability_profile"
        ]
        assert "profile-secret" not in created.text

        registry = db.get(ModelRegistryEntry, body["id"])
        config = db.get(ModelConfig, body["source_model_config_id"])
        assert json.loads(registry.vision_capability_profile_json) == payload[
            "vision_capability_profile"
        ]
        assert json.loads(config.vision_capability_profile_json) == payload[
            "vision_capability_profile"
        ]
    finally:
        _close(client, db, engine)

    finally:
        _close(client, db, engine)

def test_model_registry_edit_and_disable_keeps_registry_history() -> None:
    client, db, engine = _isolated_client()
    try:
        created = client.post("/api/model-registry", json=_valid_payload())
        assert created.status_code == 200, created.text
        entry_id = created.json()["id"]

        updated = client.put(
            f"/api/model-registry/{entry_id}",
            json=_valid_payload(name="主模型 v2", level="critical", active=True),
        )
        assert updated.status_code == 200, updated.text
        assert updated.json()["name"] == "主模型 v2"
        assert updated.json()["level"] == "critical"

        disabled = client.post(f"/api/model-registry/{entry_id}/deactivate")
        assert disabled.status_code == 200, disabled.text
        assert disabled.json()["active"] is False

        listed = client.get("/api/model-registry?active=false")
        assert listed.status_code == 200
        listed_item = next(item for item in listed.json()["items"] if item["id"] == entry_id)
        assert listed_item["level"] == "critical"
        assert listed_item["monthly_budget_micros"] == 10000
        assert listed_item["max_requests_per_minute"] == 60
        assert db.get(ModelConfig, created.json()["source_model_config_id"]).active is False
    finally:
        _close(client, db, engine)

def test_registry_credentials_use_unique_registry_accounts(monkeypatch) -> None:
    client, db, engine = _isolated_client()
    accounts: list[str] = []

    def protect_for_test(secret, *, account: str):
        if secret is None:
            return None
        accounts.append(account)
        return f"test-reference:{account}"

    monkeypatch.setattr(main_module, "_protected_api_key", protect_for_test)
    try:
        main_entry = client.post(
            "/api/model-registry",
            json=_valid_payload(name="带密钥主模型", api_key="test-main-key"),
        )
        tuning_entry = client.post(
            "/api/model-registry",
            json=_valid_payload(
                role="tuning",
                name="带密钥调优模型",
                api_key="test-tuning-key",
            ),
        )
        assert main_entry.status_code == 200, main_entry.text
        assert tuning_entry.status_code == 200, tuning_entry.text
        expected = {
            f"model-registry-{main_entry.json()['id']}",
            f"model-registry-{tuning_entry.json()['id']}",
        }
        assert set(accounts) == expected
        assert len(accounts) == len(set(accounts)) == 2
    finally:
        _close(client, db, engine)


def test_registry_migration_projects_existing_legacy_rows() -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        run_migrations(connection)
    db = Session(engine)
    try:
        db.add_all(
            [
                ModelConfig(name="迁移主模型", model_id="legacy-main"),
                OptimizerConfig(name="迁移调优模型", model_id="legacy-tuning"),
            ]
        )
        db.commit()
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.exec_driver_sql("DELETE FROM model_registry_entries")
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "DELETE FROM schema_migrations WHERE version IN (61,62)"
            )
            run_migrations(connection)
        rows = db.execute(
            select(ModelRegistryEntry).where(
                ModelRegistryEntry.model_id.in_(["legacy-main", "legacy-tuning"])
            )
        ).scalars().all()
        assert {(row.role, row.model_id) for row in rows} == {
            ("main", "legacy-main"),
            ("tuning", "legacy-tuning"),
        }
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_main_role_model_can_be_verified_for_real_execution() -> None:
    """主评测模型必须能被核验真实调用，否则真实评测在结构上无法启动。

    工作流节点冻结的模型只取 role=main（workflow_manager_seed），而真实执行要求
    benchmark_enabled；原先 benchmark_enabled 被无条件同步成 role=="benchmark"，
    两个条件互斥，任何配置操作都跑不起真实模型评测。
    """

    client, db, engine = _isolated_client()
    try:
        created = client.post("/api/model-registry", json=_valid_payload())
        assert created.status_code == 200, created.text
        config_id = created.json()["source_model_config_id"]
        assert db.get(ModelRegistryEntry, created.json()["id"]).role == "main"

        verified = client.post(
            f"/api/model-configs/{config_id}/real-execution-verification",
            json={"verified": True},
        )
        assert verified.status_code == 200, verified.text
        assert verified.json()["benchmark_enabled"] is True

        # 关键：注册表再次编辑（触发同步）不得把管理员的核验打回去。
        again = client.put(
            f"/api/model-registry/{created.json()['id']}",
            json=_valid_payload(name="主模型 v2"),
        )
        assert again.status_code == 200, again.text
        db.expire_all()
        assert db.get(ModelConfig, config_id).benchmark_enabled is True

        closed = client.post(
            f"/api/model-configs/{config_id}/real-execution-verification",
            json={"verified": False},
        )
        assert closed.status_code == 200, closed.text
        assert closed.json()["benchmark_enabled"] is False
    finally:
        _close(client, db, engine)


def test_tiered_pricing_contract_can_replace_legacy_flat_prices_for_verification() -> None:
    client, db, engine = _isolated_client()
    try:
        created = client.post(
            "/api/model-registry",
            json=_valid_payload(
                provider="doubao",
                model_id="doubao-seed-2-0-lite-260428",
                pricing_contract=doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing(),
                input_micros_per_million_tokens=0,
                output_micros_per_million_tokens=0,
            ),
        )
        assert created.status_code == 200, created.text
        verified = client.post(
            "/api/model-configs/"
            f"{created.json()['source_model_config_id']}"
            "/real-execution-verification",
            json={"verified": True},
        )
        assert verified.status_code == 200, verified.text
    finally:
        _close(client, db, engine)


def test_real_execution_verification_requires_complete_pricing() -> None:
    """没有计价就没法估费用，核验必须拒绝——这是花钱前的人工闸门。"""

    client, db, engine = _isolated_client()
    try:
        created = client.post("/api/model-registry", json=_valid_payload())
        config_id = created.json()["source_model_config_id"]
        config = db.get(ModelConfig, config_id)
        config.input_micros_per_million_tokens = 0
        db.commit()

        refused = client.post(
            f"/api/model-configs/{config_id}/real-execution-verification",
            json={"verified": True},
        )
        assert refused.status_code == 422, refused.text
        assert refused.json()["detail"]["code"] == "model_pricing_incomplete"
        db.expire_all()
        assert db.get(ModelConfig, config_id).benchmark_enabled is False
    finally:
        _close(client, db, engine)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_model_snapshot_drift.py =====
"""run178 复盘：机制版本冻结的模型与模型管理里的当前配置不一致时，必须在发起前拦住。"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database import Base
from app.model_snapshot_drift import (
    model_snapshot_drift_issues,
    model_snapshot_issue_summary,
)
from app.models import ModelConfig
from test_native_baseline_regression import _baseline
from test_native_workflow_real_regression import _attach_priced_model
from test_stock_mechanism_selection import _mechanism


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def _available_test_model_secret(monkeypatch) -> None:
    import app.native_baseline_regression as native_regression

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        native_regression,
        "secret_reference_available",
        lambda reference: bool(reference),
    )


def _manifest(workflow) -> dict:
    return {"nodes": json.loads(workflow.step_manifest_json)}


def test_drift_issues_name_the_model_and_both_ids(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'drift.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            workflow = _mechanism(db)
            model = _attach_priced_model(db, workflow)

            assert model_snapshot_drift_issues(db, _manifest(workflow)) == []
            assert model_snapshot_issue_summary([]) is None

            model.model_id = "budget-model-260428"
            db.flush()
            issues = model_snapshot_drift_issues(db, _manifest(workflow))
            assert [issue["changed_fields"] for issue in issues] == [["model_id"]]
            assert issues[0]["frozen_model_id"] == "budget-model"
            assert issues[0]["current_model_id"] == "budget-model-260428"
            summary = model_snapshot_issue_summary(issues)
            assert "真实回归模型" in summary
            assert "budget-model" in summary and "budget-model-260428" in summary
            assert "模型 ID 已变" in summary

            model.model_id = "budget-model"
            model.base_url = "https://fixture-2.invalid"
            db.flush()
            issues = model_snapshot_drift_issues(db, _manifest(workflow))
            assert issues[0]["changed_fields"] == ["base_url"]
            assert "调用端点已变" in issues[0]["message"]
            # 端点值本身不进文案（可能含内网主机名）
            assert "fixture-2" not in issues[0]["message"]

            db.delete(model)
            db.flush()
            issues = model_snapshot_drift_issues(db, _manifest(workflow))
            assert issues[0]["changed_fields"] == ["deleted"]
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_preview_and_real_run_refuse_stale_model_snapshot_before_any_call(tmp_path) -> None:
    from app.native_baseline_regression import (
        NativeBaselineRegressionError,
        create_native_baseline_run,
        preview_real_execution_budget,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'drift-preflight.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            baseline = _baseline(db)
            workflow = _mechanism(db)
            model = _attach_priced_model(db, workflow)

            preview_real_execution_budget(
                db, baseline_set=baseline, workflow_version=workflow, item_count=1
            )

            model.model_id = "budget-model-260428"
            db.flush()

            with pytest.raises(NativeBaselineRegressionError) as preview_error:
                preview_real_execution_budget(
                    db, baseline_set=baseline, workflow_version=workflow, item_count=1
                )
            assert preview_error.value.code == "model_config_snapshot_drift"
            assert preview_error.value.status_code == 409
            assert "budget-model-260428" in str(preview_error.value)
            assert "重新生成版本" in str(preview_error.value)

            with pytest.raises(NativeBaselineRegressionError) as run_error:
                create_native_baseline_run(
                    db,
                    baseline_set=baseline,
                    workflow_version=workflow,
                    actor="ops",
                    idempotency_key="drift-preflight",
                    model_execution_mode="real",
                    real_execution_confirmed=True,
                )
            assert run_error.value.code == "model_config_snapshot_drift"

            # 快速模拟不调模型，不受漂移影响
            run = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="drift-simulated",
                model_execution_mode="simulated",
            )
            assert run.id is not None
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_version_list_marks_stale_model_snapshot(tmp_path) -> None:
    from app.workflow_registry_api import _version_payload

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'drift-list.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            workflow = _mechanism(db)
            model = _attach_priced_model(db, workflow)
            assert _version_payload(workflow, db=db)["model_snapshot_issue"] is None
            assert "model_snapshot_issue" not in _version_payload(workflow)

            model.model_id = "budget-model-260428"
            db.flush()
            issue = _version_payload(workflow, db=db)["model_snapshot_issue"]
            assert issue and "budget-model-260428" in issue
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_modular_services.py =====
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace
import asyncio
import importlib.util

import pytest
from fastapi import HTTPException

from app import service_entry


def test_duplicate_background_owner_is_not_reported_ready():
    from app.worker_capacity import with_worker_capacity
    result = with_worker_capacity(
        {"active_worker_count": 8, "active_evaluation_worker_count": 4,
         "active_auxiliary_worker_count": 2, "active_optimization_worker_count": 1,
         "active_projection_worker_count": 1},
        expected_worker_count=4, model_concurrency_limit=48, worker_count_source="explicit",
    )
    assert result["worker_capacity_status"] == "degraded"
    assert result["duplicate_singleton_worker_count"] == 1


def test_api_validate_startup_never_runs_maintenance(monkeypatch):
    from app import main
    calls = []
    monkeypatch.setenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_STARTUP_MODE", "validate")
    monkeypatch.setattr(service_entry, "validate_database", lambda: calls.append("validate"))
    def fail():
        raise AssertionError("API must not migrate/seed/repair")
    monkeypatch.setattr(main, "init_database", fail)
    monkeypatch.setattr(main, "SessionLocal", fail)
    async def run():
        async with main.lifespan(main.app):
            assert calls == ["validate"]
    asyncio.run(run())


def test_api_serving_does_not_depend_on_fleet_but_rejects_readonly_and_insecure(monkeypatch):
    from app import main
    db = SimpleNamespace(connection=lambda: None)
    monkeypatch.setattr(main, "_runtime_health_snapshot", lambda _: pytest.fail("fleet dependency"))
    monkeypatch.setattr(main, "database_health", lambda _: SimpleNamespace(read_only=False))
    monkeypatch.setattr(main, "_production_security_blockers", lambda _: [])
    assert main.health_serving(db)["status"] == "serving"
    monkeypatch.setattr(main, "_production_security_blockers", lambda _: [{"code": "default_admin_password"}])
    with pytest.raises(HTTPException) as exc:
        main.health_serving(db)
    assert exc.value.status_code == 503
    monkeypatch.setattr(main, "database_health", lambda _: SimpleNamespace(read_only=True))
    with pytest.raises(HTTPException) as exc:
        main.health_serving(db)
    assert exc.value.detail["code"] == "database_read_only"


def test_singleton_lock_rejects_second_owner_and_releases(tmp_path):
    with service_entry.singleton_lock(tmp_path, "auxiliary"):
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="already running"):
            with service_entry.singleton_lock(tmp_path, "auxiliary"):
                pytest.fail("duplicate owner")
    with service_entry.singleton_lock(tmp_path, "auxiliary"):
        pass


@pytest.mark.parametrize("role", ["auxiliary", "optimization", "projection", "evaluation"])
def test_background_role_never_seeds_or_runs_other_ticks(monkeypatch, tmp_path, role):
    from app import launcher, config
    captured = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(service_entry, "validate_database", lambda: None)
    monkeypatch.setattr(config, "get_settings", lambda: SimpleNamespace(data_dir=tmp_path))
    monkeypatch.setattr(launcher, "_worker_entry", lambda **kwargs: captured.append(kwargs))
    service_entry.run_background(role)
    c = captured[0]
    assert c["database_initialized"] is True
    assert c["worker_role"] == role
    assert sum(c[k] for k in ("consume_jobs", "run_auxiliary_ticks", "run_optimization_ticks", "run_shadow_ticks")) == 1


def test_static_missing_assets_are_404_and_routes_keep_spa(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    from app import main
    (tmp_path / "index.html").write_text("<html>app</html>")
    monkeypatch.setattr(main, "settings", SimpleNamespace(frontend_dist=tmp_path))
    assert main.frontend("baseline/corrections").path == tmp_path / "index.html"
    for path in ("assets/old.js", "missing.css", "../secret"):
        with pytest.raises(HTTPException) as exc:
            main.frontend(path)
        assert exc.value.status_code == 404


def test_frontend_publish_retains_old_hashes_and_rejects_mutation(tmp_path):
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("static_publish", Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "scripts/publish_frontend.py")
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    dist = tmp_path / "dist"
    (dist / "assets").mkdir(parents=True)
    (dist / "index.html").write_text('<script src="/assets/a.js"></script>')
    (dist / "assets/a.js").write_text("old")
    root = tmp_path / "site"
    module.publish(dist, root, "a" * 40)
    (dist / "assets/a.js").unlink()
    (dist / "assets/b.js").write_text("new")
    (dist / "index.html").write_text('<script src="/assets/b.js"></script>')
    module.publish(dist, root, "b" * 40)
    assert (root / "assets/a.js").read_text() == "old"
    assert (root / "current/assets/b.js").read_text() == "new"
    module.publish(root / "releases" / ("a" * 40), root, "a" * 40)
    assert (root / "current/assets/a.js").read_text() == "old"
    assert (root / "assets/b.js").read_text() == "new"
    (dist / "assets/b.js").write_text("collision")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="collision"):
        module.publish(dist, root, "c" * 40)
    assert (root / "current/assets/a.js").read_text() == "old"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_module_api.py =====
import asyncio
import os
import subprocess
import sys

import httpx
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, FastAPI

from app.module_api import InstanceActivity, owner, serves


def test_api_boundary_and_nginx_prefix_contract():
    assert owner('/api/baseline-regressions/12') == 'stock'
    assert owner('/api/baseline-regressions-extra') == 'common'
    assert owner('/api/failures/45') == 'incremental'
    assert owner('/api/users') == 'common'
    for module in ('stock', 'incremental', 'common'):
        assert serves(module, '/api/auth/me')
        assert serves(module, '/api/health/serving')
    assert not serves('stock', '/api/failures/45')
    assert not serves('common', '/api/baseline-regressions')


def test_real_factory_serves_included_routes_without_dropping_auth_or_health():
    # Fresh process: exercise HTTP routing, including FastAPI's lazy routers;
    # inspecting only top-level .path had missed all incremental routers.
    code = """
import asyncio, os, httpx
from app.module_api import create_app
app=create_app()
async def check():
    async with httpx.AsyncClient(transport=httpx.ASGITransport(app=app),base_url='http://test') as c:
        own = '/api/baseline-sets' if os.environ['LABEL_SYSTEM_API_MODULE']=='stock' else '/api/incremental-mechanisms/automation/cards'
        other = '/api/incremental-mechanisms/automation/cards' if os.environ['LABEL_SYSTEM_API_MODULE']=='stock' else '/api/baseline-sets'
        assert (await c.get(own)).status_code==401
        assert (await c.get(other)).status_code==404
        assert (await c.get('/api/auth/me')).status_code==401
        assert (await c.get('/api/health/module')).status_code==200
asyncio.run(check())
"""
    for module in ('stock', 'incremental'):
        result = subprocess.run([sys.executable, '-c', code], env={**os.environ,
            'DATABASE_URL': 'sqlite://', 'LABEL_SYSTEM_API_MODULE': module,
            'LABEL_SYSTEM_STARTUP_MODE':'validate'}, capture_output=True, text=True)
        assert result.returncode == 0, result.stderr


def test_nested_router_preserves_prefix_dependencies_and_write_boundary(tmp_path):
    async def scenario():
        calls=[]
        async def checked(): calls.append('dependency')
        child=APIRouter(dependencies=[Depends(checked)])
        @child.post('/save')
        async def save():
            calls.append('saved'); return {'saved':True}
        parent=APIRouter(prefix='/api/incremental-corrections')
        parent.include_router(child)
        app=FastAPI();app.include_router(parent)
        for module,code in [('stock',404),('common',404),('incremental',200)]:
            wrapped=InstanceActivity(app,module=module,drain_file=tmp_path/module)
            async with httpx.AsyncClient(transport=httpx.ASGITransport(app=wrapped),base_url='http://test') as c:
                assert (await c.post('/api/incremental-corrections/save')).status_code==code
            assert calls==(['dependency','saved'] if module=='incremental' else [])
    asyncio.run(scenario())


def test_api_draining_keeps_accepted_write_and_reports_active_count(tmp_path):
    async def scenario():
        started, finish = asyncio.Event(), asyncio.Event()
        committed=[]
        app=FastAPI()
        @app.post('/api/baseline-corrections')
        async def correction():
            started.set()
            await finish.wait()
            committed.append('once')
            return {'saved':True}
        drain=tmp_path/'draining'
        wrapped=InstanceActivity(app, module='stock', drain_file=drain)
        async with httpx.AsyncClient(transport=httpx.ASGITransport(app=wrapped), base_url='http://test') as client:
            write=asyncio.create_task(client.post('/api/baseline-corrections'))
            await started.wait()
            drain.touch()
            assert (await client.get('/api/health/module')).json()['active_requests']==1
            assert (await client.post('/api/baseline-corrections')).status_code==503
            finish.set()
            assert (await write).json()=={'saved':True}
            assert (await client.get('/api/health/module')).json()['active_requests']==0
            assert committed==['once']
    asyncio.run(scenario())

===== FILE backend/tests/test_module_capacity_probe.py =====
"""Acceptance judgement of the module pool capacity probe, purely locally.

No database, no network, no subprocess: these exercise the predicate that
decides whether a 16/32/1 run certifies the admission envelope, including the
cases where a merely drained run must still be refused.
"""
import importlib.util
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

path = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / 'scripts/probe-module-native-capacity.py'
spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('module_capacity_probe', path)
probe = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(probe)


def pool(target, **changes):
    row = dict(pid=1000 + target, slots=target, executor_role=probe.ASYNC_ROLE,
               processed_steps=4 * target, in_flight_peak=target, in_flight_live=0,
               succeeded_runs=target, failed_runs=0, residual_leases=0,
               worker_errors=[], timed_out=False, exit_code=0, peak_rss_mib=100.0)
    row.update(changes)
    return row


def report(**changes):
    body = dict(items=49, runs={'succeeded': 49}, global_limit=49,
                workload='baseline', multiprocess_passed=True,
                pool_shares=dict(probe.POOL_TARGETS),
                global_in_flight_peak=49, global_in_flight_live=0,
                reserved_cost_micros=28384 * 49, synthetic_cost_micros=260 * 49,
                actual_total_tokens=230 * 49, reserved_input_tokens=12000 * 49,
                reserved_output_tokens=8192 * 49, duplicate_invocations=0,
                invalid_invocation_accounting=0, unreleased_quota_leases=0,
                external_connect_attempts=0, forced_termination_pids=[],
                pools={name: pool(target) for name, target in probe.POOL_TARGETS.items()})
    body.update(changes)
    body.setdefault('succeeded_runs', (body.get('runs') or {}).get('succeeded'))
    return body


def test_a_complete_run_passes_and_still_states_what_it_does_not_cover():
    assert probe.refusals(report()) == []
    notes = probe.uncovered_roles(report())
    assert any('legacy_evaluation_worker' in note for note in notes)
    assert any('singletons are UNCOVERED' in note for note in notes)
    assert any('not a throughput certification' in note for note in notes)
    assert any('does not authorize any .85 release' in note for note in notes)
    # The production synchronous role would clear only that one note.
    covered = report(pools={**report()['pools'],
                            'compatibility': pool(1, executor_role=probe.LEGACY_ROLE)})
    assert not any('EvaluationJob path is UNCOVERED' in note
                   for note in probe.uncovered_roles(covered))


def test_a_pool_that_never_reached_its_capacity_is_a_shortfall_not_a_pass():
    for short in (0, 15, None):
        body = report(pools={**report()['pools'], 'incremental': pool(16, in_flight_peak=short)})
        reasons = probe.refusals(body)
        assert any('never reached its 16 capacity' in reason for reason in reasons)
        assert any('uncovered' in reason for reason in reasons)
    # Draining cleanly at the end never compensates for the missing peak.
    assert probe.refusals(report(pools={**report()['pools'],
        'stock': pool(32, in_flight_peak=4, in_flight_live=0)}))


def test_exceeding_a_pool_or_the_global_limit_is_refused():
    assert any('exceeded its 1 capacity' in reason for reason in probe.refusals(
        report(pools={**report()['pools'], 'compatibility': pool(1, in_flight_peak=2)})))
    assert any('exceeded the 49 limit' in reason
               for reason in probe.refusals(report(global_in_flight_peak=50)))
    assert any('no global in-flight peak' in reason
               for reason in probe.refusals(report(global_in_flight_peak=0)))
    # Falling short of the shared envelope is a shortfall, never a quiet pass.
    for short in (1, 48):
        assert any('never reached the 49 envelope' in reason
                   for reason in probe.refusals(report(global_in_flight_peak=short)))


def test_failures_timeouts_and_process_anomalies_are_refused():
    cases = [
        ({'worker_errors': ['RuntimeError: boom']}, 'worker errors'),
        ({'timed_out': True}, 'timed out'),
        ({'failed_runs': 3}, 'failed 3 runs'),
        ({'processed_steps': 0}, 'processed no step'),
        ({'exit_code': -9}, 'exited with -9'),
    ]
    for changes, expected in cases:
        body = report(pools={**report()['pools'], 'stock': pool(32, **changes)})
        assert any(expected in reason for reason in probe.refusals(body)), expected
    assert any('force-terminated' in reason
               for reason in probe.refusals(report(forced_termination_pids=[42])))
    assert any('non-loopback' in reason
               for reason in probe.refusals(report(external_connect_attempts=1)))
    assert any('48 of 49 runs succeeded' in reason
               for reason in probe.refusals(report(succeeded_runs=48)))


def test_a_missing_pool_or_a_shared_process_is_refused():
    without = {name: row for name, row in report()['pools'].items() if name != 'compatibility'}
    assert probe.refusals(report(pools=without)) == [
        'pools must report exactly compatibility, incremental, stock']
    assert probe.refusals(report(pools={'a': {}, 'b': {}, 'c': {}}))
    assert probe.refusals(report(pools=None))
    shared = {name: pool(target, pid=777) for name, target in probe.POOL_TARGETS.items()}
    assert any('own independent process' in reason
               for reason in probe.refusals(report(pools=shared)))
    assert any('own independent process' in reason for reason in probe.refusals(
        report(pools={**report()['pools'], 'stock': pool(32, pid=None)})))


def test_residual_leases_and_broken_accounting_are_refused():
    assert any('leases unsettled' in reason for reason in probe.refusals(
        report(pools={**report()['pools'], 'incremental': pool(16, residual_leases=2)})))
    assert any('did not drain' in reason for reason in probe.refusals(
        report(pools={**report()['pools'], 'incremental': pool(16, in_flight_live=1)})))
    assert any('did not drain' in reason
               for reason in probe.refusals(report(global_in_flight_live=3)))
    for broken in ({'duplicate_invocations': 1}, {'unreleased_quota_leases': 1},
                   {'invalid_invocation_accounting': 1},
                   {'synthetic_cost_micros': 28384 * 49 + 1},
                   {'actual_total_tokens': (12000 + 8192) * 49 + 1},
                   {'reserved_cost_micros': None}, {'reserved_cost_micros': -1}):
        assert not probe.cost_conserved(report(**broken)), broken
        assert any('not conserved' in reason for reason in probe.refusals(report(**broken)))


def test_the_pool_envelope_and_shares_are_fixed():
    assert probe.validate_pool_envelope(dict(probe.POOL_TARGETS), 49) == 49
    for bad, limit in [({'incremental': 16, 'stock': 32}, 48),
                       ({'incremental': 16, 'stock': 32, 'other': 1}, 49),
                       ({'incremental': 16, 'stock': 32, 'compatibility': 0}, 48),
                       ({'incremental': 16, 'stock': 32, 'compatibility': True}, 49),
                       (dict(probe.POOL_TARGETS), 48)]:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError):
            probe.validate_pool_envelope(bad, limit)
    shares = probe.pool_shares(147, probe.POOL_TARGETS)
    assert sum(shares.values()) == 147
    assert all(shares[name] >= target for name, target in probe.POOL_TARGETS.items())
    assert probe.pool_shares(49, probe.POOL_TARGETS) == dict(probe.POOL_TARGETS)
    for short in (48, 0, -1, 49.0):
        with pytest.raises(ValueError):
            probe.pool_shares(short, probe.POOL_TARGETS)


def test_the_probe_never_reads_a_real_dsn_or_production_config():
    source = path.read_text()
    assert '--database-kind' in source and 'mysql-isolated' in source
    assert 'probe-native-mysql-capacity.py' in source
    assert 'probe-native-multiprocess-capacity.py' in source
    # No DSN, host or credential of its own; everything comes from the reused base.
    assert 'mysql+pymysql://' not in source
    for forbidden in ('192.168.', 'root@', 'password', 'load_dotenv', 'docker-compose'):
        assert forbidden not in source, forbidden
    # The one environment variable it sets is the pool binding, nothing else.
    assert source.count('os.environ[') == 1 and 'LABEL_SYSTEM_EXECUTION_POOL' in source


def test_inherited_multiprocess_evidence_and_baseline_workload_are_required():
    for missing in (None, False, 'yes'):
        assert any('inherited multiprocess evidence' in reason
                   for reason in probe.refusals(report(multiprocess_passed=missing)))
    for workload in ('incremental', None):
        assert any('only the baseline workload' in reason
                   for reason in probe.refusals(report(workload=workload)))


def test_each_pool_must_settle_exactly_its_own_share():
    assert probe.refusals(report(pool_shares=None))
    assert any('shares' in reason for reason in probe.refusals(
        report(pool_shares={'incremental': 16, 'stock': 32})))
    assert any('do not add up to 49 items' in reason for reason in probe.refusals(
        report(pool_shares={'incremental': 16, 'stock': 32, 'compatibility': 2})))
    short = report(items=147, runs={'succeeded': 147},
                   pool_shares=probe.pool_shares(147, probe.POOL_TARGETS))
    short['pools'] = {name: pool(target, succeeded_runs=short['pool_shares'][name])
                      for name, target in probe.POOL_TARGETS.items()}
    assert probe.refusals(short) == []
    short['pools']['stock'] = pool(32, succeeded_runs=short['pool_shares']['stock'] - 1)
    assert any('stock succeeded' in reason for reason in probe.refusals(short))


def test_finalize_restores_child_totals_the_parent_overwrote():
    # What the parent post-processing leaves behind: its own zero counters.
    body = report(external_connect_attempts=0, loopback_connect_attempts=0,
                  runtime_connection_live=0, lease_heartbeats=0, lease_heartbeat_errors=0)
    child_totals = dict(external_connect_attempts=0, loopback_connect_attempts=812,
                        runtime_connection_live=0, lease_heartbeats=96, lease_heartbeat_errors=0)
    finished = probe.finalize(body, child_totals)
    assert finished is body
    assert finished['loopback_connect_attempts'] == 812 and finished['lease_heartbeats'] == 96
    assert finished['succeeded_runs'] == 49
    assert finished['evidence_kind'] == 'synthetic_module_pool_admission_shared_mysql_loopback'
    assert finished['module_capacity_passed'] is True and finished['pool_refusals'] == []
    assert finished['uncovered_roles']
    # A child that saw an external connection must not be masked by the parent's zero.
    leaked = probe.finalize(report(external_connect_attempts=0),
                            dict(child_totals, external_connect_attempts=1))
    assert leaked['module_capacity_passed'] is False
    assert any('non-loopback' in reason for reason in leaked['pool_refusals'])


def test_an_exercise_result_reaches_the_final_acceptance_decision():
    """The shape exercise_pools() returns, wired through the acceptance closure."""
    shares = probe.pool_shares(49, probe.POOL_TARGETS)
    result = dict(multiprocess_passed=True, provider_peak=49, provider_live=0,
                  child_exit_codes={1016: 0, 1032: 0, 1001: 0},
                  child_reports=[], forced_termination_pids=[],
                  pools={name: pool(target, succeeded_runs=shares[name])
                         for name, target in probe.POOL_TARGETS.items()},
                  pool_targets=dict(probe.POOL_TARGETS), pool_shares=shares,
                  global_limit=49, global_in_flight_peak=49, global_in_flight_live=0,
                  loopback_connect_attempts=812)
    # The base probe then adds its own sweep and parent-only counters.
    result.update(items=49, workload='baseline', runs={'succeeded': 49},
                  reserved_cost_micros=28384 * 49, synthetic_cost_micros=260 * 49,
                  actual_total_tokens=230 * 49, reserved_input_tokens=12000 * 49,
                  reserved_output_tokens=8192 * 49, duplicate_invocations=0,
                  invalid_invocation_accounting=0, unreleased_quota_leases=0,
                  external_connect_attempts=0, loopback_connect_attempts=0)
    ledger_calls = []

    def inherited(report_body):
        ledger_calls.append(report_body['succeeded_runs'])
        return True

    decided = probe.finalize(result, {'loopback_connect_attempts': 812}) and result
    passed = inherited(decided) and decided['module_capacity_passed']
    assert passed is True and ledger_calls == [49]
    assert decided['loopback_connect_attempts'] == 812
    # The inherited ledger judgement still vetoes on its own.
    assert not (False and decided['module_capacity_passed'])
    # ...and a shortfall in one pool vetoes even with a clean ledger.
    result['pools']['incremental'] = pool(16, in_flight_peak=9)
    probe.finalize(result, {})
    assert result['module_capacity_passed'] is False
    assert inherited(result) and not result['module_capacity_passed']


@pytest.fixture
def seeded_engine(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """Real rows on real SQLite; only the dialect bootstrap is swapped."""
    from sqlalchemy import create_engine
    from app.database import Base
    import test_runtime_queue_mysql as fixture
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'pools.db'}")
    monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, 'create_mysql_baseline',
                        lambda connection: Base.metadata.create_all(connection))
    fixture._seed_dispatch_items(engine, count=49)
    yield engine
    engine.dispose()


def test_seed_module_pools_configures_and_assigns_real_rows(seeded_engine):
    from sqlalchemy import text
    from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=seeded_engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    shares = probe.pool_shares(49, probe.POOL_TARGETS)
    assert probe.seed_module_pools(sessions, probe.POOL_TARGETS, 49, shares) == shares
    with sessions() as db:
        total, lowest, highest = db.execute(text(
            'SELECT SUM(capacity), MIN(global_limit), MAX(global_limit) '
            'FROM module_runtime_states')).one()
        assert (total, lowest, highest) == (49, 49, 49)
        assert dict(db.execute(text('SELECT pool, capacity FROM module_runtime_states'))
                    .fetchall()) == dict(probe.POOL_TARGETS)
        assigned = dict(db.execute(text(
            'SELECT execution_pool, COUNT(*) FROM production_runs GROUP BY execution_pool'))
            .fetchall())
        assert assigned == shares
        assert db.execute(text("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM production_runs "
                               "WHERE executor_revision <> 'runtime-v1'")).scalar_one() == 0


def test_seed_module_pools_refuses_shares_that_do_not_match_the_seeded_rows(seeded_engine):
    from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=seeded_engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    wrong = dict(probe.POOL_TARGETS, stock=31)
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match='does not match the planned pool shares'):
        probe.seed_module_pools(sessions, probe.POOL_TARGETS, 49, wrong)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_module_release.py =====
import fcntl
import hashlib
import importlib.util
import json
import os
import subprocess
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

path=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]/'scripts/module_release.py'
spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('module_release',path)
release=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec);spec.loader.exec_module(release)


def plan(tmp_path):
    from app.module_api import PREFIXES
    units={}
    for m,n in [('common',10),('incremental',12),('stock',14)]:
        for slot in ('a','b'):
            units[f'api-{m}-{slot}']={'kind':'api','module':m,'container':f'api-{m}-{slot}',
                'address':f'172.29.66.{n if slot=="a" else n+1}:8080','old_image':'sha256:'+'a'*64}
    units['executor-stock']={'kind':'executor','module':'stock','container':'executor-stock','old_image':'sha256:'+'a'*64}
    return {'contract':release.CONTRACT,'schema':release.SCHEMA,'executor_revision':'runtime-v1',
        'release_id':'reviewed-release','source_sha':'b'*40,'image':'sha256:'+'b'*64,
        'units':units,'targets':['api-incremental-a','api-incremental-b'],
        'env_file':'/etc/label/runtime.env','nginx_upstreams':str(tmp_path/'upstreams'),
        'compose_files':['/srv/source/compose.yml'],'prefixes':{k:list(v) for k,v in PREFIXES.items()},
        'control_container':'auxiliary','shared_changes':False}


def test_manifest_fails_closed_for_schema_revision_and_missing_peer(tmp_path):
    p=plan(tmp_path);release.validate(p)
    from app.migrations.runner import MIGRATIONS
    assert release.SCHEMA == max(m.version for m in MIGRATIONS)
    for key,value in [('schema',release.SCHEMA-1),('schema',release.SCHEMA+1),('executor_revision','future'),('image','latest')]:
        invalid={**p,key:value}
        with pytest.raises(ValueError):release.validate(invalid)
    p['units'].pop('api-incremental-b');p['targets']=['api-incremental-a']
    with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='peer'):release.validate(p)


def test_shared_change_requires_all_dependent_units(tmp_path):
    p=plan(tmp_path);p['shared_changes']=True
    with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='every dependent'):release.validate(p)
    p['targets']=list(p['units']);release.validate(p)


def test_proxy_removes_only_target_and_never_retries_non_idempotent(tmp_path):
    p=plan(tmp_path)
    all_config=release.render_upstreams(p['units'])
    drained=release.render_upstreams(p['units'],['api-incremental-a'])
    assert '172.29.66.12:8080' not in drained
    assert all_config.replace('    server 172.29.66.12:8080 max_fails=1 fail_timeout=2s;\n','')==drained
    locations=release.render_locations(p['prefixes'])
    assert 'label_module_stock' in locations and 'baseline-regressions' in locations
    assert 'non_idempotent' not in locations and 'http_503' not in locations
    # Common authenticated media must win over later file-extension locations.
    import re
    patterns=re.findall(r'location ~ "([^"]+)"',locations)
    assert next(i for i,p in enumerate(patterns) if re.search(p,'/api/assets/example.jpg'))==2
    assert next(i for i,p in enumerate(patterns) if re.search(p,'/api/baseline-exports/run.csv'))==1


def test_machine_metrics_is_proxied_exactly_to_common_not_spa(tmp_path):
    locations = release.render_locations(plan(tmp_path)['prefixes'])
    import re
    block = re.search(r'location = /metrics \{([^}]+)\}', locations)
    assert block is not None, 'the authenticated release metrics endpoint must not fall back to index.html'
    assert 'proxy_pass http://label_module_common;' in block.group(1)
    assert 'include /etc/nginx/snippets/label-module-proxy.conf;' in block.group(1)
    assert 'auth_request off' not in block.group(1)


def test_completed_rollback_pins_exact_prior_artifacts(tmp_path):
    p=plan(tmp_path)
    before={k:{'image':v['old_image']} for k,v in p['units'].items()}
    after={k:{'image':p['image'] if k in p['targets'] else v['old_image']} for k,v in p['units'].items()}
    r=release.rollback_plan(p,{'status':'completed','before':before,'after':after},old_source_sha='a'*40)
    assert r['image']=='sha256:'+'a'*64
    assert r['source_sha']=='a'*40
    assert r['targets']==list(reversed(p['targets']))
    assert r['units']['api-stock-a']==p['units']['api-stock-a']


def test_drain_timeout_does_not_run_stop_or_mutate_work(monkeypatch):
    clock=iter([0,0,1])
    monkeypatch.setattr(release.time,'monotonic',lambda:next(clock))
    monkeypatch.setattr(release.time,'sleep',lambda _:None)
    monkeypatch.setattr(release,'command',lambda *a,**k:pytest.fail('no process termination during pending drain'))
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError,match='no forced kill'):
        release.wait_for(lambda:False,seconds=.5,message='provider still running')


OLD_IMAGE='sha256:'+'a'*64
NEW_IMAGE='sha256:'+'b'*64
BUILD_SHA={OLD_IMAGE:'a'*40,NEW_IMAGE:'b'*40,'sha256:'+'c'*64:'c'*40}


class Scene:
    """Deterministic stand-in for the installed fleet.

    No Docker, no network, no database: every probe and control answers from
    this object, so the tests observe exactly which steps a rollout takes.
    """

    def __init__(self, plan):
        self.plan=plan;self.units=plan['units'];self.tick=0
        self.containers={key:{'Id':'id-'+key,'Image':unit['old_image'],
            'State':{'StartedAt':'2026-09-22T00:00:00Z','Running':True}}
            for key,unit in self.units.items()}
        self.by_container={unit['container']:key for key,unit in self.units.items()}
        self.by_address={unit['address']:key for key,unit in self.units.items() if unit['kind']=='api'}
        self.pools={pool:{'pool':pool,'capacity':4,'global_limit':49,'accepting':True,
            'release_id':'','leased':0} for pool in ('incremental','stock','compatibility')}
        self.workers={unit['module']:{'worker_id':'w-'+key,'pool':unit['module'],
            'build_sha':BUILD_SHA[unit['old_image']],'readiness':'ready',
            'started_at':'2026-09-22T00:00:00+00:00'}
            for key,unit in self.units.items() if unit['kind']=='executor'}
        self.draining=set();self.calls=[];self.probes=[];self.generation=0
        self.fail=set();self.interrupt=set()
        self.ingress=Path(plan['nginx_upstreams'])
        self.ingress.write_text(release.render_upstreams(self.units))

    def status(self):
        return {'configured':True,'supported_executor_revisions':['runtime-v1'],
            'workers':[dict(w) for w in self.workers.values()],
            'pools':[dict(row) for row in self.pools.values()],'unsupported':[]}

    def command(self,*args,timeout=60):
        self.calls.append(args)
        if args in self.interrupt:
            # Models the owning process dying, not a handled failure: drive()
            # catches Exception, so the receipt stays exactly as last written.
            raise KeyboardInterrupt('release process interrupted at '+repr(args))
        if args[:3]==('docker','image','inspect'):
            return json.dumps([{'Config':{'Env':['LABEL_LAB_BUILD_SHA='+BUILD_SHA[args[3]]]}}])
        if args[:2]==('docker','inspect'):
            return json.dumps([self.containers[self.by_container[args[2]]]])
        if args[:2]==('docker','run'):
            return json.dumps({'schema':release.SCHEMA,'prefixes':self.plan['prefixes'],'revisions':['runtime-v1']})
        if args[:2]==('nginx','-t'):
            return ''
        if args[:2]==('nginx','-s'):
            self.generation+=1;return ''
        if args[:2]==('docker','exec'):
            if 'Read-only probe sent by the host publisher' in args[5]:
                unit=self.units[self.by_container[args[2]]]
                self.guard(('probe',unit['role']))
                return json.dumps({'healthy':True,'role':unit['role'],
                    'build_sha':BUILD_SHA[self.containers[self.by_container[args[2]]]['Image']]})
            if args[3:5]==('python','-m'):
                return self.runtime(args)
            if 'label-api-draining' in args[5]:
                self.draining.add(args[2]);return ''
            if 'validate_database' in args[5]:
                return ''
        if args[:2]==('docker','stop'):
            key=self.by_container[args[4]];self.guard(('stop',key))
            self.containers[key]['State']['Running']=False
            # Stopping a container does not erase its writable filesystem.
            if self.units[key]['kind']=='executor':
                self.workers.pop(self.units[key]['module'],None)
            return ''
        if args[:2]==('docker','compose') and 'up' in args:
            return self.up(args)
        raise AssertionError('unexpected command '+repr(args))

    def guard(self,tag):
        if tag in self.fail:
            raise RuntimeError('injected failure at '+repr(tag))

    def runtime(self,args):
        action=args[6]
        if action!='status':
            pool=args[args.index('--pool')+1];release_id=args[args.index('--release-id')+1]
            self.guard((action,pool))
            row=self.pools[pool]
            if action=='drain':
                if row['release_id'] and row['release_id']!=release_id:
                    raise RuntimeError('module is owned by another release')
                row['accepting']=False;row['release_id']=release_id
            else:
                if row['release_id']!=release_id:
                    raise RuntimeError('resume requires the owning release_id')
                row['accepting']=True;row['release_id']=''
        return json.dumps(self.status())

    def up(self,args):
        target=args[-1];self.guard(('up',target))
        pins=json.loads(Path(args[args.index('up')-1]).read_text())
        image=pins['services'][target]['image']
        previous=self.containers.get(target)
        if '--force-recreate' not in args and previous and image==previous['Image']:
            self.containers[target]['State']['Running']=True
            return ''
        if '--force-recreate' in args and previous:
            assert not self.containers[target]['State']['Running'], 'never replace a running target'
        self.tick+=1
        stamp='2026-09-22T00:00:%02d'%self.tick
        self.containers[target]={'Id':'id-%s-%d'%(target,self.tick),'Image':image,
            'State':{'StartedAt':stamp+'Z','Running':True}}
        unit=self.units[target];self.draining.discard(unit['container'])
        if unit['kind']=='executor':
            self.workers[unit['module']]={'worker_id':'w-%s-%d'%(target,self.tick),
                'pool':unit['module'],'build_sha':BUILD_SHA[image],'readiness':'ready',
                'started_at':stamp+'+00:00'}
        return ''

    def get(self,address,path):
        self.probes.append((address,path))
        key=self.by_address[address];unit=self.units[key];state=self.containers[key]
        if not state['State']['Running']:
            raise OSError('connection refused')
        draining=unit['container'] in self.draining
        if path=='/api/health/module':
            return {'module':unit['module'],'active_requests':0,'draining':draining,
                    'build_sha':BUILD_SHA[state['Image']]}
        if path=='/api/health/serving':
            return {'status':'draining' if draining else 'serving'}
        raise AssertionError('unexpected probe '+path)


def scene(monkeypatch,plan):
    installed=Scene(plan)
    monkeypatch.setattr(release,'command',installed.command)
    monkeypatch.setattr(release,'get',installed.get)
    monkeypatch.setattr(release,'nginx_workers',lambda:{(1,installed.generation)})
    monkeypatch.setattr(release.time,'sleep',lambda _:None)
    return installed


def failed_release(monkeypatch,root,*,targets=None,fail):
    p=plan(root)
    if targets is not None:
        p['targets']=targets
    installed=scene(monkeypatch,p)
    installed.fail.add(fail)
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
        release.publish(p,root/'run1')
    installed.fail.clear()
    receipt=json.loads((root/'run1/receipt.json').read_text())
    assert receipt['status']=='needs_recovery'
    assert receipt['steps'][-1]['phase']=='stopped_safely'
    return p,installed,root/'run1/receipt.json'


def mutating(calls):
    return [args for args in calls
            if args[:2]==('docker','stop') or (args[:2]==('docker','compose') and 'up' in args)]


def test_recovery_finishes_a_release_that_failed_after_the_first_api_drained(tmp_path,monkeypatch):
    p,installed,prior=failed_release(monkeypatch,tmp_path,fail=('up','api-incremental-a'))
    # Scene retained: the target is stopped on its reviewed old image and off the proxy.
    assert installed.containers['api-incremental-a']['State']['Running'] is False
    assert installed.containers['api-incremental-a']['Image']==OLD_IMAGE
    assert '172.29.66.12:8080' not in installed.ingress.read_text()
    assert installed.containers['api-incremental-b']['Image']==OLD_IMAGE

    receipt=release.recover(p,prior,tmp_path/'run2')
    assert receipt['status']=='completed' and receipt['mode']=='recovery'
    assert receipt['release_id']==p['release_id']
    assert [receipt['observed'][t]['action'] for t in p['targets']]==['update','update']
    assert all(installed.containers[t]['Image']==NEW_IMAGE for t in p['targets'])
    assert installed.ingress.read_text()==release.render_upstreams(p['units'])
    assert receipt['recovers']=={'receipt':str(prior),'sha256':hashlib.sha256(prior.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
        'release_id':p['release_id'],'status':'needs_recovery','takeover':None,
        'stopped_at':json.loads(prior.read_text())['steps'][-1]['at'],
        'error':json.loads(prior.read_text())['steps'][-1]['error']}
    assert json.loads((tmp_path/'run2/image-pins.json').read_text())['services']['api-stock-a']['image']==OLD_IMAGE


def test_recovery_resumes_a_replaced_executor_without_restarting_it(tmp_path,monkeypatch):
    p,installed,prior=failed_release(monkeypatch,tmp_path,targets=['executor-stock'],fail=('resume','stock'))
    assert installed.containers['executor-stock']['Image']==NEW_IMAGE
    assert installed.pools['stock']['accepting'] is False
    assert installed.pools['stock']['release_id']==p['release_id']
    replaced=dict(installed.containers['executor-stock']);mark=len(installed.calls)

    receipt=release.recover(p,prior,tmp_path/'run2')
    assert receipt['observed']['executor-stock']['action']=='restore'
    assert mutating(installed.calls[mark:])==[]
    assert installed.containers['executor-stock']==replaced
    assert installed.pools['stock']['accepting'] is True
    assert installed.pools['stock']['release_id']==''
    assert receipt['status']=='completed'


def test_recovery_never_restarts_a_target_that_already_completed(tmp_path,monkeypatch):
    p,installed,prior=failed_release(monkeypatch,tmp_path,fail=('up','api-incremental-b'))
    done=dict(installed.containers['api-incremental-a']);mark=len(installed.calls)

    receipt=release.recover(p,prior,tmp_path/'run2')
    assert [receipt['observed'][t]['action'] for t in p['targets']]==['completed','update']
    assert [args for args in mutating(installed.calls[mark:]) if 'api-incremental-a' in args]==[]
    assert installed.containers['api-incremental-a']==done
    assert receipt['after']['api-incremental-a']['id']==done['Id']
    assert receipt['status']=='completed'
    assert installed.containers['api-incremental-b']['Image']==NEW_IMAGE


def test_recovery_refuses_when_an_unrelated_unit_changed(tmp_path,monkeypatch):
    p,installed,prior=failed_release(monkeypatch,tmp_path,fail=('up','api-incremental-a'))
    installed.containers['api-stock-a']['Id']='id-api-stock-a-restarted';mark=len(installed.calls)
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError,match='unrelated unit changed'):
        release.recover(p,prior,tmp_path/'run2')
    assert mutating(installed.calls[mark:])==[]
    assert not (tmp_path/'run2/receipt.json').exists()


def test_recovery_fails_closed_on_unknown_image_and_a_foreign_release_hold(tmp_path,monkeypatch):
    one=tmp_path/'one';one.mkdir()
    p,installed,prior=failed_release(monkeypatch,one,fail=('up','api-incremental-a'))
    installed.containers['api-incremental-a']['Image']='sha256:'+'c'*64;mark=len(installed.calls)
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError,match='unknown image'):
        release.recover(p,prior,one/'run2')
    assert mutating(installed.calls[mark:])==[]

    two=tmp_path/'two';two.mkdir()
    q,other,prior=failed_release(monkeypatch,two,targets=['executor-stock'],fail=('resume','stock'))
    other.pools['stock']['release_id']='someone-else';mark=len(other.calls)
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError,match='held by another release'):
        release.recover(q,prior,two/'run2')
    assert mutating(other.calls[mark:])==[]
    assert other.pools['stock']['release_id']=='someone-else'


def test_recovery_refuses_an_unreviewed_plan_receipt_or_reused_directory(tmp_path,monkeypatch):
    p,installed,prior=failed_release(monkeypatch,tmp_path,fail=('up','api-incremental-a'))
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError,match='receipt already exists'):
        release.recover(p,prior,prior.parent)
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError,match='reviewed plan'):
        release.recover({**p,'release_id':'another-release'},prior,tmp_path/'run2')
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError,match='reviewed plan'):
        release.recover({**p,'source_sha':'c'*40},prior,tmp_path/'run3')
    completed=json.loads(prior.read_text());completed['status']='completed'
    (tmp_path/'done').mkdir();(tmp_path/'done/receipt.json').write_text(json.dumps(completed))
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError,match='needs_recovery'):
        release.recover(p,tmp_path/'done/receipt.json',tmp_path/'run4')


def test_recovery_refuses_an_ingress_generation_it_did_not_review(tmp_path,monkeypatch):
    p,installed,prior=failed_release(monkeypatch,tmp_path,fail=('up','api-incremental-a'))
    installed.ingress.write_text(installed.ingress.read_text().replace('least_conn;','ip_hash;'))
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError,match='installed ingress'):
        release.recover(p,prior,tmp_path/'run2')


def test_failure_and_recovery_never_force_kill_or_replay_business(tmp_path,monkeypatch):
    p,installed,prior=failed_release(monkeypatch,tmp_path,fail=('up','api-incremental-a'))
    release.recover(p,prior,tmp_path/'run2')
    for args in installed.calls:
        assert not {'kill','rm','restart','--force','-9','SIGKILL'} & set(args)
        if args[:2]==('docker','stop'):
            assert args[2:4]==('--time','-1')
    # Every liveness probe is a GET on a health route; no business request is replayed.
    assert {path for _,path in installed.probes}<={'/api/health/serving','/api/health/module'}


def edited(p,**changes):
    return {**json.loads(json.dumps(p)),**changes}


def test_recovery_refuses_a_manifest_edited_after_the_review(tmp_path,monkeypatch):
    p,installed,prior=failed_release(monkeypatch,tmp_path,fail=('up','api-incremental-a'))
    assert json.loads(prior.read_text())['plan_sha256']==release.plan_digest(p)
    reordered=edited(p,targets=list(reversed(p['targets'])))
    repinned=edited(p);repinned['units']['api-stock-a']['old_image']='sha256:'+'c'*64
    relocated=edited(p,compose_files=['/srv/source/other-compose.yml'])
    widened=edited(p);widened['units']['executor-incremental']={'kind':'executor',
        'module':'incremental','container':'executor-incremental','old_image':OLD_IMAGE}
    for candidate in (reordered,repinned,relocated,widened):
        release.validate(candidate)
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError,match='reviewed plan'):
            release.recover(candidate,prior,tmp_path/'rejected')
    unbound=json.loads(prior.read_text());unbound.pop('plan_sha256')
    (tmp_path/'unbound').mkdir();(tmp_path/'unbound/receipt.json').write_text(json.dumps(unbound))
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError,match='reviewed plan'):
        release.recover(p,tmp_path/'unbound/receipt.json',tmp_path/'rejected2')
    assert release.recover(p,prior,tmp_path/'run2')['status']=='completed'


def test_recovery_without_replacement_still_writes_the_required_image_pins(tmp_path,monkeypatch):
    p,installed,prior=failed_release(monkeypatch,tmp_path,targets=['executor-stock'],fail=('resume','stock'))
    mark=len(installed.calls)
    receipt=release.recover(p,prior,tmp_path/'run2')
    assert receipt['observed']['executor-stock']['action']=='restore'
    assert mutating(installed.calls[mark:])==[]
    pins=json.loads((tmp_path/'run2/image-pins.json').read_text())['services']
    assert set(pins)==set(p['units'])
    assert pins['executor-stock']['image']==NEW_IMAGE and pins['api-stock-a']['image']==OLD_IMAGE

    # A run whose targets all finished but whose completion was never recorded must
    # still leave the override its receipt directory is supposed to carry.
    unclosed=json.loads((tmp_path/'run2/receipt.json').read_text());unclosed['status']='needs_recovery'
    unclosed['steps'].append({'at':0,'phase':'stopped_safely','error':'receipt never closed'})
    (tmp_path/'run3').mkdir();(tmp_path/'run3/receipt.json').write_text(json.dumps(unclosed))
    mark=len(installed.calls)
    again=release.recover(p,tmp_path/'run3/receipt.json',tmp_path/'run4')
    assert again['observed']['executor-stock']['action']=='completed' and again['status']=='completed'
    assert mutating(installed.calls[mark:])==[]
    assert json.loads((tmp_path/'run4/image-pins.json').read_text())['services']==pins


def test_recovery_chain_keeps_the_pre_release_scene_for_rollback(tmp_path,monkeypatch):
    p,installed,prior=failed_release(monkeypatch,tmp_path,fail=('up','api-incremental-b'))
    origin=json.loads(prior.read_text())['before']
    installed.fail.add(('up','api-incremental-b'))
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
        release.recover(p,prior,tmp_path/'run2')
    second=json.loads((tmp_path/'run2/receipt.json').read_text())
    assert second['status']=='needs_recovery' and second['origin_before']==origin
    # Its own starting scene already carries the candidate on the finished target.
    assert second['before']['api-incremental-a']['image']==NEW_IMAGE
    installed.fail.clear()

    third=release.recover(p,tmp_path/'run2/receipt.json',tmp_path/'run3')
    assert third['status']=='completed' and third['origin_before']==origin
    assert all(third['origin_before'][key]['image']==OLD_IMAGE for key in p['units'])
    back=release.rollback_plan(p,third,old_source_sha='a'*40)
    assert back['image']==OLD_IMAGE
    assert back['units']['api-incremental-a']['old_image']==NEW_IMAGE
    assert back['units']['api-stock-a']['old_image']==OLD_IMAGE
    # Without the carried origin the mid-chain scene is mixed and cannot be reversed.
    with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='mixed old images'):
        release.rollback_plan(p,{**third,'origin_before':third['before']},old_source_sha='a'*40)


OWNER_PID=424242


def hosted(monkeypatch,*,machine='host-a',boot='boot-1',starts=None,lock=None):
    """Deterministic stand-in for the Linux host facts this host cannot provide.

    Real /etc/machine-id, /proc/sys/kernel/random/boot_id and /proc/<pid>/stat
    are Linux-only; the gate logic is exercised here, the readers are not. The
    fixed lock path is redirected into the test's own directory.
    """
    live=dict(starts or {})
    if lock is not None:
        monkeypatch.setattr(release,'LOCK_PATH',Path(lock))
    monkeypatch.setattr(release,'host_identity',lambda:machine)
    monkeypatch.setattr(release,'boot_id',lambda:boot)
    monkeypatch.setattr(release,'process_start',
                        lambda pid:live.get(pid) or ('gone',None))
    return live


def interrupted_release(monkeypatch,root,*,at,targets=None):
    """A publish whose process dies without ever recording an outcome."""
    p=plan(root)
    if targets is not None:
        p['targets']=targets
    installed=scene(monkeypatch,p)
    installed.interrupt.add(at)
    with release.host_release_lock():
        with pytest.raises(KeyboardInterrupt):
            release.publish(p,root/'run1')
    installed.interrupt.clear()
    prior=root/'run1/receipt.json'
    receipt=json.loads(prior.read_text())
    assert receipt['status']=='running'
    return p,installed,prior,receipt


def reowned(prior,**changes):
    """Rewrite the recorded owner.

    The proof is a lock-protected on-disk record, not a signature, so a test can
    state the owner facts it needs to exercise.
    """
    receipt=json.loads(prior.read_text());receipt['owner']={**receipt['owner'],**changes}
    prior.write_text(json.dumps(receipt));return receipt


def can_lock(path):
    handle=os.open(path,os.O_RDWR|os.O_CREAT,0o600)
    try:
        fcntl.flock(handle,fcntl.LOCK_EX|fcntl.LOCK_NB);fcntl.flock(handle,fcntl.LOCK_UN);return True
    except OSError:
        return False
    finally:
        os.close(handle)


def test_the_running_receipt_is_complete_before_the_first_change(tmp_path,monkeypatch):
    hosted(monkeypatch,starts={os.getpid():('running','111')},lock=tmp_path/'release.lock')
    p,installed,prior,receipt=interrupted_release(monkeypatch,tmp_path,at=('nginx','-t'))
    # The interrupt lands on the first change to the fleet: the upstream file is
    # rewritten, and nothing has been reloaded, stopped or replaced yet.
    assert installed.ingress.read_text()==release.render_upstreams(p['units'],['api-incremental-a'])
    assert installed.generation==0 and mutating(installed.calls)==[]
    assert receipt['steps'][0]['phase']=='started'
    assert receipt['plan_sha256']==release.plan_digest(p)
    assert set(receipt['before'])==set(p['units'])
    assert receipt['owner']['protected'] and receipt['owner']['host_sha256']=='host-a'
    assert receipt['owner']['boot_id']=='boot-1' and receipt['owner']['pid']==os.getpid()
    assert receipt['owner']['start_ticks']=='111'
    assert receipt['owner']['lock']['path']==str(tmp_path/'release.lock')
    assert set(json.loads((tmp_path/'run1/image-pins.json').read_text())['services'])==set(p['units'])


def test_running_receipt_is_refused_without_the_explicit_takeover_flag(tmp_path,monkeypatch):
    hosted(monkeypatch,starts={os.getpid():('running','111')},lock=tmp_path/'release.lock')
    p,installed,prior,_=interrupted_release(monkeypatch,tmp_path,at=('nginx','-t'))
    with release.host_release_lock():
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError,match='--recover-interrupted'):
            release.recover(p,prior,tmp_path/'run2')
    # A completed release never qualifies, flag or not.
    done=json.loads(prior.read_text());done['status']='completed'
    (tmp_path/'done').mkdir();(tmp_path/'done/receipt.json').write_text(json.dumps(done))
    with release.host_release_lock():
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError,match='rollback_plan'):
            release.recover(p,tmp_path/'done/receipt.json',tmp_path/'run3',interrupted=True)
    assert mutating(installed.calls)==[]


def test_takeover_refuses_a_live_owner_a_foreign_host_and_a_missing_proof(tmp_path,monkeypatch):
    hosted(monkeypatch,starts={os.getpid():('running','111')},lock=tmp_path/'release.lock')
    p,installed,prior,_=interrupted_release(monkeypatch,tmp_path,at=('nginx','-t'))
    # The recorded owner is this very process, which is demonstrably running.
    with release.host_release_lock():
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError,match='still running'):
            release.recover(p,prior,tmp_path/'alive',interrupted=True)
    reowned(prior,host_sha256='host-b',pid=OWNER_PID,start_ticks='900')
    with release.host_release_lock():
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError,match='another host'):
            release.recover(p,prior,tmp_path/'foreign',interrupted=True)
    unproven=[{'protected':False},
        {'protected':True,'host_sha256':'host-a','boot_id':'boot-1','pid':OWNER_PID,'start_ticks':None},
        {'protected':True,'host_sha256':None,'boot_id':'boot-1','pid':OWNER_PID,'start_ticks':'900'},
        # a recorded pid must be a real positive pid, and bool is not one; these
        # carry an otherwise complete proof, so only the pid can reject them
        *[{'protected':True,'host_sha256':'host-a','boot_id':'boot-1','pid':bad,'start_ticks':'900',
           'lock':{'path':str(tmp_path/'release.lock'),'dev':1,'ino':1}} for bad in (True,0,-9,'424242')],
        # ...and the proof must name the lock file it was taken under
        {'protected':True,'host_sha256':'host-a','boot_id':'boot-1','pid':OWNER_PID,
         'start_ticks':'900'}]
    for index,owner in enumerate(unproven):
        receipt=json.loads(prior.read_text());receipt['owner']=owner;prior.write_text(json.dumps(receipt))
        with release.host_release_lock():
            with pytest.raises(RuntimeError,match='no lock-protected owner proof'):
                release.recover(p,prior,tmp_path/('unproven%d'%index),interrupted=True)
    # And a caller that does not hold the host lock can never take over at all.
    reowned(prior,protected=True,host_sha256='host-a',boot_id='boot-1',
            pid=OWNER_PID,start_ticks='900',lock={'path':str(tmp_path/'release.lock'),'dev':1,'ino':1})
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError,match='requires holding the fixed host'):
        release.recover(p,prior,tmp_path/'unlocked',interrupted=True)
    assert mutating(installed.calls)==[]


def test_takeover_proceeds_only_when_the_owner_is_provably_gone(tmp_path,monkeypatch):
    def interrupted(name,boot='boot-1'):
        root=tmp_path/name;root.mkdir()
        live=hosted(monkeypatch,boot=boot,lock=root/'release.lock',
                    starts={os.getpid():('running','111'),OWNER_PID:('running','900')})
        p,installed,prior,_=interrupted_release(monkeypatch,root,at=('nginx','-t'))
        reowned(prior,pid=OWNER_PID,start_ticks='900')
        return root,p,installed,prior,live

    # 1. The owner is gone; nothing answers on its pid.
    root,p,installed,prior,live=interrupted('gone')
    live.pop(OWNER_PID)
    with release.host_release_lock():
        receipt=release.recover(p,prior,root/'run2',interrupted=True)
    assert receipt['status']=='completed'
    assert receipt['recovers']['status']=='running'
    assert receipt['recovers']['takeover']['reason']=='owner exited within the same boot'
    assert receipt['recovers']['takeover']['owner']['pid']==OWNER_PID
    assert receipt['recovers']['sha256']==hashlib.sha256(prior.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    assert receipt['origin_before']==json.loads(prior.read_text())['before']
    assert all(installed.containers[t]['Image']==NEW_IMAGE for t in p['targets'])
    assert installed.ingress.read_text()==release.render_upstreams(p['units'])
    assert set(json.loads((root/'run2/image-pins.json').read_text())['services'])==set(p['units'])

    # 2. Its pid now answers, but as an unrelated process: still gone.
    root,p,installed,prior,live=interrupted('reused')
    with release.host_release_lock():
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError,match='still running'):
            release.recover(p,prior,root/'alive',interrupted=True)
    live[OWNER_PID]=('running','901')
    with release.host_release_lock():
        receipt=release.recover(p,prior,root/'run2',interrupted=True)
    assert 'pid was reused' in receipt['recovers']['takeover']['reason']
    assert receipt['status']=='completed'

    # 3. Same pid and same start time, but after a reboot: a different process.
    root,p,installed,prior,live=interrupted('rebooted')
    monkeypatch.setattr(release,'boot_id',lambda:'boot-2')
    with release.host_release_lock():
        receipt=release.recover(p,prior,root/'run2',interrupted=True)
    assert 'rebooted' in receipt['recovers']['takeover']['reason']
    assert receipt['recovers']['takeover']['observed']['boot_id']=='boot-2'
    assert receipt['status']=='completed'


def test_takeover_of_an_interrupted_run_keeps_every_other_gate(tmp_path,monkeypatch):
    live=hosted(monkeypatch,lock=tmp_path/'release.lock',
                starts={os.getpid():('running','111'),OWNER_PID:('running','900')})
    p,installed,prior,_=interrupted_release(monkeypatch,tmp_path,at=('nginx','-t'))
    reowned(prior,pid=OWNER_PID,start_ticks='900');live.pop(OWNER_PID)
    relocated=edited(p,compose_files=['/srv/source/other-compose.yml'])
    release.validate(relocated)
    with release.host_release_lock():
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError,match='reviewed plan'):
            release.recover(relocated,prior,tmp_path/'edited',interrupted=True)
    installed.containers['api-stock-a']['Id']='id-api-stock-a-restarted'
    with release.host_release_lock():
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError,match='unrelated unit changed'):
            release.recover(p,prior,tmp_path/'drifted',interrupted=True)
    assert mutating(installed.calls)==[]


def test_takeover_never_restarts_finished_targets_and_still_pins(tmp_path,monkeypatch):
    live=hosted(monkeypatch,lock=tmp_path/'release.lock',
                starts={os.getpid():('running','111'),OWNER_PID:('running','900')})
    # The run is interrupted inside the long stop of the second target, after
    # the first one has fully finished and returned to the proxy.
    p=plan(tmp_path);installed=scene(monkeypatch,p)
    installed.interrupt.add(('docker','stop','--time','-1','api-incremental-b'))
    with release.host_release_lock():
        with pytest.raises(KeyboardInterrupt):
            release.publish(p,tmp_path/'run1')
    installed.interrupt.clear()
    prior=tmp_path/'run1/receipt.json'
    assert json.loads(prior.read_text())['status']=='running'
    reowned(prior,pid=OWNER_PID,start_ticks='900');live.pop(OWNER_PID)
    done=dict(installed.containers['api-incremental-a']);mark=len(installed.calls)

    with release.host_release_lock():
        receipt=release.recover(p,prior,tmp_path/'run2',interrupted=True)
    assert receipt['observed']['api-incremental-a']['action']=='completed'
    assert [args for args in mutating(installed.calls[mark:]) if 'api-incremental-a' in args]==[]
    assert installed.containers['api-incremental-a']==done
    assert receipt['status']=='completed'
    pins=json.loads((tmp_path/'run2/image-pins.json').read_text())['services']
    assert set(pins)==set(p['units']) and pins['api-incremental-b']['image']==NEW_IMAGE


def test_a_child_outliving_a_dead_release_keeps_the_host_lock(tmp_path):
    """Real processes: a lock released by a dead parent would let a second
    release start on top of a still-running docker/nginx step."""
    lock=tmp_path/'release.lock';child_pid_file=tmp_path/'child.pid'
    holder=tmp_path/'holder.py'
    holder.write_text(
        'import importlib.util,sys\n'
        'from pathlib import Path\n'
        'spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("module_release",sys.argv[1])\n'
        'release=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec);spec.loader.exec_module(release)\n'
