def test_mysql_importer_registers_all_models_in_a_fresh_process() -> None:
    completed = subprocess.run(
        [
            sys.executable,
            "-c",
            "from app.database import Base; import app.mysql_importer; "
            "print(len(Base.metadata.tables))",
        ],
        cwd=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1],
        check=False,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
    )

    assert completed.returncode == 0, completed.stderr
    assert completed.stdout.strip() == "153"


def _source_snapshot(path: Path, *, user_count: int = 5) -> Path:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{path}")
    try:
        Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
        users = Base.metadata.tables["users"]
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.execute(
                users.insert(),
                [
                    {
                        "id": row_id,
                        "username": f"mysql-operator-{row_id}",
                        "password_hash": "fixture-hash",
                        "display_name": f"MySQL 运营 {row_id}",
                        "is_active": row_id % 2 == 1,
                        "is_admin": row_id == 1,
                        "role": "operator",
                        "permissions_json": '{"b":2,"a":1}',
                        "last_login_at": None,
                        "created_at": datetime(
                            2026,
                            8,
                            28,
                            row_id,
                            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
                        ),
                    }
                    for row_id in range(1, user_count + 1)
                ],
            )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()
    path.chmod(0o400)
    return path


def _sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def _create_import_target(mysql_engine: Engine) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection, install_invariants=False)


def _user_row(row_id: int, *, display_name: str | None = None) -> dict[str, object]:
    return {
        "id": row_id,
        "username": f"mysql-operator-{row_id}",
        "password_hash": "fixture-hash",
        "display_name": display_name or f"MySQL 运营 {row_id}",
        "is_active": row_id % 2 == 1,
        "is_admin": row_id == 1,
        "role": "operator",
        "permissions_json": '{"a":1,"b":2}',
        "last_login_at": None,
        "created_at": datetime(2026, 8, 28, row_id % 24),
    }


def test_mysql_import_resumes_and_finalizes_with_next_auto_increment(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    source = _source_snapshot(tmp_path / "source.db", user_count=5)
    source_hash = _sha256(source)
    source_stat = source.stat()
    checkpoint_dir = tmp_path / "checkpoints"
    _create_import_target(mysql_engine)

    with pytest.raises(InjectedImportStop, match="1"):
        import_sqlite_snapshot_to_mysql(
            source,
            mysql_engine,
            checkpoint_dir,
            batch_size=2,
            stop_after_batches=1,
        )

    with mysql_engine.connect() as connection:
        assert connection.scalar(text("SELECT count(*) FROM users")) == 2
        assert connection.scalar(text("SELECT @@FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS")) == 1

    report = resume_mysql_import(
        source,
        mysql_engine,
        checkpoint_dir,
        batch_size=2,
    )

    assert report.resumed is True
    assert report.duplicate_rows == 0
    assert report.table_rows["users"] == 5
    assert report.completed_tables == 139
    assert _sha256(source) == source_hash
    assert source.stat() == source_stat
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        imported = connection.execute(
            text(
                "SELECT id, permissions_json, is_active FROM users "
                "ORDER BY id"
            )
        ).mappings().all()
        assert [row["id"] for row in imported] == [1, 2, 3, 4, 5]
        assert imported[0]["permissions_json"] == '{"a":1,"b":2}'
        assert imported[0]["is_active"] == 1
        connection.execute(
            Base.metadata.tables["users"].insert(),
            {
                "username": "after-mysql-import",
                "password_hash": "fixture-hash",
                "display_name": "迁移后",
                "is_active": True,
                "is_admin": False,
                "role": "operator",
                "permissions_json": "[]",
            },
        )
        assert connection.scalar(
            text("SELECT id FROM users WHERE username='after-mysql-import'")
        ) == 6
        assert len(installed_mysql_invariants(connection)) == sum(
            contract.owner == "database_trigger"
            for contract in INVARIANT_CATALOG.values()
        )
        assert connection.scalar(
            text(
                "SELECT count(*) FROM information_schema.COLUMNS "
                "WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA=DATABASE() AND COLUMN_NAME LIKE 'g_uq_%'"
            )
        ) == 8


def test_mysql_resume_accepts_exact_row_committed_before_checkpoint(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    source = _source_snapshot(tmp_path / "source.db", user_count=4)
    checkpoint_dir = tmp_path / "checkpoints"
    _create_import_target(mysql_engine)
    with pytest.raises(InjectedImportStop):
        import_sqlite_snapshot_to_mysql(
            source,
            mysql_engine,
            checkpoint_dir,
            batch_size=2,
            stop_after_batches=1,
        )
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        connection.execute(Base.metadata.tables["users"].insert(), _user_row(3))

    report = resume_mysql_import(
        source,
        mysql_engine,
        checkpoint_dir,
        batch_size=2,
    )

    assert report.duplicate_rows == 1
    with mysql_engine.connect() as connection:
        assert connection.scalar(text("SELECT count(*) FROM users")) == 4


def test_mysql_resume_rejects_same_primary_key_with_different_row(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    source = _source_snapshot(tmp_path / "source.db", user_count=4)
    checkpoint_dir = tmp_path / "checkpoints"
    _create_import_target(mysql_engine)
    with pytest.raises(InjectedImportStop):
        import_sqlite_snapshot_to_mysql(
            source,
            mysql_engine,
            checkpoint_dir,
            batch_size=2,
            stop_after_batches=1,
        )
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        connection.execute(
            Base.metadata.tables["users"].insert(),
            _user_row(3, display_name="must-not-echo-conflicting-value"),
        )

    with pytest.raises(MySQLImportError, match="users") as captured:
        resume_mysql_import(
            source,
            mysql_engine,
            checkpoint_dir,
            batch_size=2,
        )

    assert "must-not-echo-conflicting-value" not in str(captured.value)


def test_mysql_import_rejects_a_nonempty_first_target(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    source = _source_snapshot(tmp_path / "source.db", user_count=1)
    _create_import_target(mysql_engine)
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        connection.execute(Base.metadata.tables["users"].insert(), _user_row(99))

    with pytest.raises(MySQLImportError, match="非空"):
        import_sqlite_snapshot_to_mysql(
            source,
            mysql_engine,
            tmp_path / "checkpoints",
        )


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("column", "value", "message"),
    [
        ("permissions_json", "must-not-echo-{bad-json", "非法 JSON"),
        ("display_name", "must-not-echo\x00nul", "NUL"),
        ("is_active", 2, "非法布尔值"),
    ],
)
def test_mysql_import_rejects_malformed_source_values_without_echoing_them(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
    tmp_path: Path,
    column: str,
    value: object,
    message: str,
) -> None:
    source = _source_snapshot(tmp_path / "source.db", user_count=1)
    source.chmod(0o600)
    connection = sqlite3.connect(source)
    try:
        connection.execute("PRAGMA ignore_check_constraints = ON")
        connection.execute(
            f'UPDATE users SET "{column}"=? WHERE id=1',
            (value,),
        )
        connection.commit()
    finally:
        connection.close()
    source.chmod(0o400)
    _create_import_target(mysql_engine)

    with pytest.raises(MySQLImportError, match=message) as captured:
        import_sqlite_snapshot_to_mysql(
            source,
            mysql_engine,
            tmp_path / "checkpoints",
        )

    assert "must-not-echo" not in str(captured.value)


def test_mysql_checkpoint_is_bound_to_one_target_schema(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    source = _source_snapshot(tmp_path / "source.db", user_count=3)
    checkpoint_dir = tmp_path / "checkpoints"
    _create_import_target(mysql_engine)
    with pytest.raises(InjectedImportStop):
        import_sqlite_snapshot_to_mysql(
            source,
            mysql_engine,
            checkpoint_dir,
            batch_size=2,
            stop_after_batches=1,
        )
    checkpoint_path = checkpoint_dir / "sqlite-to-mysql-import.json"
    checkpoint = json.loads(checkpoint_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    checkpoint["target_schema"] = "another_schema"
    checkpoint_path.chmod(0o600)
    checkpoint_path.write_text(
        json.dumps(checkpoint, ensure_ascii=False),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    with pytest.raises(MySQLImportError, match="target_schema"):
        resume_mysql_import(source, mysql_engine, checkpoint_dir, batch_size=2)


def test_mysql_import_cli_uses_migration_role_and_emits_only_safe_report(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module = _integration_script()
    source = tmp_path / "frozen.db"
    source.write_bytes(b"sqlite-fixture")
    accounts = tmp_path / "accounts.json"
    accounts.write_text("{}", encoding="utf-8")
    checkpoint_dir = tmp_path / "checkpoints"
    calls: dict[str, object] = {}

    def fake_url(
        accounts_file: Path,
        *,
        role: str,
        database: str,
    ) -> str:
        calls["accounts_file"] = accounts_file
        calls["role"] = role
        calls["database"] = database
        return "mysql+pymysql://migration:must-not-echo@localhost/labels"

    class FakeConnection:
        def __enter__(self) -> "FakeConnection":
            return self

        def __exit__(self, *_args: object) -> None:
            return None

    class FakeEngine:
        def connect(self) -> FakeConnection:
            return FakeConnection()

        def dispose(self) -> None:
            calls["disposed"] = True

    fake_engine = FakeEngine()
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "mysql_database_url_from_references", fake_url)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "create_mysql_migration_engine",
        lambda url: calls.setdefault("url", url) and fake_engine,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "assert_mysql_runtime_contract",
        lambda connection: calls.setdefault("contract_connection", connection),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "import_sqlite_snapshot_to_mysql",
        lambda source_path, target, checkpoints, *, batch_size: (
            calls.update(
                {
                    "source": source_path,
                    "target": target,
                    "checkpoints": checkpoints,
                    "batch_size": batch_size,
                }
            )
            or SimpleNamespace(
                source=source_path.resolve(),
                source_sha256="a" * 64,
                inserted_rows=12,
                duplicate_rows=1,
                table_rows={"users": 12},
                completed_tables=107,
                resumed=True,
            )
        ),
    )
    output = StringIO()

    exit_code = module.main(
        [
            "import-snapshot",
            "--source",
            str(source),
            "--checkpoint-dir",
            str(checkpoint_dir),
            "--database",
            "labels_migration",
            "--accounts",
            str(accounts),
            "--batch-size",
            "250",
        ],
        output=output,
    )

    payload = json.loads(output.getvalue())
    assert exit_code == 0
    assert calls["accounts_file"] == accounts
    assert calls["role"] == "migration"
    assert calls["database"] == "labels_migration"
    assert calls["source"] == source
    assert calls["target"] is fake_engine
    assert calls["checkpoints"] == checkpoint_dir
    assert calls["batch_size"] == 250
    assert calls["disposed"] is True
    assert payload == {
        "command": "import-snapshot",
        "completed_tables": 107,
        "duplicate_rows": 1,
        "inserted_rows": 12,
        "resumed": True,
        "source": str(source.resolve()),
        "source_sha256": "a" * 64,
        "status": "ok",
        "table_rows": {"users": 12},
        "target_schema": "labels_migration",
    }
    assert "must-not-echo" not in output.getvalue()


def test_mysql_import_cli_accepts_private_runtime_dsn_file(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module = _integration_script()
    source = tmp_path / "snapshot.sqlite3"
    source.write_bytes(b"sqlite-fixture")
    secret_file = tmp_path / "migration-database-url"
    secret_file.write_text(
        "mysql+pymysql://migration:must-not-echo@mysql/labels_migration\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    secret_file.chmod(0o600)
    calls: dict[str, object] = {}

    class FakeConnection:
        def __enter__(self) -> "FakeConnection":
            return self

        def __exit__(self, *_args: object) -> None:
            return None

    class FakeEngine:
        def connect(self) -> FakeConnection:
            return FakeConnection()

        def dispose(self) -> None:
            calls["disposed"] = True

    fake_engine = FakeEngine()
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "create_mysql_migration_engine",
        lambda url: calls.setdefault("url", str(url)) and fake_engine,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "assert_mysql_runtime_contract",
        lambda _connection: None,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "import_sqlite_snapshot_to_mysql",
        lambda source_path, target, checkpoints, *, batch_size: SimpleNamespace(
            source=source_path.resolve(),
            source_sha256="b" * 64,
            inserted_rows=4,
            duplicate_rows=0,
            table_rows={"users": 4},
            completed_tables=119,
            resumed=False,
        ),
    )
    output = StringIO()

    exit_code = module.main(
        [
            "import-snapshot",
            "--source",
            str(source),
            "--checkpoint-dir",
            str(tmp_path / "checkpoints"),
            "--database",
            "labels_migration",
            "--database-url-file",
            str(secret_file),
        ],
        output=output,
    )

    assert exit_code == 0
    assert calls["url"] == (
        "mysql+pymysql://migration:***@mysql/labels_migration"
    )
    assert calls["disposed"] is True
    assert "must-not-echo" not in output.getvalue()


def test_prepare_import_target_creates_baseline_without_invariants(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module = _integration_script()
    secret_file = tmp_path / "migration-database-url"
    secret_file.write_text(
        "mysql+pymysql://migration:must-not-echo@mysql/labels_migration",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    secret_file.chmod(0o600)
    calls: dict[str, object] = {}

    class FakeConnection:
        pass

    class FakeBegin:
        def __enter__(self) -> FakeConnection:
            connection = FakeConnection()
            calls["connection"] = connection
            return connection

        def __exit__(self, *_args: object) -> None:
            return None

    class FakeEngine:
        def begin(self) -> FakeBegin:
            return FakeBegin()

        def dispose(self) -> None:
            calls["disposed"] = True

    fake_engine = FakeEngine()
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "create_mysql_migration_engine",
        lambda _url: fake_engine,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "assert_mysql_runtime_contract",
        lambda connection: calls.setdefault("contract", connection),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "assert_mysql_import_schema_empty",
        lambda connection: calls.setdefault("empty_schema", connection),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "create_mysql_baseline",
        lambda connection, *, install_invariants: calls.update(
            {
                "baseline": connection,
                "install_invariants": install_invariants,
            }
        ),
    )
    output = StringIO()

    exit_code = module.main(
        [
            "prepare-import-target",
            "--database",
            "labels_migration",
            "--database-url-file",
            str(secret_file),
        ],
        output=output,
    )

    assert exit_code == 0
    assert calls["contract"] is calls["connection"]
    assert calls["empty_schema"] is calls["connection"]
    assert calls["baseline"] is calls["connection"]
    assert calls["install_invariants"] is False
    assert calls["disposed"] is True
    assert json.loads(output.getvalue()) == {
        "command": "prepare-import-target",
        "status": "ok",
        "target_schema": "labels_migration",
    }
    assert "must-not-echo" not in output.getvalue()


def test_upgrade_existing_schema_uses_migration_identity_and_latest_version(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module = _integration_script()
    secret_file = tmp_path / "migration-database-url"
    secret_file.write_text(
        "mysql+pymysql://migration:must-not-echo@mysql/labels_migration",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    secret_file.chmod(0o600)
    calls: dict[str, object] = {}

    class FakeConnection:
        pass

    class FakeBegin:
        def __enter__(self) -> FakeConnection:
            connection = FakeConnection()
            calls["connection"] = connection
            return connection

        def __exit__(self, *_args: object) -> None:
            return None

    class FakeEngine:
        def begin(self) -> FakeBegin:
            return FakeBegin()

        def dispose(self) -> None:
            calls["disposed"] = True

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "create_mysql_migration_engine",
        lambda _url: FakeEngine(),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "assert_mysql_runtime_contract",
        lambda connection: calls.setdefault("contract", connection),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "upgrade_mysql_schema",
        lambda connection, *, expected: calls.update(
            {"upgraded": connection, "expected": expected}
        ),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "assert_mysql_schema_version",
        lambda connection, *, expected: calls.update(
            {"verified": connection, "verified_expected": expected}
        ),
    )
    output = StringIO()

    exit_code = module.main(
        [
            "upgrade-schema",
            "--database",
            "labels_migration",
            "--database-url-file",
            str(secret_file),
        ],
        output=output,
    )

    expected = max(item.version for item in module.MIGRATIONS)
    assert exit_code == 0
    assert calls["contract"] is calls["connection"]
    assert calls["upgraded"] is calls["connection"]
    assert calls["verified"] is calls["connection"]
    assert calls["expected"] == calls["verified_expected"] == expected
    assert calls["disposed"] is True
    assert json.loads(output.getvalue()) == {
        "command": "upgrade-schema",
        "migration": expected,
        "status": "ok",
        "target_schema": "labels_migration",
    }
    assert "must-not-echo" not in output.getvalue()


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("url", "error"),
    [
        ("sqlite:////tmp/not-mysql.db", "MySQL"),
        (
            "mysql+pymysql://migration:secret@mysql/wrong_schema",
            "database",
        ),
    ],
)
def test_private_runtime_dsn_file_rejects_wrong_target(
    tmp_path: Path,
    url: str,
    error: str,
) -> None:
    module = _integration_script()
    secret_file = tmp_path / "migration-database-url"
    secret_file.write_text(url, encoding="utf-8")
    secret_file.chmod(0o600)

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=error):
        module.database_url_from_private_file(
            secret_file,
            expected_database="labels_migration",
        )

) -> None:
    module = _integration_script()
    secret_file = tmp_path / "migration-database-url"
    secret_file.write_text(
        "mysql+pymysql://migration:secret@mysql/labels_migration",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    secret_file.chmod(0o600)

        module.database_url_from_private_file(
            secret_file,
            expected_database="labels;drop",
        )


def test_mysql_migration_engine_installs_the_session_contract(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module = _integration_script()
    database_url = object()
    fake_engine = object()
    dbapi_connection = object()
    calls: dict[str, object] = {}

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "engine_options",
        lambda value: calls.setdefault("options_url", value)
        and {"pool_pre_ping": True},
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "create_engine",
        lambda value, **options: calls.update(
            {"create_url": value, "create_options": options}
        )
        or fake_engine,
    )

    def fake_listen(target: object, event_name: str, listener: object) -> None:
        calls["listen_target"] = target
        calls["event_name"] = event_name
        calls["listener"] = listener

    monkeypatch.setattr(module.event, "listen", fake_listen)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "configure_mysql_dbapi_connection",
        lambda connection: calls.setdefault("configured", connection),
    )

    result = module.create_mysql_migration_engine(database_url)

    assert result is fake_engine
    assert calls["options_url"] is database_url
    assert calls["create_url"] is database_url
    assert calls["create_options"] == {"pool_pre_ping": True}
    assert calls["listen_target"] is fake_engine
    assert calls["event_name"] == "connect"
    listener = calls["listener"]
    assert callable(listener)
    listener(dbapi_connection, object())
    assert calls["configured"] is dbapi_connection


def test_source_verification_reports_removed_snapshot_as_import_error(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    source = tmp_path / "removed-during-import.db"
    source.write_bytes(b"frozen")
    source_stat = source.stat()
    source_hash = _sha256(source)
    source.unlink()

    with pytest.raises(SnapshotImportError, match="无法复核"):
        verify_source_unchanged(
            source,
            initial_stat=source_stat,
            initial_sha256=source_hash,
        )


def test_clone_schema_cli_uses_root_maintenance_and_emits_safe_report(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module = _integration_script()
    accounts_file = tmp_path / "accounts.json"
    accounts_file.write_text("{}", encoding="utf-8")
    private_runtime = tmp_path / "private-runtime"
    calls: dict[str, object] = {"roles": []}

    def fake_url(
        _accounts_file: Path,
        *,
        role: str,
        database: str | None,
    ) -> URL:
        roles = calls["roles"]
        assert isinstance(roles, list)
        roles.append((role, database))
        if role == "root":
            return URL.create(
                "mysql+pymysql",
                username="root",
                password="root-fixture-secret",
                host="localhost",
                port=33084,
                query={"unix_socket": "/tmp/mysql.sock", "charset": "utf8mb4"},
            )
        return URL.create(
            "mysql+pymysql",
            username="source-migration",
            password="source-fixture-secret",
            host="localhost",
            port=33084,
            database=database,
            query={"unix_socket": "/tmp/mysql.sock", "charset": "utf8mb4"},
        )

    class Scope:
        def __init__(self, connection: object) -> None:
            self.connection = connection

        def __enter__(self) -> object:
            return self.connection

        def __exit__(self, *_args: object) -> None:
            return None

    root_connection = object()
    target_connection = object()

    class FakeEngine:
        def __init__(self, connection: object) -> None:
            self.connection = connection

        def begin(self) -> Scope:
            return Scope(self.connection)

        def connect(self) -> Scope:
            return Scope(self.connection)

        def dispose(self) -> None:
            calls.setdefault("disposed", []).append(self.connection)

    root_engine = FakeEngine(root_connection)
    target_engine = FakeEngine(target_connection)

    def fake_engine(url: URL) -> FakeEngine:
        return root_engine if url.username == "root" else target_engine

    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "mysql_database_url_from_references", fake_url)
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "create_mysql_migration_engine", fake_engine)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "prepare_candidate_database",
        lambda connection, **kwargs: calls.update(
            {"prepared_connection": connection, "prepared": kwargs}
        ),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "clone_schema_with_clients",
        lambda **kwargs: calls.update({"cloned": kwargs})
        or SimpleNamespace(
            source_schema=kwargs["source_schema"],
            target_schema=kwargs["target_schema"],
            stripped_definers=44,
        ),
    )
    evidence = SimpleNamespace(
        migration=87,
        tables={"assets": object(), "workflow_versions": object()},
        views=(),
        trigger_count=0,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "mysql_schema_evidence", lambda *_args: evidence)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "assert_exact_schema_clone",
        lambda *_args: SimpleNamespace(
            table_count=2,
            row_count=5,
            evidence_hash="a" * 64,
        ),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "upgrade_mysql_schema",
        lambda connection, *, expected: calls.update(
            {"upgrade_connection": connection, "upgrade_expected": expected}
        ),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "assert_mysql_schema_version",
        lambda connection, *, expected: calls.update(
            {"assert_connection": connection, "assert_expected": expected}
        ),
    )
    app_file = private_runtime / "app-database-url"
    migration_file = private_runtime / "migration-database-url"

    def fake_write(private_root: Path, **_kwargs: object) -> object:
        private_root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        app_file.write_text("private", encoding="utf-8")
        migration_file.write_text("private", encoding="utf-8")
        return SimpleNamespace(app=app_file, migration=migration_file)

    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "write_candidate_runtime_dsns", fake_write)
    monkeypatch.setattr(module.secrets, "token_urlsafe", lambda _size: "generated-secret")
    output = StringIO()

    exit_code = module.main(
        [
            "clone-schema",
            "--source",
            "labelsystem_integration_candidate_20260829_f3b95eb",
            "--target",
            "labelsystem_docker_candidate_20260829_04c8298",
            "--accounts",
            str(accounts_file),
            "--mysql-prefix",
            str(tmp_path / "mysql84"),
            "--private-runtime-dir",
            str(private_runtime),
        ],
        output=output,
    )

    payload = json.loads(output.getvalue())
    assert exit_code == 0
    assert calls["roles"] == [
        ("root", None),
        ("migration", "labelsystem_integration_candidate_20260829_f3b95eb"),
    ]
    assert calls["prepared_connection"] is root_connection
    assert calls["upgrade_connection"] is target_connection
    assert payload == {
        "app_database_url_file": str(app_file.resolve()),
        "clone_evidence_hash": "a" * 64,
        "cloned_migration": 87,
        "cloned_rows": 5,
        "cloned_tables": 2,
        "command": "clone-schema",
        "migration_database_url_file": str(migration_file.resolve()),
        "source_schema": "labelsystem_integration_candidate_20260829_f3b95eb",
        "status": "ok",
        "stripped_definers": 44,
        "target_migration": max(item.version for item in MIGRATIONS),
        "target_schema": "labelsystem_docker_candidate_20260829_04c8298",
    }
    assert "fixture-secret" not in output.getvalue()
    assert "generated-secret" not in output.getvalue()
